## What changes between consecutive stops (drives the morph)

### (start) → 00-t7-1959
- First stop - no previous stop

### 00-t7-1959 → 01-901-1963
- Greenhouse: T7 four-seat notchback with wrap-around rear glass -> 2+2 fastback with large sloping rear window ([caranddriver.com](https://www.caranddriver.com/news/butzis-rough-draft-the-porsche-t7-concept-was-the-911s-direct-forefather), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-evolutionary-history-754-901-ferry-porsche-14641.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/1.-Generation-Design-und-Modellvarianten.html))
- Wheelbase shortened (Newsroom: 2.40 m -> 2.20 m; 901 brochure: 2,204 mm) ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-evolutionary-history-754-901-ferry-porsche-14641.html), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf))
- Engine lid gains a slatted chrome grille; T7 relied on small side vents ([caranddriver.com](https://www.caranddriver.com/news/butzis-rough-draft-the-porsche-t7-concept-was-the-911s-direct-forefather), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-f/911-f-901/))
- Front end essentially carried over (T7 front 'could easily be mistaken for its production successor'); the T7's two dark intake openings under the bumper are not seen on the 1963 901 photos ([caranddriver.com](https://www.caranddriver.com/news/butzis-rough-draft-the-porsche-t7-concept-was-the-911s-direct-forefather), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20100708044429/http://vista.pca.org/stl/695.htm), [cdn.motor1.com](https://cdn.motor1.com/images/mgl/0eNOmn/s1/porsche-type-754-t7-concept-1963.jpg), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:9db50efe-1600-4efe-b1b1-8891c93be833/b-HDK%207059.jpg))
- Exterior mirror moves from a stalk on the front wing (T7) to the front of the door (901) ([cdn.motor1.com](https://cdn.motor1.com/images/mgl/koW4XM/s1/porsche-type-754-t7-concept-1963.jpg), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-f/911-f-901/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/.imaging/mte/porsche-templating-theme/image_1290x726/dam/pnr/porsche_newsroom/Historie/911-Nr-57/Entwicklung-hin-zu-911/b-HNE-4727.jpg/jcr:content/b-HNE%204727.jpg))
- Tail: T7 flat tail with rear-flank intake grilles, black guards around a mesh centre panel and twin centre exhausts -> 901 sloping fastback engine lid with top chrome grille, slim chrome-strip bumper and a single left-hand tailpipe ([commons.wikimedia.org](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Porsche_754_T7_in_the_Porsche-Museum_(2009)_1X7A0333.jpg), [motor1.com](https://www.motor1.com/news/690730/porsche-type-754-concept-history/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/db96470d-1b16-4bf9-b7a6-bdc7f65b3465.zip))
- Side glass: T7's long rear side window (four seats) -> shorter 2+2 quarter window with curved trailing edge ([cdn.motor1.com](https://cdn.motor1.com/images/mgl/koW4XM/s1/porsche-type-754-t7-concept-1963.jpg), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/db96470d-1b16-4bf9-b7a6-bdc7f65b3465.zip))

### 01-901-1963 → 02-911-swb-1964
- Length +28 mm (4135 -> 4163), width +10 (1600 -> 1610), height +47 (1273 -> 1320 unladen), wheelbase +7 (2204 -> 2211), tracks +5 mm, ground clearance +32 (118 -> 150) ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf), [archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260421_20260421_1757))
- Front bumper gains two chrome guards; two fog lamps appear under the front bumper ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-f/911-f-901/), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/af972fe2-03e8-4723-aca7-6bc41556df09.zip), [archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260421_20260421_1757))
- Rear script: planned '901' badge replaced by 'PORSCHE' + '911' scripts ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-history-901-911-iaa-12274.html), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-f/911-f-911/))

### 02-911-swb-1964 → 03-911-lwb-1969
- Wheelbase +57 mm (2,211 -> 2,268 mm; 2,271 from MY1972) by moving the rear wheels aft; overall length unchanged -> rear overhang ~57 mm shorter ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:c1a8cb3d-ea37-495f-8e29-48262a263c3c/S18_3621_en.pdf), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(classic)), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/12))
- Wheel arches get a subtle flare lip; rear arch cut-out moves back with the wheel; front indicators slightly larger ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-model-research/porsche-911-research/porsche-911-f-series-1st-generation-research-hub/), [classicandsportscar.com](https://www.classicandsportscar.com/features/buyers-guide-porsche-911-1964-73), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(1963)))
- MY1972 only: round oil-filler flap appears on the right rear wing (omit for a generic LWB car) ([en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(classic)), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_1972.pdf))
- Wider wheels/tyres on E/S: 6J x 15, 185/70 VR 15 (vs 4.5J/5.5J x 15, 165 HR 15 on 1965-68) ([excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/9), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/12), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/13), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(1963)))
- Wider tracks: 1,367/1,335 -> 1,374/1,355 mm (E/S) ([de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(1963)), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/13))
- From MY1970 door handles lose the push button (hidden pull lever) ([en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(classic)))
- MY1972-73 911 S: chin spoiler added under the front bumper ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_1972.pdf), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(classic)))

### 03-911-lwb-1969 → 04-carrera-rs-27-1973
- ADD ducktail spoiler on the engine lid ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-50-years-911-carrera-rs-2-7-germanys-fastest-sports-car-28486.html))
- Rear wings widened +42 mm (width 1,610 -> 1,652 mm); rear track 1,354 -> ~1,394 mm ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-50-years-911-carrera-rs-2-7-germanys-fastest-sports-car-28486.html), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1973-rs.pdf), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(1963)))
- Rear wheels 6J -> 7J x 15, tyres 185/70 -> 215/60 VR 15 ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-50-years-911-carrera-rs-2-7-germanys-fastest-sports-car-28486.html))
- Front spoiler with central oil-cooler opening replaces the 911 S apron spoiler ([de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(1963)))
- 'Carrera' script on sides + 'PORSCHE' decal on rear + colour-matched wheels ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-50-years-911-carrera-rs-2-7-germanys-fastest-sports-car-28486.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/pnr/2022/History/50-Years-911-Carrera-RS-2.7/Galerie-NEU-2/S22_2793_fine.jpg))
- 1973-spec black horn grilles / indicator surrounds; MY1972 oil-filler flap disappears (if 03 is modelled as MY1972) ([en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(classic)))

### 04-carrera-rs-27-1973 → 05-g-series-1974
- Impact bumpers with black bellows front and rear, body-coloured; overall length 4,102/4,147 -> 4,291 mm (front overhang now 932 mm, rear 1,088 mm) ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-%E2%80%93-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf))
- Front indicators move from the wing corners into the bumper; wing-corner horn grilles disappear ([de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(1963)), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250550&w=1000&h=1000&crop=0&public=1&cs=b07864ac7bd7f798))
- Red reflector strip with PORSCHE lettering added between the tail lamps ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-%E2%80%93-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html))
- Front spoiler removed; plain lower apron (911/911 S) ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-%E2%80%93-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html))
- Reference 911 S returns to narrow rear wings (1,652 -> 1,610 mm) and loses the ducktail, 'Carrera' decals and colour-keyed wheels ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1974.pdf))
- Fuchs 6J/7J -> cast 'cookie-cutter' 6J/6J (US) or steel 5.5J with hub caps (RoW base) ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf), [supercarnostalgia.com](https://supercarnostalgia.com/blog/porsche-911-g-h-series-1974-1975))
- New body-coloured sill panel with black rubbing strip; chrome retained on window frames/handles (911/911 S), black on Carrera ([supercarnostalgia.com](https://supercarnostalgia.com/blog/porsche-911-g-h-series-1974-1975), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1974.pdf))

### 05-g-series-1974 → 06-930-turbo-1975
- Rear arches flare out: width 1610 mm (1974 911/911 S) or 1652 mm (Carrera 2.7) → 1775 mm; front body 1610 → 1700 mm ([excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/30), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/40), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/1-137.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/02-489.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fia-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-930-3-0-1976/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fisa-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-911-sc-1982/))
- Tracks widen: front ~1360 → 1432 mm, rear ~1342 → ~1500 mm (vs 1974 911/911 S) ([excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/30), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/372))
- Fixed whale-tail wing with black rubber rim grows out of a GRP engine lid ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-turbo/))
- Deep front spoiler with integrated fog lamps ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/Porsche1976USA), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260421_20260421_1846/115.png))
- Wheels: 15-in ATS/5.5–6J → Fuchs 7J/8J (15 in, 16 in from MY1977) with low-profile tyres ([excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/30), [archive.org](https://archive.org/details/Porsche1976USA), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-turbo-3-0-930/))
- Trim turns black: anodised-black window frames/handles, body-colour headlight rings (911/911 S had chrome) ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-turbo/), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-911-s/))
- Black stone-chip foils ahead of rear arches (MY1977+) and 'turbo' lid script appear ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-turbo/), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260421_20260421_1846/115.png))

### 06-930-turbo-1975 → 07-911-sc-1978
- Turbo flares removed: rear 1775 → 1652 mm, front 1700 → 1610 mm; tracks F 1432→1369, R ~1500→1379 mm ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/1-137.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/02-489.jpg), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/132.png), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/36.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fia-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-930-3-0-1976/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fisa-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-911-sc-1982/))
- Whale-tail wing and deep front spoiler disappear (plain lid with black grille; spoilers only optional from MY1981) ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-sc/), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-turbo/))
- Fuchs 7J/8J x 16 → ATS cast 6J/7J x 15; tyres 185/70 & 215/60 VR15 ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-sc/), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/132.png), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-turbo-3-0-930/))
- Trim: anodised black (Turbo) → chrome window surrounds, door handles, headlight rings (MY1978 only) ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-sc/), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-turbo/))
- Stone-chip foils, 'turbo' script and standard fog lamps go away ('911 SC' script instead); exhaust stays a single left-rear outlet ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-turbo/), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/119.png), [commons.wikimedia.org](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Porsche_911_1978_SC_LSideRear_FOSSP_7April2013_(14563945076).jpg))
- Height 1310 → 1320 mm ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/36.jpg), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/132.png))

### 07-911-sc-1978 → 08-carrera-32-1984
- Fog lamps move from optional under-bumper units into the front valance ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/119.png), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(classic)), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-carrera-3-2-1984-1989/))
- Standard wheels: ATS cast → forged Fuchs (15 in; 16 in from MY1989) ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-sc/), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-carrera/))
- Chrome trim (1978 SC reference) → anodised-black window frames/handles, body-colour headlight rings ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-sc/), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-carrera/))
- Side repeaters on front wings; 'Carrera' script on engine lid ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-carrera/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/01-545.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fia-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-911-carrera-3-2-1986/))
- MY1987+: rear strip with red reflective logo and integrated fog lights ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-carrera/))
- Rear track 1379 → 1405 mm with the MY1989 8J x 16 wheels (body shell and overhangs unchanged: 932/1087 mm) ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/132.png), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/36.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/02-489.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/02-514.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fisa-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-911-sc-1982/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fia-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-911-carrera-3-2-1986/))

### 08-carrera-32-1984 → 09-964-1989
- Separate bumpers replaced by integrated body-coloured front and rear deformable plastic panels ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/3.-Generation---Design-und-Modellvarianten.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-seven-generations-part-3-type-964-16466.html), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/964/964/))
- Fog lights, indicators, number plate and air intakes now integrated into the front bumper ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/3.-Generation---Design-und-Modellvarianten.html))
- New aerodynamic sill covers (side skirts) ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/3.-Generation-Porsche-911,-(964),-1988---1994.html))
- Automatically extending rear spoiler integrated in the engine lid replaces the previously optional fixed spoiler ([de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_964), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/3.-Generation-Porsche-911,-(964),-1988---1994.html))
- Smooth underbody, smoothed rain gutters ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/3.-Generation---Design-und-Modellvarianten.html))
- 16-inch 'Design 90' wheels (6J/8J) as standard ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/964-1991.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1989-1994-sales-brochures/))
- Rear: full-width red reflective light strip with PORSCHE logo, red rear indicators ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/964/964/))

### 09-964-1989 → 10-993-1994
- Headlamps: upright round 'tunnel' lamps -> flatter raked poly-ellipsoid units in lower, wider wings ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/30-Jahre-911-Carrera-der-Generation-993-%E2%80%93-Der-letzte-seiner-Art.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/4.-Generation-Porsche-911,-(993),-1993-1998.html))
- Front lid shortened; black rubber lip between lid and bumper removed ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/993/993/))
- Wider rear wings running straighter to the tail; overall width +83 mm (1652 -> 1735) ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/993/993/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/964-1991.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1989-1994-sales-brochures/), [auto-motor-und-sport.de](https://www.auto-motor-und-sport.de/marken-modelle/porsche/911/993/technische-daten/))
- Rear light strip now angled/slanted; tail lights set higher; orange instead of red indicators on Carrera ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/993/993/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/30-Jahre-911-Carrera-der-Generation-993-%E2%80%93-Der-letzte-seiner-Art.html))
- Mirrors: Cup design teardrop mirrors (964 got them from MY1992) ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/993/993/), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/964/964/))
- Larger extending spoiler; redesigned third brake light ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1993.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1994-1998-sales-brochures/))
- Exhaust: two tailpipes at the sides (dual-flow) ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/993/993/), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(993)))
- Wheels: 16-in 7J/9J 5-spoke (wider rear 245/45) ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1993.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1994-1998-sales-brochures/))

### 10-993-1994 → 11-996-1-1998
- Entire body new: +185 mm length, +80 mm wheelbase, +30 mm width ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-seven-generations-part-5-typ-996-16487.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/5.-Generation-Porsche-911,-(996),-1997-2005.html))
- Headlamps: round poly-ellipsoid -> teardrop 'fried egg' multi-function units; fog lights and indicators move from bumper into headlamp ([en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(996)), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/))
- Rear: full-width light strip removed; separate tail lamps ([de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_996))
- Mirrors moved from door to A-pillar/window-front position ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/996/996/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/))
- Windshield rake 60° -> 55° ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/))
- Wheels 16 -> 17 in (7J/9J), 5 curved spokes ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1993.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1994-1998-sales-brochures/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/))

### 11-996-1-1998 → 12-996-2-2002
- Headlamps: Boxster-shared 996.1 'fried-egg' units -> 911 Turbo 'teardrop' units rounded off / S-shaped at the bottom (still one combined module incl. indicator) ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/5.-Generation-Porsche-911,-(996),-1997-2005.html), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20010801160045/http://www.porsche.com:80/english/news/pressreleases/010529.htm), [elferspot.com](https://www.elferspot.com/en/magazine/porsche-996-1-and-996-2-carrera-in-comparison-what-are-the-differences/), [porscheclubgb.com](https://www.porscheclubgb.com/regions-registers/registers/classic/996/porsche-911-(996)-buyers-guide))
- Front apron 'completely reshaped' (Turbo-inspired): larger radiator intakes (+15 % airflow), new brake-cooling ducts, reshaped front wheel-arch radii and small flexible spoilers ahead of the front wheels ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20010801160045/http://www.porsche.com:80/english/news/pressreleases/010529.htm), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2002-911_carrera/), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2003-911_carrera/))
- Rear: 'extended rear end panel' -- apron now has a raised brace-like section wheel-arch to wheel-arch below the plate recess (996 Turbo-based design) instead of a continuous round surface ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20010801160045/http://www.porsche.com:80/english/news/pressreleases/010529.htm), [elferspot.com](https://www.elferspot.com/en/magazine/porsche-996-1-and-996-2-carrera-in-comparison-what-are-the-differences/))
- Exhaust: redesigned -- two oval tailpipes, one at each outer corner ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20010801160045/http://www.porsche.com:80/english/news/pressreleases/010529.htm), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2002-911_carrera/), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2002-Rear_Three-Quarter.a8496a1a.jpg))
- Body: 'widened rear quarter panels' -> width 1,765 -> 1,770 mm; front track 1,455 -> 1,465 mm; length 4,430, height 1,305, wheelbase 2,350 unchanged ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2002-911_carrera/), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/57), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/63), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20030724115326/http://www3.porsche.com:80/english/usa/911/c2/technicalspecifications/default.htm))
- Wheels: new 17-inch 'Carrera II' slender five-spoke design (sizes unchanged 7J/9J x 17) ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2002-911_carrera/), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2003-911_carrera/), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20030724115326/http://www3.porsche.com:80/english/usa/911/c2/technicalspecifications/default.htm))

### 12-996-2-2002 → 13-997-1-2005
- Headlamps: 996 teardrop units -> steeper compact oval clear-glass units set in an arching wing (return to round-lamp 911 cue) ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-seven-generations-part-6-type-997-16489.html), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071130055840/http://www.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY08_PCGB.pdf), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2005-911_carrera/))
- Front: indicators, side lights and fog lamps leave the headlamp and move into two separate horizontal modules on the front apron above the outer intakes; redesigned three-part intake layout ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071130055840/http://www.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY08_PCGB.pdf), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2005-911_carrera/), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2005-Front_Three-Quarter.8e284e72.jpg))
- Body: all visible panels new except the roof; more muscular wings, accentuated arches, stronger waist, doors inset with more pronounced lower sills; width 1,770 -> 1,808 mm; tracks 1,465/1,500 -> 1,486/1,534 ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/6.-Generation-Porsche-911,-(997),-2004-012.html), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2005-911_carrera/), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20040614021122/http://www2.porsche.com:80/english/int/news/pressreleases/pag/2004-05-12-2.htm), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071130055840/http://www.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY08_PCGB.pdf))
- Mirrors: single-stalk -> dual-arm (Carrera GT style); door handles: bow-type ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20040614021122/http://www2.porsche.com:80/english/int/news/pressreleases/pag/2004-05-12-2.htm), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071130055840/http://www.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY08_PCGB.pdf))
- Rear: new wide red/silver tail lamps, auto spoiler with integrated air scoops and LED brake light on its trailing edge, LED third brake light at base of rear screen, rear wiper mounted on the glass ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2005-911_carrera/), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071130055840/http://www.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY08_PCGB.pdf))
- Exhaust: one oval tailpipe per side on Carrera (Carrera S: twin round pipes each side) ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071130055840/http://www.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY08_PCGB.pdf), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2005-911_carrera/))
- Wheels: 17-inch Carrera II -> 18-inch 'Carrera III' five-spoke (8J/10J; 235/40 & 265/40 ZR18) ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071130055840/http://www.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY08_PCGB.pdf), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2005-911_carrera/))
- Length: 4,430 -> 4,427 mm (RoW official); Cd 0.30 -> 0.28 ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20030724115326/http://www3.porsche.com:80/english/usa/911/c2/technicalspecifications/default.htm), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071130055840/http://www.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY08_PCGB.pdf), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20070618195353/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/featuresandspecs/))

### 13-997-1-2005 → 14-997-2-2009
- Front apron: larger air intakes; the 997.1 fog/indicator/side-light modules are replaced by horizontal LED units (indicator + LED DRL + position light) above the outer intakes; no front fog lamps ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20091227041946/http://files.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY10_EN.pdf), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2009-911_carrera/), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20110928143539/http://files.porsche.com/filestore.aspx/default.pdf?pool=japan&type=download&id=porscheservice-ownersmanualdownload-911carrera&lang=jp&filetype=default&version=3))
- Headlamps: same oval shape; Bi-Xenon now standard with headlight cleaning; optional dynamic cornering light ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20091227041946/http://files.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY10_EN.pdf), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2009-911_carrera/))
- Rear: new LED tail lights drawn further into the wing and tapering outward (red + silver-transparent lenses); tailpipes integrated seamlessly into the rear apron; black trim along the bottom of the rear apron ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20091227041946/http://files.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY10_EN.pdf), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2009-911_carrera/))
- Mirrors: enlarged (similar look) with a water gutter above the housing ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20091227041946/http://files.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY10_EN.pdf), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2009-911_carrera/))
- Wheels: 18-inch Carrera III -> 18-inch 'Carrera IV' twin-blade five-spoke; rear rim 10J ET58 -> 10.5J ET60 ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20091227041946/http://files.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY10_EN.pdf), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071130055840/http://www.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY08_PCGB.pdf), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2009-911_carrera/), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20110928143539/http://files.porsche.com/filestore.aspx/default.pdf?pool=japan&type=download&id=porscheservice-ownersmanualdownload-911carrera&lang=jp&filetype=default&version=3))
- Length 4,427 -> 4,435 mm (RoW official); width/height/wheelbase unchanged (1,808/1,310/2,350); Cd 0.28 -> 0.29 ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071130055840/http://www.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY08_PCGB.pdf), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20091227041946/http://files.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY10_EN.pdf))

### 14-997-2-2009 → 15-991-1-2012
- Wheelbase +100 mm (to 2,450 mm) with shorter front and rear overhangs; lower roof line; wider front track (+46 mm Carrera / +52 mm S) ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:05c3198c-772f-4e4d-88ad-b6fce31b66ed/S11_0327_en.pdf), [web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20130520121306/http://www.porsche.com/kbase.aspx?pool=uk&type=kbase&id=991-c2-detail-concept-exterior&lang=none&callpath=%2finternational%2fmodels%2f911%2f911-carrera%2fdetail%2f), [auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_2014.pdf))
- Exterior mirrors move from the A-pillar/window triangle (997) onto the upper edge of the door ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:05c3198c-772f-4e4d-88ad-b6fce31b66ed/S11_0327_en.pdf), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/997/997/))
- New narrow, horizontally stretched LED taillights; reflectors moved lower ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20130520121306/http://www.porsche.com/kbase.aspx?pool=uk&type=kbase&id=991-c2-detail-concept-exterior&lang=none&callpath=%2finternational%2fmodels%2f911%2f911-carrera%2fdetail%2f), [auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_2014.pdf))
- Front LED light units (DRL/position/indicator) above the side intakes are retained from the 997.2 concept but become slim, swept strips contrasting with the round headlights ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20130520121306/http://www.porsche.com/kbase.aspx?pool=uk&type=kbase&id=991-c2-detail-concept-exterior&lang=none&callpath=%2finternational%2fmodels%2f911%2f911-carrera%2fdetail%2f), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/997/997/))
- Wider, variably extending rear spoiler integrated in the lid ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:05c3198c-772f-4e4d-88ad-b6fce31b66ed/S11_0327_en.pdf))
- Standard wheels grow one inch: 19 in (Carrera) / 20 in (Carrera S) vs 18/19 in on 997.2 ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20130520121306/http://www.porsche.com/kbase.aspx?pool=uk&type=kbase&id=991-c2-detail-concept-exterior&lang=none&callpath=%2finternational%2fmodels%2f911%2f911-carrera%2fdetail%2f), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/997/997/))
- Length: Porsche/Wikipedia put the 991.1 Carrera at 4,491 mm; Wikipedia says +70 mm vs 997 - check against stop 14 length ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20130520150153/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/featuresandspecs/), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(991)))

### 15-991-1-2012 → 16-991-2-2016
- DRL moves into the headlight as a four-point LED signature; apron light strips narrowed (position + indicator only) ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:29e27626-6871-4885-93cc-cb48d10b76b9/PCNA18_0115_us.pdf))
- Rear-lid grille changes from horizontal to vertical louvres (high-gloss black) ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:aed99995-b38a-42ad-846b-878cbd249c6f/~6042931.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:29e27626-6871-4885-93cc-cb48d10b76b9/PCNA18_0115_us.pdf))
- New 3D taillights with four-point brake lights ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:aed99995-b38a-42ad-846b-878cbd249c6f/~6042931.pdf))
- Door handles lose their recessed grip shells ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:aed99995-b38a-42ad-846b-878cbd249c6f/~6042931.pdf))
- Front fascia with stronger profile, two horizontal vanes in the centre intake, active flaps ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:29e27626-6871-4885-93cc-cb48d10b76b9/PCNA18_0115_us.pdf))
- Intercooler outlets appear at the lower sides of the rear fascia ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:29e27626-6871-4885-93cc-cb48d10b76b9/PCNA18_0115_us.pdf))
- Length 4,491 -> 4,499 mm; height 1,303 -> 1,294 mm (PASM -10 mm standard) ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20130520150153/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/featuresandspecs/), [auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_2016-1.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:aed99995-b38a-42ad-846b-878cbd249c6f/~6042931.pdf))
- New standard wheels with five slim twin spokes; rear rims +0.5 in ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:aed99995-b38a-42ad-846b-878cbd249c6f/~6042931.pdf))
- First active cooling-air flaps in the front intakes (three per intake, staged), plus larger PASM-Sport front lip option ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:29e27626-6871-4885-93cc-cb48d10b76b9/PCNA18_0115_us.pdf), [presskit.porsche.de](https://presskit.porsche.de/models/en/porsche-new-911/topic/eighth-generation-of-an-icon.html?file=daten/assets/models-porsche-new-911/documents/press/en/PAG_Valencia_PM_EN.pdf (via http://web.archive.org/web/20210509100604id_/ snapshot)))
- Tail-light strip on AWD cars becomes narrower and blends into the lamps (rear-drive cars still have separate lamps) ([auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_2017.pdf))

### 16-991-2-2016 → 17-992-1-2019
- All models get the wide body: rear +44 mm on rear-drive, front +45 mm ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/products/porsche-911-new-eigth-generation-992-timeless-machine-design-icon-world-premiere-los-angeles-2018-16522.html))
- Length +20 mm (4,499 -> 4,519), height +4 mm (1,294 -> 1,298), wheelbase unchanged ([presskit.porsche.de](https://presskit.porsche.de/workshops/en/porsche-new-911/downloads.html?file=daten/assets/workshops-porsche-new-911/documents/presentation/en/TWS_992_Body_EN.pdf (retrieved via http://web.archive.org/web/2024id_/ snapshot)))
- Bonnet extended forward with recess before windscreen ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/products/porsche-911-new-eigth-generation-992-timeless-machine-design-icon-world-premiere-los-angeles-2018-16522.html))
- LED headlights standard (991.2: Bi-Xenon), set at a sharper angle ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:cea78bb0-8770-434e-be37-ca597fda76ad/PAG_991_Cabrio_Athen_PM_EN.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/8.-Generation---Design-und-Modellvarianten.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:29e27626-6871-4885-93cc-cb48d10b76b9/PCNA18_0115_us.pdf))
- Separate taillights replaced by seamless full-width LED light bar ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/products/porsche-911-new-eigth-generation-992-timeless-machine-design-icon-world-premiere-los-angeles-2018-16522.html))
- Flush pop-out door handles; new rectangular mirrors ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/products/porsche-911-new-eigth-generation-992-timeless-machine-design-icon-world-premiere-los-angeles-2018-16522.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:cea78bb0-8770-434e-be37-ca597fda76ad/PAG_991_Cabrio_Athen_PM_EN.pdf))
- Grille louvres echo rear-window contour with integrated third brake light; spoiler much wider ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/products/porsche-911-new-eigth-generation-992-timeless-machine-design-icon-world-premiere-los-angeles-2018-16522.html))
- Rear plate moved down between the tailpipes; black lower rear section ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:cea78bb0-8770-434e-be37-ca597fda76ad/PAG_991_Cabrio_Athen_PM_EN.pdf))
- Staggered wheel diameters (rear one inch larger) ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:cea78bb0-8770-434e-be37-ca597fda76ad/PAG_991_Cabrio_Athen_PM_EN.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/8.-Generation---Karosserievarianten-und-Innovationen.html))
- Front: apron light modules much narrower, intakes unified by a continuous black trim, side intakes enlarged, cooling flaps now continuously variable ([presskit.porsche.de](https://presskit.porsche.de/models/en/porsche-new-911/topic/eighth-generation-of-an-icon.html?file=daten/assets/models-porsche-new-911/documents/press/en/PAG_Valencia_PM_EN.pdf (via http://web.archive.org/web/20210509100604id_/ snapshot)))
- Grille: 9 vertical slats per side + central vertical '11' brake light, framed with the rear window by a high-gloss black panel ([presskit.porsche.de](https://presskit.porsche.de/models/en/porsche-new-911/topic/eighth-generation-of-an-icon.html?file=daten/assets/models-porsche-new-911/documents/press/en/PAG_Valencia_PM_EN.pdf (via http://web.archive.org/web/20210509100604id_/ snapshot)), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(992)))
- Overhangs (derived): front grows to ~1,028 mm (bonnet pulled forward), rear ~1,041 mm ([files.porsche.com](https://files.porsche.com/filestore/image/multimedia/none/992-c2-modelimage-sideshot/model/5df3abc0-999a-11e9-80c4-005056bbdc38/porsche-model.png (linked from http://web.archive.org/web/20200227225631/https://www.porsche.com/uk/models/911/911-carrera-models/)), [files.porsche.com](https://files.porsche.com/filestore/image/multimedia/none/992-c2-technicalspecs/normal/ae5c8230-999a-11e9-80c4-005056bbdc38/porsche-normal.jpg))

### 17-992-1-2019 → 18-992-2-2024
- Apron lights deleted, all lighting in the headlights; bigger horizontal intakes ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:baaedc84-1430-447f-9bd1-94636087e9bc/PAG_Pressemappe_911-EN.pdf))
- Front plate holder relocated with high-gloss sensor panel below it ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:baaedc84-1430-447f-9bd1-94636087e9bc/PAG_Pressemappe_911-EN.pdf))
- Rear light strip redesigned with light arc; raised PORSCHE lettering ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:baaedc84-1430-447f-9bd1-94636087e9bc/PAG_Pressemappe_911-EN.pdf))
- Rear grille louvres reduced from nine to five per side + two extra intakes ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:baaedc84-1430-447f-9bd1-94636087e9bc/PAG_Pressemappe_911-EN.pdf))
- Rear plate moved up; lower rear black; new tailpipes in diffuser fins ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:baaedc84-1430-447f-9bd1-94636087e9bc/PAG_Pressemappe_911-EN.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en_US/2024/products/porsche-new-911-world-premiere-hybrid-36337.html))
- Length 4,519 -> 4,542 mm (+23) ([de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_992), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:7f3751ae-477c-48a5-b087-31f4fde1c124/pag-911-carrera-en.pdf))
- Overhangs (derived): rear grows to ~1,073 mm (new rear apron), front ~1,020 mm ([a.storyblok.com](https://a.storyblok.com/f/322327/2616x712/5d81165a01/cz25w01ix0010911-carrera-side.png))
- Published track: EU sheet 1,597/1,551 mm (vs 1,591/1,557 on 992.1); US sheets list 1,591/1,557 for both - treat as unchanged within +/-6 mm ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:7f3751ae-477c-48a5-b087-31f4fde1c124/pag-911-carrera-en.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:86fc9a9b-ece1-4cca-9930-39a78e89baf3/992.2%20911%20Carrera%20and%20Carrera%20Cabriolet%20Technical%20Specifications.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:5f3af6f6-7e71-41c1-85ed-51f215261b42/PAG_911Carrera_EN.pdf))

## Per-stop detail

### 00-t7-1959 — Porsche Type 754 T7 (a.k.a. Type 695 / 'T7 prototype') - F. A. 'Butzi' Porsche four-seat design study for the 356 successor

- **blueprints**: `blueprints/00-t7-1959/` — primary:  — NO orthographic drawing of the Type 754 T7 found (the-blueprints.com, drawingdatabase.com, getoutlines.com, carblueprints.info and Wikimedia Commons searched; Commons only has photographs of the museum car, e.g. 'File:Porsche Typ 754 1959 T7 RSideFront PorscheM 9June2013 (14825880219).jpg' and 'File:Porsche 754 T7 Prototype 1959 backleft 2010-03-12 A.jpg').
- **production_years**: 1959 design model / full-scale mock-up; 1960-61 drivable one-off prototype (not a production car) — _Porsche Newsroom: F. A. Porsche 'produced a model of a coupe' in 1959 and 'the prototype 754 was created in 1960 as a highly promising four-seater study'. Wikipedia infobox: 'Production 1961'. Car and Driver: 'the full-scale mockup of the car was cobbled together in the waning days of 1959'. Porsche Museum plaque (#301) dates it 1959._ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-evolutionary-history-754-901-ferry-porsche-14641.html), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_Typ_754_T7), [caranddriver.com](https://www.caranddriver.com/news/butzis-rough-draft-the-porsche-t7-concept-was-the-911s-direct-forefather))
- **reference_variant**: The single surviving T7 prototype in the Porsche Museum, Stuttgart (green metallic, as displayed 2009-2013) ([en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_Typ_754_T7), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-evolutionary-history-754-901-ferry-porsche-14641.html), [commons.wikimedia.org](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/Category:Porsche_754_T7))
- **dimensions_mm**
  - **length**: _not found_ — _not published (checked: Porsche Newsroom 754->901 article, Wikipedia Typ 754 T7, Car and Driver, Motor1, PCA archive, Porsche Museum plaque). ESTIMATE ~4,060-4,200 mm: Motor1 studio side photo L/WB = 1798/1031 px = 1.744 x WB 2,330 = ~4,064 mm, plus a few % for perspective at the bumper extremes._ ([cdn.motor1.com](https://cdn.motor1.com/images/mgl/koW4XM/s1/porsche-type-754-t7-concept-1963.jpg))
  - **width**: _not found_ — _not found in: Porsche Newsroom, Wikipedia, Car and Driver, Motor1, PCA archive, museum plaque_
  - **height**: _not found_ — _not published. ESTIMATE ~1,260 mm (+-60): Motor1 studio side photo roof-to-ground 556 px / WB 1031 px x 2,330._ ([cdn.motor1.com](https://cdn.motor1.com/images/mgl/koW4XM/s1/porsche-type-754-t7-concept-1963.jpg))
  - **wheelbase**: 2330 — _DERIVED photo measurement (recommended value). Rim-flange calibration from the Porsche Museum studio side photo of 901 No. 57 (WB 2211 mm = 1585.5 px, 15-in rim flange 298 px -> 415.6 mm). T7: Motor1 studio side photo WB 1031 px / rim 185 px = 5.57 -> ~2,316 mm; Commons 'Projet T7' (Retromobile 2013, Panasonic FZ8, 36 mm-equiv.) WB 1087 px / rim 192.5 px = 5.65 -> ~2,347 mm. Assumes the T7 runs 15-in rims like the 356/901 (same slotted steel-wheel style; rim size itself not documented). Published claims conflict: Porsche Newsroom 'the 2.40-metre wheelbase from the previous design was shortened to 2.20 metres' (2,400); Wikipedia/PCA/Motor1 '100 mm longer than the 356' (356 = 2,100 mm per Wikipedia -> 2,200). Photos exclude 2,200; 2,400 is ~3% above both photo estimates._ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-evolutionary-history-754-901-ferry-porsche-14641.html), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_Typ_754_T7), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20100708044429/http://vista.pca.org/stl/695.htm), [motor1.com](https://www.motor1.com/news/690730/porsche-type-754-concept-history/), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_356), [cdn.motor1.com](https://cdn.motor1.com/images/mgl/koW4XM/s1/porsche-type-754-t7-concept-1963.jpg), [commons.wikimedia.org](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Projet_T7.JPG), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/af972fe2-03e8-4723-aca7-6bc41556df09.zip))
  - **track_front**: _not found_ — _not found in: Porsche Newsroom, Wikipedia, Car and Driver, Motor1, PCA archive, museum plaque_
  - **track_rear**: _not found_ — _not found in: Porsche Newsroom, Wikipedia, Car and Driver, Motor1, PCA archive, museum plaque_
  - **ground_clearance**: _not found_ — _not found in: Porsche Newsroom, Wikipedia, Car and Driver, Motor1, PCA archive, museum plaque_
- **overhang_mm**
  - **front**: _not found_ — _not published; photo estimate only: front bumper to front wheel centre ~334 px of 1798 px overall (Motor1 studio side) -> ~18.5% of length (~750 mm at L~4,064) - shorter than the 901 (~22%)_ ([cdn.motor1.com](https://cdn.motor1.com/images/mgl/koW4XM/s1/porsche-type-754-t7-concept-1963.jpg))
  - **rear**: _not found_ — _not published; photo estimate: rear wheel centre to rear bumper ~433 px -> ~24% of length (~980 mm)_ ([cdn.motor1.com](https://cdn.motor1.com/images/mgl/koW4XM/s1/porsche-type-754-t7-concept-1963.jpg))
- **other_published**
  - **curb_weight_kg**: _not found_ — _not found in: Newsroom, Wikipedia, Car and Driver, Motor1, museum plaque_
  - **drag_cd**: _not found_ — _not found in: Newsroom, Wikipedia, Car and Driver, Motor1_
  - **windshield_rake_deg**: _not found_ — _not found in: Newsroom, Wikipedia, Car and Driver, Motor1_
  - **top_speed_kmh**: 200 — _Wikipedia; also legible on the Porsche Museum plaque in the Commons photo_ ([en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_Typ_754_T7), [commons.wikimedia.org](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Porsche_754_T7_Prototype_1959_frontright_2010-03-12_A.jpg))
  - **seats**: 4 — _Newsroom: 'four-seater study'; Wikipedia: 'unlike the 911, the T7 had four seats'_ ([en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_Typ_754_T7), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-evolutionary-history-754-901-ferry-porsche-14641.html))
  - **engine_note**: Type 695 with 356 pushrod 1600 Super engine; as Type 754 fitted with the 2-litre 4-cam Carrera 2 engine (130 PS on the museum plaque) ([en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(classic)), [caranddriver.com](https://www.caranddriver.com/news/butzis-rough-draft-the-porsche-t7-concept-was-the-911s-direct-forefather), [commons.wikimedia.org](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Porsche_754_T7_Prototype_1959_frontright_2010-03-12_A.jpg))
- **wheels**
  - **tyre_front**: _not found_ — _not found in: Newsroom, Wikipedia, Car and Driver, Motor1, museum plaque_
  - **tyre_rear**: _not found_ — _not found in: Newsroom, Wikipedia, Car and Driver, Motor1, museum plaque_
  - **rim_front**: _not found_ — _not found in: Newsroom, Wikipedia, Car and Driver, Motor1, museum plaque_
  - **rim_rear**: _not found_ — _not found in: Newsroom, Wikipedia, Car and Driver, Motor1, museum plaque_
  - **rim_diameter_in**: _not found_ — _not documented; the rim-to-body proportions match the 15-in wheels of the 901 (see wheelbase method)_ ([cdn.motor1.com](https://cdn.motor1.com/images/mgl/koW4XM/s1/porsche-type-754-t7-concept-1963.jpg))
  - **design_name**: Silver-painted steel disc wheels with a ring of slots and domed chrome hubcaps (356/901 style) — _visual: museum car and 1960s archive photo_ ([cdn.motor1.com](https://cdn.motor1.com/images/mgl/koW4XM/s1/porsche-type-754-t7-concept-1963.jpg), [commons.wikimedia.org](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Projet_T7.JPG), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:4f65eb89-1762-47ae-b2cb-4da523a81279/b-HNE%205391.jpg))
  - **spoke_count**: 0 — _disc wheel_ ([cdn.motor1.com](https://cdn.motor1.com/images/mgl/koW4XM/s1/porsche-type-754-t7-concept-1963.jpg))
  - **finish**: silver steel rim + chrome domed hubcap with small centre emblem ([cdn.motor1.com](https://cdn.motor1.com/images/mgl/koW4XM/s1/porsche-type-754-t7-concept-1963.jpg), [commons.wikimedia.org](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Projet_T7.JPG))
  - **hub_cap_center**: small round emblem in centre of domed cap (visual) ([commons.wikimedia.org](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Projet_T7.JPG))
  - **options_notes**: None
- **headlights**
  - **shape**: round — _Motor1: 'the circular headlamps are an inheritance from the 356'_ ([motor1.com](https://www.motor1.com/news/690730/porsche-type-754-concept-history/), [cdn.motor1.com](https://cdn.motor1.com/images/mgl/0eNOmn/s1/porsche-type-754-t7-concept-1963.jpg))
  - **surround_ring**: chrome ring ([cdn.motor1.com](https://cdn.motor1.com/images/mgl/0eNOmn/s1/porsche-type-754-t7-concept-1963.jpg))
  - **mounting**: inclined/upright in the tops of the free-standing front wings (901/911-style 'tunnels'), flat bonnet between — _Newsroom: 'The flattened bonnet between the free-standing wings. The inclined built-in headlights'_ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-evolutionary-history-754-901-ferry-porsche-14641.html), [cdn.motor1.com](https://cdn.motor1.com/images/mgl/0eNOmn/s1/porsche-type-754-t7-concept-1963.jpg))
  - **lens_technology**: _not found_ — _not found in: no source_
  - **washers**: _not found_ — _none visible_
  - **other**: None
- **front**
  - **bumper_type**: wide body-colour bumper carrying a prominent full-width chrome strip; no guards — _Motor1: 'the front end features a chrome element that makes the car appear wider'_ ([motor1.com](https://www.motor1.com/news/690730/porsche-type-754-concept-history/), [cdn.motor1.com](https://cdn.motor1.com/images/mgl/0eNOmn/s1/porsche-type-754-t7-concept-1963.jpg))
  - **overriders**: none ([cdn.motor1.com](https://cdn.motor1.com/images/mgl/0eNOmn/s1/porsche-type-754-t7-concept-1963.jpg), [commons.wikimedia.org](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Porsche_754_T7_Prototype_1959_frontright_2010-03-12_A.jpg))
  - **indicators**: clear lamp outboard + chrome slatted horn grille inboard, set under each headlight above the bumper (like the 901) ([cdn.motor1.com](https://cdn.motor1.com/images/mgl/0eNOmn/s1/porsche-type-754-t7-concept-1963.jpg))
  - **fog_lights**: _not found_ — _none visible_
  - **horn_grilles**: chrome slatted grilles below the headlights ([cdn.motor1.com](https://cdn.motor1.com/images/mgl/0eNOmn/s1/porsche-type-754-t7-concept-1963.jpg))
  - **air_intakes**: two dark rectangular openings in the lower valance below the bumper; chrome-framed fresh-air vent on the lid ahead of the windscreen ([cdn.motor1.com](https://cdn.motor1.com/images/mgl/0eNOmn/s1/porsche-type-754-t7-concept-1963.jpg))
  - **front_lid_hood**: flattened bonnet lying between the free-standing wings ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-evolutionary-history-754-901-ferry-porsche-14641.html))
  - **badge_crest**: coloured Porsche crest on the lid nose ([cdn.motor1.com](https://cdn.motor1.com/images/mgl/0eNOmn/s1/porsche-type-754-t7-concept-1963.jpg))
  - **other**: 'viewed head on, the concept could easily be mistaken for its production successor' — _PCA archive: 'the front half is identical to the production 911'_ ([caranddriver.com](https://www.caranddriver.com/news/butzis-rough-draft-the-porsche-t7-concept-was-the-911s-direct-forefather), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20100708044429/http://vista.pca.org/stl/695.htm))
- **rear**
  - **taillights**: horizontal rectangular lamps at the rear corners, wrapping round, sitting on the bumper line (red with a clear section) — _Motor1: 'at the rear we find a flat tail with horizontally developed [lamps]'_ ([motor1.com](https://www.motor1.com/news/690730/porsche-type-754-concept-history/), [commons.wikimedia.org](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Porsche_754_T7_in_the_Porsche-Museum_(2009)_1X7A0333.jpg), [commons.wikimedia.org](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Porsche_754_T7_Prototype_1959_backleft_2010-03-12_A.jpg))
  - **reflector_band**: none ([commons.wikimedia.org](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Porsche_754_T7_in_the_Porsche-Museum_(2009)_1X7A0333.jpg))
  - **bumper**: body-colour bumper with chrome strip; two tall black guards flanking a recessed centre panel covered with mesh ([commons.wikimedia.org](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Porsche_754_T7_in_the_Porsche-Museum_(2009)_1X7A0333.jpg), [commons.wikimedia.org](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Porsche_Typ_754_1959_T7_LSideRear_PorscheM_9June2013_(15012609555).jpg))
  - **exhaust**: two tailpipes exiting under the bumper either side of centre — _Motor1: 'two central exhaust outlets' - confirmed on the 2009 museum photo_ ([motor1.com](https://www.motor1.com/news/690730/porsche-type-754-concept-history/), [commons.wikimedia.org](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Porsche_754_T7_in_the_Porsche-Museum_(2009)_1X7A0333.jpg))
  - **license_plate**: _not found_ — _no plate fitted on museum car; centre panel between the guards presumably the plate area (not documented)_ ([commons.wikimedia.org](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Porsche_754_T7_in_the_Porsche-Museum_(2009)_1X7A0333.jpg))
  - **badging**: gold 'PORSCHE' script on the engine lid ([commons.wikimedia.org](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Porsche_754_T7_in_the_Porsche-Museum_(2009)_1X7A0333.jpg))
  - **other**: Rear 'somewhat different' from the 911 (Wikipedia); 'the rear of the car turned out very awkward' (PCA) ([en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_Typ_754_T7), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20100708044429/http://vista.pca.org/stl/695.htm))
- **rear_spoiler**
  - **type**: none ([commons.wikimedia.org](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Porsche_754_T7_in_the_Porsche-Museum_(2009)_1X7A0333.jpg))
  - **deploy**: _not found_ — _n/a_
- **engine_lid_grille**
  - **design**: No grille on the rounded engine lid; engine air enters through chrome slatted grilles on each rear flank just ahead of the taillights — _Car and Driver: 'its small side-mounted vents didn't allow enough airflow through the engine compartment'_ ([caranddriver.com](https://www.caranddriver.com/news/butzis-rough-draft-the-porsche-t7-concept-was-the-911s-direct-forefather), [commons.wikimedia.org](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Porsche_754_T7_in_the_Porsche-Museum_(2009)_1X7A0333.jpg), [cdn.motor1.com](https://cdn.motor1.com/images/mgl/koW4XM/s1/porsche-type-754-t7-concept-1963.jpg))
- **body**
  - **fender_flares**: none; narrow body ([cdn.motor1.com](https://cdn.motor1.com/images/mgl/koW4XM/s1/porsche-type-754-t7-concept-1963.jpg))
  - **rear_arch_width**: narrow (no flares) ([cdn.motor1.com](https://cdn.motor1.com/images/mgl/koW4XM/s1/porsche-type-754-t7-concept-1963.jpg))
  - **mirrors**: small round chrome mirror on a stalk on top of the front wing, ahead of the A-pillar ([cdn.motor1.com](https://cdn.motor1.com/images/mgl/koW4XM/s1/porsche-type-754-t7-concept-1963.jpg), [commons.wikimedia.org](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Porsche_754_T7_in_the_Porsche-Museum_(2009)_1X7A0333.jpg))
  - **door_handles**: chrome pull handle with push button ([cdn.motor1.com](https://cdn.motor1.com/images/mgl/koW4XM/s1/porsche-type-754-t7-concept-1963.jpg))
  - **side_windows**: framed door glass with front vent (quarter) window; long rear side window for the rear seats ([cdn.motor1.com](https://cdn.motor1.com/images/mgl/koW4XM/s1/porsche-type-754-t7-concept-1963.jpg), [commons.wikimedia.org](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Projet_T7.JPG))
  - **quarter_window**: large rear side window behind a slim B-pillar, ending at a near-vertical C-pillar edge ([cdn.motor1.com](https://cdn.motor1.com/images/mgl/koW4XM/s1/porsche-type-754-t7-concept-1963.jpg))
  - **drip_rails**: chrome roof-edge trim / drip rail (visual) ([cdn.motor1.com](https://cdn.motor1.com/images/mgl/koW4XM/s1/porsche-type-754-t7-concept-1963.jpg))
  - **roofline**: long, flat four-seat roof ending in a wrap-around rear window over a separate engine lid - 'notchback' read, not the 911 fastback — _Car and Driver: 'wraparound rear glass that transforms a notchback shape into a fastback design'; Newsroom: Ferry Porsche insisted on the fastback, rejecting the four-seater._ ([caranddriver.com](https://www.caranddriver.com/news/butzis-rough-draft-the-porsche-t7-concept-was-the-911s-direct-forefather), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-evolutionary-history-754-901-ferry-porsche-14641.html), [cdn.motor1.com](https://cdn.motor1.com/images/mgl/koW4XM/s1/porsche-type-754-t7-concept-1963.jpg))
  - **fuel_filler**: _not found_ — _not found in: no source; a small round flap is visible low on the rear flank behind the door on the museum car (function not documented)_
  - **windshield**: A-pillars/windscreen already 911-like — _Newsroom lists 'the A-pillars with the windscreen' among the 911 characteristics present on the 754_ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-evolutionary-history-754-901-ferry-porsche-14641.html))
  - **chrome_trim**: chrome bumper strips, window frames, rear-flank grilles ([cdn.motor1.com](https://cdn.motor1.com/images/mgl/koW4XM/s1/porsche-type-754-t7-concept-1963.jpg), [commons.wikimedia.org](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Porsche_754_T7_in_the_Porsche-Museum_(2009)_1X7A0333.jpg))
  - **side_repeaters**: _not found_ — _none visible_
  - **rocker_sills**: plain sill, no chrome strip (visual) ([cdn.motor1.com](https://cdn.motor1.com/images/mgl/koW4XM/s1/porsche-type-754-t7-concept-1963.jpg), [commons.wikimedia.org](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Projet_T7.JPG))
  - **other**: Developed from the 356 by F. A. Porsche; four real seats ([en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_Typ_754_T7), [motor1.com](https://www.motor1.com/news/690730/porsche-type-754-concept-history/))
- **lighting_signature**: Round chrome-ringed headlamps in the wing tunnels + clear lamp/slatted grille under each (911-like front); horizontal wrap-around taillamps ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-evolutionary-history-754-901-ferry-porsche-14641.html), [motor1.com](https://www.motor1.com/news/690730/porsche-type-754-concept-history/), [cdn.motor1.com](https://cdn.motor1.com/images/mgl/0eNOmn/s1/porsche-type-754-t7-concept-1963.jpg), [commons.wikimedia.org](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Porsche_754_T7_in_the_Porsche-Museum_(2009)_1X7A0333.jpg))
- **distinctive**
  - Front end almost identical to the later 901/911; greenhouse and tail very different (four-seat notchback with wrap-around rear glass, flat tail, side intake grilles, twin exhausts) ([caranddriver.com](https://www.caranddriver.com/news/butzis-rough-draft-the-porsche-t7-concept-was-the-911s-direct-forefather), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20100708044429/http://vista.pca.org/stl/695.htm), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_Typ_754_T7), [commons.wikimedia.org](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Porsche_754_T7_in_the_Porsche-Museum_(2009)_1X7A0333.jpg))
  - Longer wheelbase than the 901/911 (published 2,400 vs 2,200; photo-derived ~2,330) ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-evolutionary-history-754-901-ferry-porsche-14641.html), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_Typ_754_T7), [cdn.motor1.com](https://cdn.motor1.com/images/mgl/koW4XM/s1/porsche-type-754-t7-concept-1963.jpg))
- **open_questions**
  - Documentation sufficiency: ~17 CC-licensed Commons photos (front, rear, 3/4, near-side views; Category:Porsche_754_T7), Motor1 gallery incl. a clean studio side and front view, and 2 Porsche archive B/W photos - enough for a loose model, but NO published length/width/height/track and NO orthographic drawing. Recommend including T7 only as an approximate 'pre-history' morph source, or skipping it.
  - Wheelbase conflict: 2,400 mm (Porsche Newsroom) vs 2,200 mm (Wikipedia/PCA/Motor1); photo measurement ~2,330 mm (assumes 15-in rims).
- **estimates_mm**
  - **wheelbase**: 2330 — _photo-derived, +-60 mm (see dimensions_mm.wheelbase)_ ([cdn.motor1.com](https://cdn.motor1.com/images/mgl/koW4XM/s1/porsche-type-754-t7-concept-1963.jpg), [commons.wikimedia.org](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Projet_T7.JPG), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/af972fe2-03e8-4723-aca7-6bc41556df09.zip))
  - **length**: 4100 — _photo-derived, +-120 mm_ ([cdn.motor1.com](https://cdn.motor1.com/images/mgl/koW4XM/s1/porsche-type-754-t7-concept-1963.jpg))
  - **height**: 1260 — _photo-derived, +-60 mm_ ([cdn.motor1.com](https://cdn.motor1.com/images/mgl/koW4XM/s1/porsche-type-754-t7-concept-1963.jpg))
  - **width**: _not found_ — _no usable front photo scale; assume ~901 width (1600) only if needed - no source_

### 01-901-1963 — Porsche 901 - prototype / pre-series car of the 1963 IAA Frankfurt world premiere (12 Sep 1963)

- **blueprints**: `blueprints/01-901-1963/` — primary: `tbp-64510_porsche-901-1963_side.png` — Side elevation only; no dedicated 901 front/rear/top drawing found. For the other views use the SWB 911 drawings in ../02-911-swb-1964/ (901 prototype -> 911 production body is near-identical in plan; trim/badging differ).
- **production_years**: Shown 12 Sep 1963 (IAA, pre-series car); first prototype driven 9 Nov 1962; series production of the 901 from 14 Sep 1964; renamed 911 on 22 Oct 1964; 82 cars built as '901' ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-evolutionary-history-754-901-ferry-porsche-14641.html), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-f/911-f-901/), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_901), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-901-1963/))
- **reference_variant**: 1963 IAA / press pre-series 901 as specified in the English '901' brochure W 221 e printed 9.63 (Sept 1963); period photos of the yellow 'TYP 901' car and the white 'S-CU 902' prototype. (Production 901s built Sep-Nov 1964, e.g. museum car No. 57, are covered under stop 02 features.) ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:9db50efe-1600-4efe-b1b1-8891c93be833/b-HDK%207059.jpg), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/4e11a8c1-df69-4cac-93c7-5744ce416ec7.zip))
- **dimensions_mm**
  - **length**: 4135 — _901 brochure 9.63: 'Overall length 4135 mm (162.8 in.)'. (Newsroom 'I Am Legend' gives 164.2 in = 4171 mm for 'the 901' - later/rounded figure.)_ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf))
  - **width**: 1600 — _'Overall width 1600 mm (62.99 in.)'; Newsroom 'I Am Legend': 63.4 in (1610 mm)_ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf))
  - **height**: 1273 — _'Overall height 1273 mm (50.12 in.)'; Newsroom 'I Am Legend': 51.9 in (1318 mm). The 1963 sketch measures ~1270 mm roof-to-ground, i.e. matches 1273._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf))
  - **wheelbase**: 2204 — _'Wheelbase 2204 mm (86.77 in.)' (production 911: 2211)_ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf))
  - **track_front**: 1332 — _'Track, front 1332 mm (52.44 in.)'_ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf))
  - **track_rear**: 1312 — _'Track, rear 1312 mm (51.65 in.)'_ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf))
  - **ground_clearance**: 118 — _'Ground clearance 118 mm (4.65 in.)' (production 911: 150 mm)_ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf))
- **overhang_mm**
  - **front**: 907 — _DERIVED from the official Porsche 'Sketch, Porsche 911, 1963' side elevation (Newsroom media package, saved as blueprints/01-901-1963/official_porsche_sketch_1963_side.jpg): wheel-centre lines at x=925/2332 px (wheelbase 1407 px), extremes over front/rear bumper guards x=335/2998 px (2663 px). Front share of overhang = 590/1256 px, rear 666/1256 px. Published overhang sum 4135-2204 = 1931 mm split 590:666 -> front 907 mm (+-15 mm). (Sketch alone, scaled by WB: 924 mm.)_ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-construction-sketch-10955.html), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/db96470d-1b16-4bf9-b7a6-bdc7f65b3465.zip))
  - **rear**: 1024 — _DERIVED from the official Porsche 'Sketch, Porsche 911, 1963' side elevation (Newsroom media package, saved as blueprints/01-901-1963/official_porsche_sketch_1963_side.jpg): wheel-centre lines at x=925/2332 px (wheelbase 1407 px), extremes over front/rear bumper guards x=335/2998 px (2663 px). Front share of overhang = 590/1256 px, rear 666/1256 px. -> rear 1024 mm (+-15 mm). Sketch WB/L ratio 0.528 vs brochure 0.533 (1% agreement)._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-construction-sketch-10955.html), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/db96470d-1b16-4bf9-b7a6-bdc7f65b3465.zip))
- **other_published**
  - **curb_weight_kg**: 1001 — _DERIVED: brochure 'Weight/power ratio (ready to operate) 7.7 kg/HP (DIN)' x 130 HP = ~1001 kg_ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf))
  - **drag_cd**: _not found_ — _not found in: 901 brochure 9.63, Newsroom 754->901 and 'I Am Legend' articles_
  - **windshield_rake_deg**: 35 — _DERIVED: windscreen outer line in the 1963 side sketch runs from (1250,1191) to (1565,969) px = ~35 deg above horizontal (~55 deg from vertical), +-2 deg. Not a published figure._ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-construction-sketch-10955.html), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/db96470d-1b16-4bf9-b7a6-bdc7f65b3465.zip))
  - **turning_circle_m**: 10 ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf))
  - **fuel_tank_l**: 68 — _approx. 68 liters (production 911 brochures: 62 l)_ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf))
  - **roof_peak_position**: ~1306 mm behind front axle (59% of wheelbase), ~1270 mm above ground — _DERIVED from 1963 side sketch (roof peak x=1739-1773 px, y=954 px; ground y=1762 px)_ ([pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/db96470d-1b16-4bf9-b7a6-bdc7f65b3465.zip))
- **wheels**
  - **tyre_front**: 165 x 15, braced tread ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf))
  - **tyre_rear**: 165 x 15, braced tread ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf))
  - **rim_front**: 4 1/2 J x 15 ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf))
  - **rim_rear**: 4 1/2 J x 15 ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf))
  - **rim_diameter_in**: 15 ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf))
  - **design_name**: Steel disc wheel with a ring of ventilation slots + large domed chrome 356-style hubcap — _visual: brochure cover painting, 'S-CU 902' archive photo, 'TYP 901' photo_ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf), [pelicanparts.com](http://www.pelicanparts.com/literature/911/911_literature2.jpg), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/.imaging/mte/porsche-templating-theme/image_1290x726/dam/pnr/porsche_newsroom/Historie/911-Nr-57/Entwicklung-hin-zu-911/b-HNE-4727.jpg/jcr:content/b-HNE%204727.jpg), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:9db50efe-1600-4efe-b1b1-8891c93be833/b-HDK%207059.jpg))
  - **spoke_count**: 0 — _disc wheel_ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf))
  - **finish**: chrome domed hubcap; rim silver/chrome (brochure painting shows bright rim with slots) ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:9db50efe-1600-4efe-b1b1-8891c93be833/b-HDK%207059.jpg))
  - **hub_cap_center**: coloured (gold) Porsche crest in the centre of the hubcap — _visual on brochure cover painting_ ([pelicanparts.com](http://www.pelicanparts.com/literature/911/911_literature2.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf))
  - **options_notes**: None
- **headlights**
  - **shape**: round ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:9db50efe-1600-4efe-b1b1-8891c93be833/b-HDK%207059.jpg))
  - **surround_ring**: chrome ring ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:9db50efe-1600-4efe-b1b1-8891c93be833/b-HDK%207059.jpg))
  - **mounting**: upright in raised wing 'tunnels' at the top of each front wing; flat bonnet between — _Newsroom: 'Characteristic style elements include the distinctive headlight tunnels, the flat bonnet between them'_ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/1.-Generation-Design-und-Modellvarianten.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:9db50efe-1600-4efe-b1b1-8891c93be833/b-HDK%207059.jpg))
  - **lens_technology**: _not found_ — _not found in: 901 brochure 9.63_
  - **washers**: _not found_ — _none visible on period photos_
  - **other**: None
- **front**
  - **bumper_type**: slim body-colour steel bumper wrapping round the corners, with a full-width chrome trim strip carrying a black rubber insert; NO bumper guards on the 1963 show/press cars — _Visual: 'TYP 901' (b-HDK 7059) and 'S-CU 902' (captioned '1963, 911 Type 901') photos show a plain bumper. Production 901s of 1964 got chrome guards (Porsche Classic: 'Bumpers with chrome-plated bumper guards')._ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:9db50efe-1600-4efe-b1b1-8891c93be833/b-HDK%207059.jpg), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/.imaging/mte/porsche-templating-theme/image_1290x726/dam/pnr/porsche_newsroom/Historie/911-Nr-57/Entwicklung-hin-zu-911/b-HNE-4727.jpg/jcr:content/b-HNE%204727.jpg), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/4e11a8c1-df69-4cac-93c7-5744ce416ec7.zip))
  - **overriders**: none on 1963 prototypes (production 901 1964: two chrome guards) ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:9db50efe-1600-4efe-b1b1-8891c93be833/b-HDK%207059.jpg), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/.imaging/mte/porsche-templating-theme/image_1290x726/dam/pnr/porsche_newsroom/Historie/911-Nr-57/Entwicklung-hin-zu-911/b-HNE-4727.jpg/jcr:content/b-HNE%204727.jpg), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-f/911-f-901/), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/af972fe2-03e8-4723-aca7-6bc41556df09.zip))
  - **indicators**: combined lamp in a chrome housing below each headlight, outboard of the horn grille: clear parking-light section + amber indicator section, wrapping round the wing corner ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:9db50efe-1600-4efe-b1b1-8891c93be833/b-HDK%207059.jpg), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/af972fe2-03e8-4723-aca7-6bc41556df09.zip))
  - **fog_lights**: none on the 1963 cars (production 901/911: two fog lamps under the bumper) ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:9db50efe-1600-4efe-b1b1-8891c93be833/b-HDK%207059.jpg), [archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260429_20260429_1155))
  - **horn_grilles**: chrome horn grilles with horizontal slats below each headlight, inboard of the indicator ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-f/911-f-901/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:9db50efe-1600-4efe-b1b1-8891c93be833/b-HDK%207059.jpg))
  - **air_intakes**: no radiator grille (air-cooled); fresh-air intake slot at the rear edge of the front lid ahead of the windscreen — _visual; front sketch shows a slotted panel under the windscreen_ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:9db50efe-1600-4efe-b1b1-8891c93be833/b-HDK%207059.jpg), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/db96470d-1b16-4bf9-b7a6-bdc7f65b3465.zip))
  - **front_lid_hood**: flat bonnet sunk between the raised wings; both front wings bolt-on ('Both front fenders have been made detachable to simplify repairs') ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/1.-Generation-Design-und-Modellvarianten.html))
  - **badge_crest**: small coloured Porsche crest on the lid nose ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:9db50efe-1600-4efe-b1b1-8891c93be833/b-HDK%207059.jpg), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/4e11a8c1-df69-4cac-93c7-5744ce416ec7.zip))
  - **other**: windscreen wipers park on the right-hand side ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-f/911-f-901/))
- **rear**
  - **taillights**: separate small rectangular lamp units at each rear corner, sitting on top of the rear bumper and wrapping round the corner — _visual: brochure painting + 1963 side sketch_ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/db96470d-1b16-4bf9-b7a6-bdc7f65b3465.zip))
  - **reflector_band**: none ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf))
  - **bumper**: slim body-colour bumper with chrome/rubber strip wrapping the rear corners; guards visible in the 1963 side sketch ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/db96470d-1b16-4bf9-b7a6-bdc7f65b3465.zip))
  - **exhaust**: single tailpipe exiting below the rear bumper on the left side — _visual_ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/db96470d-1b16-4bf9-b7a6-bdc7f65b3465.zip), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/af972fe2-03e8-4723-aca7-6bc41556df09.zip))
  - **license_plate**: centred in the rear bumper/valance — _visual on production 901 No. 57_ ([pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/af972fe2-03e8-4723-aca7-6bc41556df09.zip))
  - **badging**: gold 'Porsche' script on the engine lid ('Porsche Schriftzug goldfarben'); '901' rear badge letters had already been made when Peugeot objected — _Newsroom: 'the first metal letters for the badge on the vehicle rear has already been punched'. Porsche Classic EN translates the gold script as 'Gold-coloured Porsche logo'._ ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/germany/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-f/911-f-901/), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-f/911-f-901/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-history-901-911-iaa-12274.html), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/af972fe2-03e8-4723-aca7-6bc41556df09.zip))
  - **other**: None
- **rear_spoiler**
  - **type**: none ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf))
  - **deploy**: _not found_ — _n/a_
- **engine_lid_grille**
  - **design**: chrome ventilation grille with horizontal bars at the top of the engine lid (also visible from the side as a louvred patch) — _Porsche Classic (DE): 'Heckdeckel mit verchromtem Lueftungsgitter' (EN page: 'Bonnet lid with chrome-plated ventilation grille')_ ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/germany/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-f/911-f-901/), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-f/911-f-901/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/db96470d-1b16-4bf9-b7a6-bdc7f65b3465.zip))
- **body**
