  - **fender_flares**: none - narrow body ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf))
  - **rear_arch_width**: narrow (no flares) ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf))
  - **mirrors**: round, chrome-plated exterior mirror on the front of the driver's door ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-f/911-f-901/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/.imaging/mte/porsche-templating-theme/image_1290x726/dam/pnr/porsche_newsroom/Historie/911-Nr-57/Entwicklung-hin-zu-911/b-HNE-4727.jpg/jcr:content/b-HNE%204727.jpg))
  - **door_handles**: chrome pull handle with push button ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/db96470d-1b16-4bf9-b7a6-bdc7f65b3465.zip))
  - **side_windows**: chrome-framed door glass with pivoting front vent window — _brochure: 'larger window areas have been provided'_ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/db96470d-1b16-4bf9-b7a6-bdc7f65b3465.zip))
  - **quarter_window**: large rear side window with chrome frame, curved rear edge following the fastback ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/db96470d-1b16-4bf9-b7a6-bdc7f65b3465.zip))
  - **drip_rails**: chrome roof drip rail (visual) ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf))
  - **roofline**: 2+2 fastback ('hatchback with a large rear window') ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-evolutionary-history-754-901-ferry-porsche-14641.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/1.-Generation-Design-und-Modellvarianten.html))
  - **fuel_filler**: round flap on top of the left front wing ([pelicanparts.com](http://www.pelicanparts.com/literature/911/911_literature2.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/.imaging/mte/porsche-templating-theme/image_1290x726/dam/pnr/porsche_newsroom/Historie/911-Nr-57/Entwicklung-hin-zu-911/b-HNE-4727.jpg/jcr:content/b-HNE%204727.jpg), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(classic)))
  - **windshield**: ~35 deg from horizontal (derived) ([pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/db96470d-1b16-4bf9-b7a6-bdc7f65b3465.zip))
  - **chrome_trim**: chrome bumper strips, window frames, sill strip, hubcaps, engine-lid grille ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/db96470d-1b16-4bf9-b7a6-bdc7f65b3465.zip))
  - **side_repeaters**: _not found_ — _none_
  - **rocker_sills**: narrow chrome sill strip ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf))
  - **other**: Body 120 mm longer than the 356 but 70 mm narrower; 'short overhangs' ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/1.-Generation-Design-und-Modellvarianten.html))
- **lighting_signature**: Round chrome-ringed headlamps in the wing tunnels; below each a slatted chrome horn grille + clear/amber corner lamp; small rectangular corner taillights on the rear bumper ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:9db50efe-1600-4efe-b1b1-8891c93be833/b-HDK%207059.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf))
- **distinctive**
  - Pre-series proportions: 28 mm shorter, 10 mm narrower and 47 mm lower (published) than the 1965 production 911, wheelbase 7 mm shorter, ground clearance 118 vs 150 mm ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/1963-porsche-901.pdf), [archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260421_20260421_1757))
  - Clean front bumper without guards and without fog lamps on the 1963 cars ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:9db50efe-1600-4efe-b1b1-8891c93be833/b-HDK%207059.jpg), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/.imaging/mte/porsche-templating-theme/image_1290x726/dam/pnr/porsche_newsroom/Historie/911-Nr-57/Entwicklung-hin-zu-911/b-HNE-4727.jpg/jcr:content/b-HNE%204727.jpg))
- **open_questions**
  - Whether the IAA stand car itself carried a '901' script on the engine lid (letters were made per Newsroom) - no rear photo of the stand car found.
  - Rear lamps/bumper guards of the 1963 show car not visible in any fetched rear photo; modelled from the 1963 sketch + brochure painting.

### 02-911-swb-1964 — 911 2.0 Coupe, short wheelbase (O-series MY1965-67, A-series MY1968)

- **blueprints**: `blueprints/02-911-swb-1964/` — primary: `tbp-40868_porsche-911-1967_factory-body-4view-mm.png`, `tbp-53230_porsche-911-1968_4view-colour.png` — Good coverage: factory body-shell drawing with mm dimensions (front/rear/side/top) + colour 4-view + vendor 4-views.
- **production_years**: Series production from 14 Sep 1964 (MY1965); O-series until July 1967; A-series MY1968 was the last short-wheelbase year (B-series MY1969 moved rear wheels 57 mm aft) ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-evolutionary-history-754-901-ferry-porsche-14641.html), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1964-1968/), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(classic)), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-seven-generations-part-1-original-911-16452.html))
- **reference_variant**: RECOMMENDED: 1965 911 2.0 Coupe (O-series, MY1965), as specified in the factory brochures W221 (3.65, German) and W 221 e (11.65, English); visual reference = Porsche Museum studio car captioned '911 2.0 Coupe (Model Year 1964)' and 901 No. 57 (Oct 1964) ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260421_20260421_1757), [archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260429_20260429_1155), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/4e11a8c1-df69-4cac-93c7-5744ce416ec7.zip), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/af972fe2-03e8-4723-aca7-6bc41556df09.zip))
- **dimensions_mm**
  - **length**: 4163 — _'Laenge 4163 mm' / 'Overall length 164.0 in. (4163 mm)'_ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260421_20260421_1757), [archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260429_20260429_1155), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/9), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/1.-Generation---Karosserievarianten.html))
  - **width**: 1610 ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260421_20260421_1757), [archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260429_20260429_1155), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/9))
  - **height**: 1320 — _unladen ('Hoehe (unbelastet)')_ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260421_20260421_1757), [archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260429_20260429_1155), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/9))
  - **wheelbase**: 2211 ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260421_20260421_1757), [archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260429_20260429_1155), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/9), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(classic)))
  - **track_front**: 1337 — _MY1965-66. MY1967 factory brochures: 1353 mm (911 and 911 S; 1967 US brochure + 911 S brochure 7-1966). Excellence lists 1367 for MY1967 and German Wikipedia 1367 for 1964-69 (conflict; factory values preferred)._ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260421_20260421_1757), [archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260429_20260429_1155), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/9))
  - **track_rear**: 1317 — _MY1965-66. MY1967: 1321.4 mm (911) / 1325.4 mm (911 S) per factory brochures; Excellence: 1335 (911) / 1339 (911 S) for MY1967._ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260421_20260421_1757), [archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260429_20260429_1155), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/9))
  - **ground_clearance**: 150 — _911 S brochure 7-1966 specifies 150 mm 'belastet' (laden)_ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260421_20260421_1757), [archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260429_20260429_1155), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/9))
- **overhang_mm**
  - **front**: 917 — _DERIVED from the official Porsche 'Sketch, Porsche 911, 1963' side elevation (Newsroom media package; saved as blueprints/02-911-swb-1964/official_porsche_sketch_1963_side.jpg): wheelbase 1407 px, overall 2663 px over bumper guards, front:rear overhang = 590:666 px. Published sum 4163-2211 = 1952 mm split 590:666 -> front ~917 mm (+-15)._ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260421_20260421_1757), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/db96470d-1b16-4bf9-b7a6-bdc7f65b3465.zip))
  - **rear**: 1035 — _DERIVED from the official Porsche 'Sketch, Porsche 911, 1963' side elevation (Newsroom media package; saved as blueprints/02-911-swb-1964/official_porsche_sketch_1963_side.jpg): wheelbase 1407 px, overall 2663 px over bumper guards, front:rear overhang = 590:666 px. -> rear ~1035 mm (+-15)._ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260421_20260421_1757), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/db96470d-1b16-4bf9-b7a6-bdc7f65b3465.zip))
- **other_published**
  - **curb_weight_kg**: 1080 — _'Leergewicht nach DIN 1080 kp' (911). 911 S MY1967: 1030 kp (911 S brochure 7-1966; 1967 US brochure)._ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260421_20260421_1757), [archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260429_20260429_1155))
  - **drag_cd**: 0.38 — _Excellence magazine spec table (no factory figure found in the 1965-67 brochures)_ ([excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/9))
  - **windshield_rake_deg**: 35 — _DERIVED ~35 deg above horizontal from the 1963 Porsche side sketch (+-2 deg); not published_ ([pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/db96470d-1b16-4bf9-b7a6-bdc7f65b3465.zip))
  - **turning_circle_m**: 10.3 ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260421_20260421_1757), [archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260429_20260429_1155))
  - **fuel_tank_l**: 62 ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260421_20260421_1757), [archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260429_20260429_1155))
- **wheels**
  - **tyre_front**: 165 HR 15 — _US MY1967 911: 6.9 H 15 standard, 165 HR 15 optional (1967 US brochure)_ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260421_20260421_1757), [archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260429_20260429_1155), [archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260421_20260421_1825))
  - **tyre_rear**: 165 HR 15 ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260421_20260421_1757), [archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260429_20260429_1155), [archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260421_20260421_1825))
  - **rim_front**: 4.5 J x 15 steel ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260421_20260421_1757), [archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260429_20260429_1155), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1964-1968/))
  - **rim_rear**: 4.5 J x 15 steel ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260421_20260421_1757), [archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260429_20260429_1155), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1964-1968/))
  - **rim_diameter_in**: 15 ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260421_20260421_1757))
  - **design_name**: Base 911: steel disc wheel with ventilation slots and domed chrome hubcap (Porsche Museum MY1964/65 cars have chromed wheels). 911 S from MY1967: forged Fuchs 5-spoke 'cloverleaf' light-alloy wheel 4.5 J x 15 — _Stuttcars: '4.5 x 15-inch wheels and chrome hub caps'. Porsche Classic: the 911 S 'was the first 911 to be fitted with the Fuchs wheels, featuring the characteristic five-spoke design'._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1964-1968/), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/4e11a8c1-df69-4cac-93c7-5744ce416ec7.zip), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/af972fe2-03e8-4723-aca7-6bc41556df09.zip), [archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260421_20260421_1825), [archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260429_20260429_1732), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-f/911-f-911/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/christophorus/porsche-christophorus-911-fuchs-rim-ferdinand-porsche-performance-wheels-13017.html))
  - **spoke_count**: 0 — _base car disc wheel; 911 S Fuchs = 5 spokes_ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260421_20260421_1757))
  - **finish**: painted steel wheel + chrome hubcap as standard; 'Chrome wheels' were an option (MY1967 US list) - the Porsche Museum cars wear chromed wheels; Fuchs (911 S): polished spokes with black-painted star background initially — _Newsroom 'Porsche for Dummies 2': Fuchs 'five wide spokes, which were initially blank and a black rim star background. Later, the entire rim star was black.'_ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260429_20260429_1732), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/af972fe2-03e8-4723-aca7-6bc41556df09.zip), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-for-dummies-ducktail-fuchs-rims-10058.html))
  - **hub_cap_center**: domed chrome hubcap with small crest ([pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/af972fe2-03e8-4723-aca7-6bc41556df09.zip), [archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260421_20260421_1757))
  - **options_notes**: Fuchs design credited to F. A. Porsche (Christophorus 378) or to Heinrich Klie (Newsroom 'Porsche for Dummies 2') - Porsche's own sources conflict. MY1968 911 L: 5.5 x 15 wheels (Excellence). ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/christophorus/porsche-christophorus-911-fuchs-rim-ferdinand-porsche-performance-wheels-13017.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-for-dummies-ducktail-fuchs-rims-10058.html), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/12))
- **headlights**
  - **shape**: round ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260421_20260421_1757), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/4e11a8c1-df69-4cac-93c7-5744ce416ec7.zip))
  - **surround_ring**: chrome ring ([pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/4e11a8c1-df69-4cac-93c7-5744ce416ec7.zip), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/af972fe2-03e8-4723-aca7-6bc41556df09.zip))
  - **mounting**: upright, recessed in the raised front-wing 'tunnels' ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/1.-Generation-Design-und-Modellvarianten.html), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/4e11a8c1-df69-4cac-93c7-5744ce416ec7.zip))
  - **lens_technology**: Bosch glass-lens headlamps (BOSCH marking visible on No. 57); asymmetric dipped beam ([pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/af972fe2-03e8-4723-aca7-6bc41556df09.zip), [archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260421_20260421_1757))
  - **washers**: _not found_ — _none (only windscreen washer listed in standard equipment)_
  - **other**: headlight flasher standard ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260421_20260421_1757), [archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260429_20260429_1155))
- **front**
  - **bumper_type**: slim body-colour steel bumper with full-width chrome strip + black rubber insert; two vertical chrome bumper guards ('Stossstangenhoerner'; rubber pads from 09/1966 - listed as optional on the MY1967 US 911, standard on the 911 S) ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/germany/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-f/911-f-911/), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-f/911-f-911/), [archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260429_20260429_1732), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/4e11a8c1-df69-4cac-93c7-5744ce416ec7.zip), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/af972fe2-03e8-4723-aca7-6bc41556df09.zip))
  - **overriders**: two chrome guards flanking the licence-plate area ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-f/911-f-911/), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/4e11a8c1-df69-4cac-93c7-5744ce416ec7.zip))
  - **indicators**: lamp unit below each headlight, outboard of the horn grille: amber indicator + clear parking-light section, wrapping round the wing corner (visible in side view) ([pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/af972fe2-03e8-4723-aca7-6bc41556df09.zip), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/4e11a8c1-df69-4cac-93c7-5744ce416ec7.zip), [archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260421_20260421_1757))
  - **fog_lights**: two fog lamps, rectangular with rounded ends, mounted under the front bumper either side of the licence plate (yellow lenses on 901 No. 57). Standard in the German/English brochures of 3.65 and 11.65; in the MY1967 US brochure 'Fog lights' are optional on the 911 and standard on the 911 S ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260421_20260421_1757), [archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260429_20260429_1155), [archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260429_20260429_1732), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/af972fe2-03e8-4723-aca7-6bc41556df09.zip), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/4e11a8c1-df69-4cac-93c7-5744ce416ec7.zip))
  - **horn_grilles**: chrome horn grilles with horizontal slats below each headlight, inboard of the lamp ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-f/911-f-911/), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/4e11a8c1-df69-4cac-93c7-5744ce416ec7.zip))
  - **air_intakes**: no front grille; fresh-air slot/grille at the rear edge of the front lid ahead of the windscreen — _'Heater and fresh-air vents'_ ([pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/af972fe2-03e8-4723-aca7-6bc41556df09.zip), [archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260429_20260429_1155))
  - **front_lid_hood**: flat bonnet between the wings, with a raised panel toward the windscreen; tow ring under the front ([pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/af972fe2-03e8-4723-aca7-6bc41556df09.zip), [archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260429_20260429_1155), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/1.-Generation-Design-und-Modellvarianten.html))
  - **badge_crest**: coloured enamel Porsche crest on the lid nose ([pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/af972fe2-03e8-4723-aca7-6bc41556df09.zip), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/4e11a8c1-df69-4cac-93c7-5744ce416ec7.zip))
  - **other**: wipers park on the right-hand side (to the left from Sept 1967) ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-f/911-f-911/))
- **rear**
  - **taillights**: Bosch units in chromed die-cast zinc (Zamak) housings at each rear corner, on top of the bumper, wrapping round the corner; European version inboard->outboard: red tail/brake with square red 'cat's eye' reflector, clear reversing light, amber indicator; US version red lenses — _'Two back-up lights' standard_ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2020/history/porsche-classic-indicator-tail-lights-911-short-wheelbase-available-23129.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:7679485e-0f54-4696-bf5e-c112d724f7ac/90163140400_001_low.jpeg), [archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260429_20260429_1155), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/af972fe2-03e8-4723-aca7-6bc41556df09.zip))
  - **reflector_band**: none - separate corner lamps only ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260421_20260421_1757), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/af972fe2-03e8-4723-aca7-6bc41556df09.zip))
  - **bumper**: slim body-colour bumper with chrome strip + rubber insert; two tall chrome guards flanking the licence plate and extending below the bumper ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:7679485e-0f54-4696-bf5e-c112d724f7ac/90163140400_001_low.jpeg), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/af972fe2-03e8-4723-aca7-6bc41556df09.zip), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/4e11a8c1-df69-4cac-93c7-5744ce416ec7.zip))
  - **exhaust**: single tailpipe below the rear bumper on the left side, angled outward ([pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/af972fe2-03e8-4723-aca7-6bc41556df09.zip), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/4e11a8c1-df69-4cac-93c7-5744ce416ec7.zip))
  - **license_plate**: centred between the rear guards ([pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/af972fe2-03e8-4723-aca7-6bc41556df09.zip), [archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260421_20260421_1757))
  - **badging**: gold 'PORSCHE' script centred on engine lid below the grille; gold '911' emblem at right - slanted until Aug 1965, straight and centred from Sept 1965 (911 T from MY1968: silver script/emblem) — _Porsche Classic (DE): 'Porsche Schriftzug goldfarben // 911-Emblem goldfarben, schraeg stehend; ab 09/1965 gerade und mittig montiert'_ ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/germany/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-f/911-f-911/), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-f/911-f-911/), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/4e11a8c1-df69-4cac-93c7-5744ce416ec7.zip), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/af972fe2-03e8-4723-aca7-6bc41556df09.zip))
  - **other**: None
- **rear_spoiler**
  - **type**: none ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260421_20260421_1757))
  - **deploy**: _not found_ — _n/a_
- **engine_lid_grille**
  - **design**: wide chrome-plated grille of horizontal bars (divided into sections by vertical ribs) at the top of the engine lid, just below the rear window — _Porsche Classic (DE): 'Heckdeckel mit verchromtem Lueftungsgitter'_ ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/germany/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-f/911-f-911/), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-f/911-f-911/), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/af972fe2-03e8-4723-aca7-6bc41556df09.zip), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/4e11a8c1-df69-4cac-93c7-5744ce416ec7.zip))
- **body**
  - **fender_flares**: none ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260421_20260421_1757))
  - **rear_arch_width**: narrow body (1610 mm overall) ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260421_20260421_1757))
  - **mirrors**: round, chrome-plated exterior mirror on the front of the driver's door ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-f/911-f-911/), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/af972fe2-03e8-4723-aca7-6bc41556df09.zip))
  - **door_handles**: chrome pull handle with push button — _Wikipedia: push buttons replaced by hidden pull levers only with the C-series (MY1970)_ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260421_20260421_1757), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(classic)), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/af972fe2-03e8-4723-aca7-6bc41556df09.zip))
  - **side_windows**: chrome-framed door glass with pivoting front quarter (vent) window — _'Quarter windows, burglarproof, front and rear'_ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260429_20260429_1155), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/af972fe2-03e8-4723-aca7-6bc41556df09.zip))
  - **quarter_window**: rear side window hinged to pop out ('Ausstellfenster hinten'), chrome frame ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260421_20260421_1757), [archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260429_20260429_1155))
  - **drip_rails**: chrome roof drip rails ([pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/af972fe2-03e8-4723-aca7-6bc41556df09.zip))
  - **roofline**: 2+2 fastback ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260421_20260421_1757), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/1.-Generation-Design-und-Modellvarianten.html))
  - **fuel_filler**: round flap on top of the left front wing, released from inside — _'Fuel tank cap operated from car interior'_ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260429_20260429_1155), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(classic)), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/af972fe2-03e8-4723-aca7-6bc41556df09.zip))
  - **windshield**: laminated; ~35 deg from horizontal (derived) ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260429_20260429_1155), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/db96470d-1b16-4bf9-b7a6-bdc7f65b3465.zip))
  - **chrome_trim**: narrow sill trims until Aug 1966, wide sill trims from Sept 1966; chrome window frames, bumper strips, guards ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-f/911-f-911/))
  - **side_repeaters**: _not found_ — _none on European cars; US MY1968 side markers not verified_
  - **rocker_sills**: chrome sill trim strip ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-f/911-f-911/), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/af972fe2-03e8-4723-aca7-6bc41556df09.zip))
  - **other**: None
- **lighting_signature**: Round chrome-ringed headlamps; slatted horn grille + amber/clear lamp under each; two fog lamps under the front bumper; small horizontal red/clear/amber corner taillights ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260421_20260421_1757), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:7679485e-0f54-4696-bf5e-c112d724f7ac/90163140400_001_low.jpeg), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/af972fe2-03e8-4723-aca7-6bc41556df09.zip))
- **distinctive**
  - Chrome brightwork era: chrome bumper strips/guards, window frames, sill trims, hubcaps, engine-lid grille ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-f/911-f-911/))
  - 'Short overhangs', headlight tunnels, flat bonnet, hatchback with large rear window ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/1.-Generation-Design-und-Modellvarianten.html))
- **open_questions**
  - US-market MY1968 side marker lamps not verified from a fetched source.
- **model_year_changes_within_stop**
  - MY1966 (from Sept 1965): '911' emblem straight and centred instead of slanted ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-f/911-f-911/))
  - MY1967 (from Aug/Sept 1966): bumper guards get rubber pads; wide sill trims; 911 S introduced with forged Fuchs 5-spoke 4.5 J x 15 wheels and ventilated discs; tracks 1353/1321.4 mm (911) ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-f/911-f-911/), [archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260421_20260421_1825), [archive.org](https://archive.org/details/101_20260429_20260429_1732))
  - MY1968 (A-series, from Aug 1967): wipers park on the left; 911 T with silver lettering; 911 L; 5.5 J x 15 wheels on 911 L (Excellence) ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-f/911-f-911/), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/12), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1964-1968/))

### 03-911-lwb-1969 — 911 long-wheelbase Coupé, B/C/D/E/F series (MY1969-MY1973), 2.0 / 2.2 / 2.4 litre, T / E / S

- **blueprints**: `blueprints/03-911-lwb-1969/` — primary: `tbp-68304_porsche-911e-1968_autocar-top-side-dims.png` — Top + side (Autocar-type, dimensioned) for a LWB 911E; plus a 3-view of the 1970 Monte-Carlo 911 S rally car. No clean front/rear line drawing of a road LWB coupe found - use the SWB front/rear views (../02) and add the 1969+ flared arches.
- **production_years**: MY1969 (B series, built from mid/Aug 1968) to MY1973 (F series, ended July 1973) — _Porsche press release: 'in the middle of 1968: from the 1969 model year, the wheelbase ... grew by 57 millimetres'. G-series production began Aug 1973 (press kit)._ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:c1a8cb3d-ea37-495f-8e29-48262a263c3c/S18_3621_en.pdf), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(classic)), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/2.-Generation-911,-(G-Serie),-1973---1989.html))
- **reference_variant**: 1972 911 S 2.4 Coupé (E series, MY1972) - numbers below are for this car unless noted — _Chosen because Porsche's own 1972 brochure documents its body equipment (front-apron spoiler, chrome sill strips, light-alloy wheels) and photos. Caveat: MY1972 is the only year with the oil-filler flap on the right rear wing; for a 'generic' LWB car omit that flap (MY1969-71 and MY1973 had none)._ ([excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/28), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_1972.pdf), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(1963)))
- **dimensions_mm**
  - **length**: 4147 — _Excellence 1972 911 S Coupé: 4,147 mm. Porsche press kit: the G-series body (4,291 mm) was '144 mm longer than its forerunner' -> 4,147 mm. Conflicts: 4,163 mm for all 1964-73 cars (de.wikipedia table; Porsche press kit gives 4,163 mm for the 1st-gen Targa: https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/1.-Generation---Karosserievarianten.html); Excellence lists 4,163 for 1969-71 US cars and 4,227 mm for US 1972-73 911 T/E (probably with US bumper guards). Recommended 4,147 for the 1972-73 911 S._ ([excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/28), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-%E2%80%93-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html))
  - **width**: 1610 — _Same for all 1969-73 coupés (narrow body)._ ([excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/28), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(1963)))
  - **height**: 1320 — _Unladen; source does not state load condition._ ([excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/28), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(1963)))
  - **wheelbase**: 2271 — _2.4-litre cars (MY1972-73): 2,271 mm (+3 mm via moved rear suspension mounting points - https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-model-research/porsche-911-research/porsche-911-f-series-1st-generation-research-hub/). MY1969-71: 2,268 mm (Porsche press release https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:c1a8cb3d-ea37-495f-8e29-48262a263c3c/S18_3621_en.pdf; Excellence 1969-71; de.wikipedia). History: 901 advertised 2,204 -> production 2,211 -> 2,268 (MY1969) -> 2,271 (MY1972) per Stuttcars._ ([excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/28), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(1963)), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-f-series-the-story/))
  - **track_front**: 1372 — _911 S/E 2.4 with 6J rims. 911 T 2.4 (5.5J): 1,360-1,362 mm; 1969-71 E/S: 1,374 mm._ ([excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/28), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(1963)))
  - **track_rear**: 1354 — _Excellence 1,354; de.wikipedia 1,355 (S 2.4). 911 T: 1,342-1,343; 1969-71 E/S: 1,355._ ([excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/28), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(1963)))
  - **ground_clearance**: 150 — _US-market spec sheet value; load condition not stated._ ([excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/28))
- **overhang_mm**
  - **front**: _not found_ — _Not published. Total overhang = 4,147 - 2,271 = 1,876 mm. ESTIMATES ONLY (low confidence): (a) G-series drawing 932/1,088 mm minus the 144 mm length difference split equally -> ~860 front / ~1,016 rear; (b) side-photo measurement of the F-series-bodied RS 2.7 (Porsche Museum, near-orthographic telephoto) gives a front share of ~44% of total overhang; the same method on the 1974 Carrera side photo reads 48% vs the drawing's true 46%, so ~42-44% -> ~790-825 front / ~1,050-1,085 rear. Measure an F-series orthographic before use._ ([excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/28), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/pnr/2022/History/50-Years-911-Carrera-RS-2.7/Galerie-NEU-2/S22_2797_fine.jpg), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250543&w=1000&h=528&crop=0&public=1&cs=b972431cd02e5e65))
  - **rear**: _not found_ — _See front note (estimates ~1,016-1,085 mm)._ ([excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/28), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/pnr/2022/History/50-Years-911-Carrera-RS-2.7/Galerie-NEU-2/S22_2797_fine.jpg))
- **other_published**
  - **curb_weight_kg**: 1075 — _de.wikipedia 911 S 2.4: 1,075 kg; Excellence: 2,370 lb (=1,075 kg)._ ([de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(1963)), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/28))
  - **drag_cd**: 0.38 — _Excellence lists 0.38 for every 1965-73 coupé - treat as generic/low confidence._ ([excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/28))
  - **windshield_rake_deg**: _not found_ — _not found in: Porsche newsroom press kits, 1972 brochure, Excellence, de/en Wikipedia_
- **wheels**
  - **tyre_front**: 185/70 VR 15 ([excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/28), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(1963)))
  - **tyre_rear**: 185/70 VR 15 ([excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/28), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(1963)))
  - **rim_front**: 6J x 15 ([excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/28), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(1963)))
  - **rim_rear**: 6J x 15 ([excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/28), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(1963)))
  - **rim_diameter_in**: 15 — _1972 brochure: 'these wheels (15 inches on all Porsches)'._ ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_1972.pdf))
  - **design_name**: Fuchs forged light-alloy 5-spoke ('clover-leaf') wheel — _1972 brochure lists 'Light alloy rims' as a 911 S-only item. Fuchs wheel designed by Heinrich Klie, introduced on the 911 S in 1966._ ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_1972.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-for-dummies-ducktail-fuchs-rims-10058.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:c1a8cb3d-ea37-495f-8e29-48262a263c3c/S18_3621_en.pdf))
  - **spoke_count**: 5 — _'five wide spokes'_ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-for-dummies-ducktail-fuchs-rims-10058.html))
  - **finish**: bright (polished/silver) spokes and rim with black-painted background between spokes ('rim star') — _Porsche: 'five wide spokes, which were initially blank and a black rim star background. Later, the entire rim star was black.'_ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-for-dummies-ducktail-fuchs-rims-10058.html), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250546&w=1000&h=749&crop=0&public=1&cs=bf30aed77c48fc7e))
  - **hub_cap_center**: small centre cap with Porsche crest (visible on factory photos) — _photo evidence only_ ([porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250546&w=1000&h=749&crop=0&public=1&cs=bf30aed77c48fc7e), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_1972.pdf))
  - **options_notes**: 911 T 2.4: 5.5J x 15 with 165 HR 15; 911 E 2.4: 6J x 15 with 185/70 VR 15 (a 1973 911 E factory photo shows flat-faced silver cast wheels - type not documented in text). From MY1969 Fuchs also offered in 14-inch as a comfort option (standard 14in rims on the 1969 911 E per C&SC). ([de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(1963)), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/21), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/16), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-f-series-the-story/), [classicandsportscar.com](https://www.classicandsportscar.com/features/buyers-guide-porsche-911-1964-73), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=251494&w=1000&h=652&crop=0&public=1&cs=e47f47221f2cbb49))
- **headlights**
  - **shape**: round, set upright at the front of the raised front wings ('headlight tunnels'), flat bonnet between them ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/1.-Generation-Design-und-Modellvarianten.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-%E2%80%93-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250546&w=1000&h=749&crop=0&public=1&cs=bf30aed77c48fc7e))
  - **surround_ring**: chrome headlamp ring — _photo evidence (1970 911 S factory photo; 1972 brochure p.7)_ ([porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250546&w=1000&h=749&crop=0&public=1&cs=bf30aed77c48fc7e), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_1972.pdf))
  - **mounting**: upright, integrated in the front wings ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-%E2%80%93-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html))
  - **lens_technology**: halogen headlights (MY1972 standard equipment) ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_1972.pdf))
  - **washers**: _not found_ — _not found for 1969-73 in: 1972 brochure, press kits, Wikipedia_
  - **other**: optional round auxiliary (fog/driving) lamps sat on the front bumper directly in front of the horn grilles on the 1970 911 S factory car — _911 S/T history notes the standard car had 'mounts for auxiliary headlights'._ ([porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250546&w=1000&h=749&crop=0&public=1&cs=bf30aed77c48fc7e), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/911-s-t/History-911-S-ST--1969-to-1972.html))
- **front**
  - **bumper_type**: slim body-coloured bumper blade wrapping around the nose, with black rubber profile strip along it — _1972 brochure: T/E 'Rubber profile strips on the bumpers'; 911 S 'Heavy-duty rubber profiles to protect the bumpers ... Light alloy bumper bracket'._ ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_1972.pdf), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250546&w=1000&h=749&crop=0&public=1&cs=bf30aed77c48fc7e))
  - **overriders**: none on the 1970 911 S and 1972 911 S factory photos; front overriders did exist as fitment (Ferry Porsche's July-1969 911 S 'is missing the overriders on its front bumper') — _Treat overriders as optional/market-dependent; recommended: omit on the model._ ([porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250546&w=1000&h=749&crop=0&public=1&cs=bf30aed77c48fc7e), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_1972.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-history-ferry-porsche-911-s-olive-green-metallic-13899.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/911-s-t/History-911-S-ST--1969-to-1972.html))
  - **indicators**: amber/clear indicator-parking lamp in the lower outer corner of each front wing, below the headlight and just above the bumper, with a horn grille immediately inboard; the lens wraps round the wing corner so it is also visible from the side; slightly bigger from the B series (MY1969); chrome lamp surrounds up to MY1972, blacked-out surrounds MY1973 ([porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250546&w=1000&h=749&crop=0&public=1&cs=bf30aed77c48fc7e), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_1972.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-model-research/porsche-911-research/porsche-911-f-series-1st-generation-research-hub/), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(classic)))
  - **fog_lights**: optional round lamps on top of the bumper (see headlights.other) ([porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250546&w=1000&h=749&crop=0&public=1&cs=bf30aed77c48fc7e))
  - **horn_grilles**: small slatted horn grilles in the front wing corners next to the indicators: chrome-plated aluminium up to MY1972 (E series), black plastic MY1973 (F series) — _1970 911 S photo shows chrome grilles; 1973 911 E photo shows black grilles_ ([en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(classic)), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250546&w=1000&h=749&crop=0&public=1&cs=bf30aed77c48fc7e), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=251494&w=1000&h=652&crop=0&public=1&cs=e47f47221f2cbb49))
  - **air_intakes**: no radiator grille (air-cooled). MY1972-73 911 S: 'Front apron designed as a spoiler' - a discreet glass-fibre chin spoiler under the front bumper (visible on the 1972 brochure front photo) ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_1972.pdf), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(classic)), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-f-series-the-story/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2019/history/porsche-klassik-911-eight-generations-dna-18914.html))
  - **front_lid_hood**: flat bonnet recessed between the higher front wings — _Mauer: 'a special topography where the wings are higher than the bonnet'_ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/1.-Generation-Design-und-Modellvarianten.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2019/history/porsche-klassik-911-eight-generations-dna-18914.html))
  - **badge_crest**: Porsche crest on the front lid near its leading edge — _photo evidence_ ([porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250546&w=1000&h=749&crop=0&public=1&cs=bf30aed77c48fc7e), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_1972.pdf))
  - **other**: slotted air-intake/cowl area at the base of the windscreen visible on 1972 brochure photo — _photo evidence, low confidence_ ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_1972.pdf))
- **rear**
  - **taillights**: one horizontal wrap-around lamp unit per rear-wing corner above the bumper; segments from inboard to outboard: red tail/brake, clear reversing lamp, amber indicator wrapping round the corner — _1972 brochure close-up (p.17) and Porsche Museum RS 2.7 close-up; 1972 brochure lists 'Two back-up lights'._ ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_1972.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/pnr/2022/History/50-Years-911-Carrera-RS-2.7/Galerie-NEU-2/S22_2790_fine.jpg))
  - **reflector_band**: none - the red reflector strip between the tail lamps arrived with the G series (MY1974) ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-%E2%80%93-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/2.-Generation---Design-und-Modellvarianten.html))
  - **bumper**: slim body-coloured rear bumper blade wrapping round the corners, with bright trim/rubber profile strip; two vertical overriders with chrome tubes flanking the licence plate (1972 brochure car) — _MY1973 North-American cars got black rubber overrider pads (https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-model-research/porsche-911-research/porsche-911-f-series-1st-generation-research-hub/)._ ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_1972.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/pnr/2022/History/50-Years-911-Carrera-RS-2.7/Galerie-NEU-2/S22_2793_fine.jpg))
  - **exhaust**: single tailpipe exiting below the bumper on the car's left side — _photo evidence from the (F-series based) RS 2.7; assumed identical for 911 S - verify_ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/pnr/2022/History/50-Years-911-Carrera-RS-2.7/Galerie-NEU-2/S22_2793_fine.jpg))
  - **license_plate**: centred on the rear panel below the engine lid, above/against the rear bumper ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/pnr/2022/History/50-Years-911-Carrera-RS-2.7/Galerie-NEU-2/S22_2793_fine.jpg), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_1972.pdf))
  - **badging**: model designation ('911 S' / '911 E' / '911 T') centred just below the engine-lid grille; 'PORSCHE' in widely spaced block letters low on the lid; '2.4' badge on the right of the grille (2.4-litre cars). Lettering gold-coloured up to MY1972, black on MY1973 — _1972 brochure cover photo shows the layout_ ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_1972.pdf), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(classic)), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-f-series-the-story/))
  - **other**: MY1972 only: round oil-filler flap on the RIGHT rear wing, just behind the door below the quarter window (oil tank moved ahead of the right rear wheel); deleted for MY1973 — _Visible on the green car in the 1972 brochure (PDF p.14)._ ([en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(classic)), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_1972.pdf), [classicandsportscar.com](https://www.classicandsportscar.com/features/buyers-guide-porsche-911-1964-73), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-f-series-the-story/))
- **rear_spoiler**
  - **type**: none — _The Carrera RS 2.7 was the first 911 with front and rear spoilers._ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/1.-Generation-Design-und-Modellvarianten.html))
  - **deploy**: n/a
- **engine_lid_grille**
  - **design**: one wide rectangular grille with rounded ends in the upper engine lid below the rear window, 13 horizontal bars (dark bars with bright frame/bar ends up to MY1972; all-black from MY1973); aluminium engine lid from C series (MY1970) — _bar count read from the 1972 brochure cover photo (13 bright bar ends each side)_ ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_1972.pdf), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(classic)))
- **body**
  - **fender_flares**: from the B series (MY1969) the wheel arches got a subtle flare (lip) - 'flared arches' - and the rear cut-outs were re-positioned for the rearward-moved rear wheels; body width unchanged (1,610 mm) — _Stuttcars: 'Wheelarches got a subtle flare design and the indicators were a bit bigger too'; C&SC timeline: 'Aug 1968 Rear wheels move back 2in, flared arches'; de.wikipedia: 'passte die Radausschnitte an'. Flare size not published._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-model-research/porsche-911-research/porsche-911-f-series-1st-generation-research-hub/), [classicandsportscar.com](https://www.classicandsportscar.com/features/buyers-guide-porsche-911-1964-73), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(1963)))
  - **rear_arch_width**: narrow body, overall width 1,610 mm ([excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/28), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(1963)))
  - **mirrors**: single round chrome door mirror on the driver's door, mounted forward on the door skin — _photo evidence_ ([porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250546&w=1000&h=749&crop=0&public=1&cs=bf30aed77c48fc7e), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_1972.pdf))
  - **door_handles**: chrome pull handles; from C series (MY1970) the push buttons were replaced by hidden pull levers ([en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(classic)), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250546&w=1000&h=749&crop=0&public=1&cs=bf30aed77c48fc7e))
  - **side_windows**: framed door windows with bright (chrome) frames; fixed front quarter vents not visible (Coupé) — _photo evidence_ ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_1972.pdf), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250546&w=1000&h=749&crop=0&public=1&cs=bf30aed77c48fc7e))
  - **quarter_window**: rear side (quarter) windows hinge open at their rear edge ('Hinged windows in back with burglar-proof lock (only on the Coupé)'); D-shaped quarter window with bright frame ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_1972.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-f-series-the-story/))
  - **drip_rails**: roof rain channels (drip rails) running from the A-pillars back and continuing as the 'flyline' onto the engine lid ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2019/history/porsche-klassik-911-eight-generations-dna-18914.html))
  - **roofline**: sloping fastback 'flyline' roof, 2+2 cabin, large rear window; characteristic side-window graphic ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2019/history/porsche-klassik-911-eight-generations-dna-18914.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/1.-Generation-Design-und-Modellvarianten.html))
  - **fuel_filler**: filler flap on the LEFT front wing ahead of the door — _en.wikipedia: 'fuel filler door on the left front fender'_ ([en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(classic)), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/pnr/2022/History/50-Years-911-Carrera-RS-2.7/Galerie-NEU-2/S22_2797_fine.jpg))
  - **windshield**: laminated safety-glass windscreen with bright surround ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_1972.pdf), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250546&w=1000&h=749&crop=0&public=1&cs=bf30aed77c48fc7e))
  - **chrome_trim**: chrome headlamp rings, window surrounds, door handles, horn grilles & indicator surrounds (to MY1972), 911 S chrome sill strips ('Chrome strips under the doors'); trim went progressively from chrome to matt black from 1973 ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_1972.pdf), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(classic)), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-design-50-years-anniversary-911-s-24-targa-27073.html))
  - **side_repeaters**: none: no separate repeater on the front-wing flank of the 1972 brochure car; the wrap-around front indicator lens is visible from the side — _photo evidence (right side, PDF p.14); no text source found_ ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_1972.pdf))
  - **rocker_sills**: 911 S: chrome strip along the sill under the doors (also visible on the green 1972 brochure car) ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_1972.pdf))
  - **other**: Rear wheels moved 57 mm aft relative to the SWB car WITHOUT lengthening the body ([en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(classic)), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:c1a8cb3d-ea37-495f-8e29-48262a263c3c/S18_3621_en.pdf))
- **lighting_signature**: Round chrome-ringed upright headlights + small wing-corner indicators with horn grilles at the front; separate wrap-around red/clear/amber lamp clusters at each rear corner, no connecting light strip ([porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250546&w=1000&h=749&crop=0&public=1&cs=bf30aed77c48fc7e), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_1972.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-%E2%80%93-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html))
- **distinctive**
  - Long wheelbase (2,268/2,271 mm) with unchanged body length - rear axle 57 mm further back than 1964-68 cars ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:c1a8cb3d-ea37-495f-8e29-48262a263c3c/S18_3621_en.pdf), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(classic)))
  - 911 S 2.4 (MY1972-73) chin spoiler under the front bumper ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_1972.pdf), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(classic)))
  - MY1972 one-year-only oil-filler flap on the right rear wing ([en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(classic)))
  - MY1973 black horn grilles, black indicator surrounds, black engine grille and lettering ([en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(classic)))
- **open_questions**
  - Front/rear overhang split for 1969-73 (no published figure found yet) - measure from an F-series orthographic.
  - Length conflict 4,147 vs 4,163 mm (and 4,227 mm US T/E).
  - Exact size of the MY1969 arch flare lip (not published).

### 04-carrera-rs-27-1973 — 911 Carrera RS 2.7 Coupé (MY1973, F series) - Touring (M472) / Sport 'Lightweight' (M471)

- **blueprints**: `blueprints/04-carrera-rs-27-1973/` — primary: `tbp-83845_porsche-911-carrera-rs-27-1973_4view.png` — Good: clean 4-view line drawing + vendor 4-view with dimensions.
- **production_years**: Presented 5 Oct 1972 (Paris Motor Show); built through the end of MY1973 (F-series production ended July 1973); 1,580 built (200 Sport M471, 1,308 Touring M472, 55 RSR, 17 base vehicles) — _Other counts: 1,525 (Porsche press release https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:c1a8cb3d-ea37-495f-8e29-48262a263c3c/S18_3621_en.pdf), 1,590 incl. 10 prototypes (https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(1963))._ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-50-years-911-carrera-rs-2-7-germanys-fastest-sports-car-28486.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-museum-special-exhibition-50-years-911-carrera-rs-27-29751.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/scene-passion/porsche-911-carrera-rs-2-7-chassis-number-0027-south-of-france-andre-lotterer-works-driver-30162.html), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-model-research/porsche-911-research/porsche-911-f-series-1st-generation-research-hub/))
- **reference_variant**: Carrera RS 2.7 Touring (M472) Coupé; dimensions from the factory RS brochure (Sport weight) ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-50-years-911-carrera-rs-2-7-germanys-fastest-sports-car-28486.html), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1973-rs.pdf))
- **dimensions_mm**
  - **length**: 4102 — _Factory RS brochure technical data (VW-Porsche Vertriebsgesellschaft, Ludwigsburg). de.wikipedia gives 4,163 mm (https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(1963)). Touring cars carried 911 S bumpers/overriders so may be ~4,147 like the 911 S - unverified._ ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1973-rs.pdf))
  - **width**: 1652 — _= 1,610 + 42 mm rear-wing widening (https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-50-years-911-carrera-rs-2-7-germanys-fastest-sports-car-28486.html)._ ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1973-rs.pdf), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(1963)))
  - **height**: 1320 ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1973-rs.pdf), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(1963)))
  - **wheelbase**: 2271 ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1973-rs.pdf), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(1963)))
  - **track_front**: 1372 ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1973-rs.pdf), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(1963)))
  - **track_rear**: 1394 — _With 7J rear rims. The early RS brochure prints 1,368 mm (digits partly illegible, and that brochure lists 6J wheels all round)._ ([de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(1963)))
  - **ground_clearance**: 150 ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1973-rs.pdf))
- **overhang_mm**
  - **front**: _not found_ — _Not published. Total overhang 4,102 - 2,271 = 1,831 mm. Photo-derived split (low confidence, see 03 note): front ~42-44% -> ~770-805 mm._ ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1973-rs.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/pnr/2022/History/50-Years-911-Carrera-RS-2.7/Galerie-NEU-2/S22_2797_fine.jpg))
  - **rear**: _not found_ — _Not published. Photo-derived ~1,025-1,060 mm (low confidence)._ ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1973-rs.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/pnr/2022/History/50-Years-911-Carrera-RS-2.7/Galerie-NEU-2/S22_2797_fine.jpg))
- **other_published**
  - **curb_weight_kg**: 960 — _Sport (M471) 960 kg DIN; Touring (M472) 115 kg heavier = 1,075 kg (https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-50-years-911-carrera-rs-2-7-germanys-fastest-sports-car-28486.html)._ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-50-years-911-carrera-rs-2-7-germanys-fastest-sports-car-28486.html), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1973-rs.pdf))
  - **drag_cd**: _not found_ — _Cd not published; ducktail cut rear-axle lift coefficient from 0.29 to 0.08 and raised top speed by 4.5 km/h to 240 km/h._ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-rear-ducktail-911-carrera-rs-27-christophorus-404-30154.html))
  - **windshield_rake_deg**: _not found_ — _not found_
- **wheels**
  - **tyre_front**: 185/70 VR 15 ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-50-years-911-carrera-rs-2-7-germanys-fastest-sports-car-28486.html))
  - **tyre_rear**: 215/60 VR 15 ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-50-years-911-carrera-rs-2-7-germanys-fastest-sports-car-28486.html))
  - **rim_front**: 6J x 15 ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-50-years-911-carrera-rs-2-7-germanys-fastest-sports-car-28486.html))
  - **rim_rear**: 7J x 15 — _First Porsche series car with different tyre sizes front/rear. Early brochure listed 6 x 15 forged, 165 VR 15 all round (https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1973-rs.pdf)._ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-50-years-911-carrera-rs-2-7-germanys-fastest-sports-car-28486.html))
  - **rim_diameter_in**: 15 ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-50-years-911-carrera-rs-2-7-germanys-fastest-sports-car-28486.html))
  - **design_name**: Fuchs forged alloy 5-spoke ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-50-years-911-carrera-rs-2-7-germanys-fastest-sports-car-28486.html), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(1963)))
  - **spoke_count**: 5 ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-for-dummies-ducktail-fuchs-rims-10058.html))
  - **finish**: spoke star painted to match the body or the 'Carrera' lettering colour (e.g. red or green on white cars), polished rim lip; some cars plain silver — _factory brochure shows red wheels with red script; Porsche Museum white car green/green, yellow car silver wheels with black script_ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-50-years-911-carrera-rs-2-7-germanys-fastest-sports-car-28486.html), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1973-rs.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/pnr/2022/History/50-Years-911-Carrera-RS-2.7/Galerie-NEU-2/S22_2797_fine.jpg))
  - **hub_cap_center**: crest centre cap — _photo evidence_ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/pnr/2022/History/50-Years-911-Carrera-RS-2.7/Galerie-NEU-2/S22_2797_fine.jpg))
  - **options_notes**: _not found_ — _not researched further_
- **headlights**
  - **shape**: round, upright in the front wings - unchanged from 911 F series ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/pnr/2022/History/50-Years-911-Carrera-RS-2.7/Galerie-NEU-2/S22_2787_fine.jpg), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/1.-Generation-Design-und-Modellvarianten.html))
  - **surround_ring**: chrome ring — _photo evidence_ ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1973-rs.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/pnr/2022/History/50-Years-911-Carrera-RS-2.7/Galerie-NEU-2/S22_2797_fine.jpg))
  - **mounting**: upright in wings ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-%E2%80%93-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html))
  - **lens_technology**: _not found_ — _not found (911 S of the same period: halogen)_
  - **washers**: _not found_ — _not found_
  - **other**: _not found_
- **front**
  - **bumper_type**: slim F-series bumper with black rubber strip; Touring: 'heavy' rubber strip and chrome side trim; the side stripe continues along the bumper flanks ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1973-rs.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/pnr/2022/History/50-Years-911-Carrera-RS-2.7/Galerie-NEU-2/S22_2797_fine.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-f-series-the-story/))
  - **overriders**: none at the front (factory brochure front 3/4 photo) — _photo evidence_ ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1973-rs.pdf))
  - **indicators**: in the front-wing corners as on the F-series 911 (amber/clear, wrapping round) — _photo_ ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1973-rs.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/pnr/2022/History/50-Years-911-Carrera-RS-2.7/Galerie-NEU-2/S22_2797_fine.jpg))
  - **fog_lights**: _not found_ — _not standard per sources found_
  - **horn_grilles**: F-series wing-corner horn grilles (MY1973: black plastic) ([en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(classic)), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1973-rs.pdf))
  - **air_intakes**: dedicated body-colour front spoiler under the bumper with a central opening for the oil cooler (prototypes used the 911 S spoiler) — _de.wikipedia: 'Der spezielle Frontspoiler hatte für den Ölkühler in der Mitte eine Öffnung'. Porsche: first series car with front AND rear spoilers._ ([de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(1963)), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-50-years-911-carrera-rs-2-7-germanys-fastest-sports-car-28486.html), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1973-rs.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-f-series-the-story/))
  - **front_lid_hood**: front lid in thin sheet steel (lightweight) ([de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(1963)), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-50-years-911-carrera-rs-2-7-germanys-fastest-sports-car-28486.html))
  - **badge_crest**: Porsche crest on the front lid - initially a glued-on sticker on the Sport ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-50-years-911-carrera-rs-2-7-germanys-fastest-sports-car-28486.html))
  - **other**: _not found_
- **rear**
  - **taillights**: F-series wrap-around units (red / clear reversing / amber) at each corner; red rectangular reflector on the bumper below each lamp ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/pnr/2022/History/50-Years-911-Carrera-RS-2.7/Galerie-NEU-2/S22_2790_fine.jpg), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/pnr/2022/History/50-Years-911-Carrera-RS-2.7/Galerie-NEU-2/S22_2793_fine.jpg))
  - **reflector_band**: none (painted rear panel); instead a stylised 'PORSCHE' decal in the stripe colour runs across the rear between the lamps ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/pnr/2022/History/50-Years-911-Carrera-RS-2.7/Galerie-NEU-2/S22_2793_fine.jpg), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/pnr/2022/History/50-Years-911-Carrera-RS-2.7/Galerie-NEU-2/S22_2787_fine.jpg))
  - **bumper**: slim rear bumper with rubber strip and two tall black rubber-faced, chrome-edged overriders flanking the plate (Touring; also on factory brochure car). Lightweight/Sport: one-piece glass-fibre rear bumper ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/pnr/2022/History/50-Years-911-Carrera-RS-2.7/Galerie-NEU-2/S22_2793_fine.jpg), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1973-rs.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-f-series-the-story/))
  - **exhaust**: single tailpipe below the bumper, car's left side ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/pnr/2022/History/50-Years-911-Carrera-RS-2.7/Galerie-NEU-2/S22_2793_fine.jpg), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/pnr/2022/History/50-Years-911-Carrera-RS-2.7/Galerie-NEU-2/S22_2787_fine.jpg))
  - **license_plate**: centred between the overriders ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/pnr/2022/History/50-Years-911-Carrera-RS-2.7/Galerie-NEU-2/S22_2793_fine.jpg))
  - **badging**: 'Carrera RS' script on the right-hand side of the ducktail; 'Carrera' side script designed by Harm Lagaaij ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/pnr/2022/History/50-Years-911-Carrera-RS-2.7/Galerie-NEU-2/S22_2793_fine.jpg), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-50-years-911-carrera-rs-2-7-germanys-fastest-sports-car-28486.html), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-f-series-the-story/), [thedrive.com](https://www.thedrive.com/culture/how-the-1972-porsche-911-carrera-rs-2-7-and-its-ducktail-made-history))
  - **other**: _not found_
- **rear_spoiler**
  - **type**: ducktail ('Entenbürzel') - fixed upswept lip integral with the glass-fibre engine lid; relatively narrow; material under the trailing edge removed ('we did not want the eye to perceive it as an extra element') — _Developed by Hermann Burst, Tilman Brodbeck and stylist Rolf Wiener; patent disclosure 2238704 filed 5 Aug 1972; rear lift CL 0.29 -> 0.08; Stuttcars quotes 103 kg downforce at top speed (https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-f-series-the-story/)._ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-50-years-911-carrera-rs-2-7-germanys-fastest-sports-car-28486.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-rear-ducktail-911-carrera-rs-27-christophorus-404-30154.html), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(1963)), [thedrive.com](https://www.thedrive.com/culture/how-the-1972-porsche-911-carrera-rs-2-7-and-its-ducktail-made-history))
  - **deploy**: fixed ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-rear-ducktail-911-carrera-rs-27-christophorus-404-30154.html))
- **engine_lid_grille**
  - **design**: slatted grille on the GRP engine lid ahead of the ducktail lip ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/pnr/2022/History/50-Years-911-Carrera-RS-2.7/Galerie-NEU-2/S22_2787_fine.jpg), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/pnr/2022/History/50-Years-911-Carrera-RS-2.7/Galerie-NEU-2/S22_2795_fine.jpg), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(1963)))
- **body**
  - **fender_flares**: rear wings widened by 42 mm around the wheel arches to clear the 7J rear wheels ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-50-years-911-carrera-rs-2-7-germanys-fastest-sports-car-28486.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-%E2%80%93-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html))
  - **rear_arch_width**: overall width 1,652 mm across the rear wings ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1973-rs.pdf))
  - **mirrors**: chrome door mirror on the driver's door, near the front of the door — _photo_ ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1973-rs.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/pnr/2022/History/50-Years-911-Carrera-RS-2.7/Galerie-NEU-2/S22_2797_fine.jpg), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/pnr/2022/History/50-Years-911-Carrera-RS-2.7/Galerie-NEU-2/S22_2793_fine.jpg))
  - **door_handles**: F-series chrome pull handles — _photo_ ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1973-rs.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/pnr/2022/History/50-Years-911-Carrera-RS-2.7/Galerie-NEU-2/S22_2797_fine.jpg))
  - **side_windows**: chrome-framed; thinner glass on lightweight cars ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-50-years-911-carrera-rs-2-7-germanys-fastest-sports-car-28486.html), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1973-rs.pdf), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(classic)), [classicandsportscar.com](https://www.classicandsportscar.com/features/buyers-guide-porsche-911-1964-73))
  - **quarter_window**: CONFLICT: de.wikipedia says the Sport lacked opening ('ausstellbare') rear side windows; Stuttcars says opening quarter windows were on all cars 1964-73 'including the Carrera RS' — _Visually identical when closed - irrelevant for the model._ ([de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(1963)), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-f-series-the-story/))
  - **drip_rails**: as 911 F series ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2019/history/porsche-klassik-911-eight-generations-dna-18914.html))
  - **roofline**: unchanged 911 fastback, interrupted at the tail by the ducktail ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/scene-passion/porsche-911-carrera-rs-2-7-chassis-number-0027-south-of-france-andre-lotterer-works-driver-30162.html))
  - **fuel_filler**: round flap on the left front wing just ahead of the windscreen/A-pillar — _photo_ ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1973-rs.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/pnr/2022/History/50-Years-911-Carrera-RS-2.7/Galerie-NEU-2/S22_2797_fine.jpg), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/pnr/2022/History/50-Years-911-Carrera-RS-2.7/Galerie-NEU-2/S22_2787_fine.jpg))
  - **windshield**: thin glass (weight saving), bright surround ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-50-years-911-carrera-rs-2-7-germanys-fastest-sports-car-28486.html), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1973-rs.pdf))
  - **chrome_trim**: bright window frames, door handles, mirror and headlamp rings on factory brochure and Porsche Museum cars; Touring adds chrome side trim on bumpers — _photo evidence; Sport-spec trim: 'Zierleisten aus PVC statt Gummi' (de.wikipedia)_ ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1973-rs.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/pnr/2022/History/50-Years-911-Carrera-RS-2.7/Galerie-NEU-2/S22_2797_fine.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-f-series-the-story/))
  - **side_repeaters**: none visible (factory brochure side photo) — _photo_ ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1973-rs.pdf))
  - **rocker_sills**: 'Carrera' script sits on a stripe running along the lower doors/sills between the arches; black strip below it on brochure car; chrome sill strip on Porsche Museum yellow Touring ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1973-rs.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/pnr/2022/History/50-Years-911-Carrera-RS-2.7/Galerie-NEU-2/S22_2797_fine.jpg), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-50-years-911-carrera-rs-2-7-germanys-fastest-sports-car-28486.html))
  - **other**: 'Carrera' script between the wheel arches (first time on a 911); 27 colours produced; script red/blue/green on white ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-50-years-911-carrera-rs-2-7-germanys-fastest-sports-car-28486.html))
- **lighting_signature**: identical to the 1973 911 F series (round headlights, wing-corner indicators, corner tail clusters) ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/pnr/2022/History/50-Years-911-Carrera-RS-2.7/Galerie-NEU-2/S22_2797_fine.jpg), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/pnr/2022/History/50-Years-911-Carrera-RS-2.7/Galerie-NEU-2/S22_2793_fine.jpg))
- **distinctive**
  - Ducktail rear spoiler ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-50-years-911-carrera-rs-2-7-germanys-fastest-sports-car-28486.html))
  - First production car with front AND rear spoilers ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-50-years-911-carrera-rs-2-7-germanys-fastest-sports-car-28486.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-museum-special-exhibition-50-years-911-carrera-rs-27-29751.html))
  - Flared rear wings (+42 mm) and mixed 6J/7J Fuchs ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-50-years-911-carrera-rs-2-7-germanys-fastest-sports-car-28486.html))
  - 'Carrera' side script with colour-matched wheels ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-50-years-911-carrera-rs-2-7-germanys-fastest-sports-car-28486.html))
- **open_questions**
  - Length: 4,102 (factory brochure) vs 4,163 mm (de.wikipedia); Touring likely longer (911 S-type bumpers with overriders) - unverified.
  - Rear track: 1,368 (early brochure, 6J wheels, digits blurred) vs 1,394 mm (de.wikipedia, 7J).
  - Exact ducktail profile (height/chord) not published - take from an orthographic.

### 05-g-series-1974 — 911 G series Coupé, MY1974-1977 (G, H, I, J series): 911 2.7 / 911 S 2.7 / Carrera 2.7 (-3.0) - impact bumpers

- **blueprints**: `blueprints/05-g-series-1974/` — primary: `dd_911-1974-lined-drawing_side-front-rear-inch-dims.jpg`, `tbp-27778_porsche-911-1976_side-front-rear-mm-dims.gif` — Good: dimensioned period drawings (inch and mm versions) with side/front/rear, plus side line drawing and vendor 3-view. No top view specific to 1974-77 -> use the 930/Carrera 3.2 top views (../06, ../08) with narrow-body arches.
- **production_years**: Series production from August 1973 (MY1974); narrow-body 2.7-litre 911 sold until August 1977 ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/2.-Generation-911,-(G-Serie),-1973---1989.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-%E2%80%93-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html))
- **reference_variant**: 1974-75 911 S 2.7 Coupé, narrow body (dimension drawing is the 1975 US-market 911 S) — _Base 911 differs by 5.5J steel wheels; Carrera 2.7 by flares, spoilers and black trim (see options)._ ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1974.pdf))
- **dimensions_mm**
  - **length**: 4291 — _'4,291-mm-long body - 144 mm longer than its forerunner' (Porsche)._ ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1974.pdf), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-%E2%80%93-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/32))
  - **width**: 1610 — _911 / 911 S.
