Carrera: 1,652 mm._ ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1974.pdf), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/32))
  - **height**: 1320 — _unladen (1975 brochure)_ ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1974.pdf), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf))
  - **wheelbase**: 2271 — _de.wikipedia gives 2,272 mm for some later years._ ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/32), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(1963)))
  - **track_front**: 1372 — _911 S (6J). Base 911 (5.5J): 1,360 mm (https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/30, https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(1963)). US Carrera 1975 (7J): 1,398 mm (https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf)._ ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf))
  - **track_rear**: 1354 — _911 S. Base 911: 1,342 mm. US Carrera 1975 (8J): 1,394 mm; de.wikipedia Carrera 2.7: 1,380 mm._ ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf))
  - **ground_clearance**: 180 — _1975 brochure tech data 'Ground clearance (unladen) 180 (7.0 in.)'; the same brochure's drawing labels 6.3 in (160 mm) under the floor centre; Excellence lists 150 mm._ ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf))
- **overhang_mm**
  - **front**: 932 — _Drawing dimension 36.69 in (bumper face to front-axle centre)._ ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf))
  - **rear**: 1088 — _Derived: 168.94 - 89.41 - 36.69 = 42.84 in = 1,088 mm._ ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf))
- **other_published**
  - **curb_weight_kg**: 1100 — _US-spec DIN incl. fuel. de.wikipedia (European 911 1973-75): 1,075 kg._ ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1974.pdf), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf))
  - **drag_cd**: _not found_ — _not found_
  - **windshield_rake_deg**: _not found_ — _Not published. DERIVED (low confidence): the windscreen line in the 1975 brochure side view is inclined ~33 deg above horizontal (~57 deg from vertical) - measured on the illustration, whose views are not to exact scale. Drawing also gives windscreen length 23.03 in (585 mm) along the slope, approach angle 16 deg, departure angle 17 deg, ramp-breakover radius 26.25 ft._ ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf))
- **wheels**
  - **tyre_front**: 185/70 VR 15 ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1974.pdf), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf))
  - **tyre_rear**: 185/70 VR 15 ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1974.pdf), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf))
  - **rim_front**: 6J x 15 ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1974.pdf), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf))
  - **rim_rear**: 6J x 15 ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1974.pdf), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf))
  - **rim_diameter_in**: 15 ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf))
  - **design_name**: pressure-cast light-alloy wheel - the flat-faced ATS 'cookie cutter' ('Hackmesser') design — _1974 US brochure: 911 S 6J x 15 'light alloy'; 1975 US brochure: '6J x 15 pressure cast alloy rims'; de.wikipedia and Supercar Nostalgia name the ATS wheels 'Hackmesser' / 'Cookie Cutter'. CONFLICT: Supercar Nostalgia says RoW 911 and 911 S had 5.5J x 15 steel wheels with chrome hub caps as standard in MY1974, ATS optional (steel wheels deleted for MY1975) - market-dependent._ ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(1963)), [supercarnostalgia.com](https://supercarnostalgia.com/blog/porsche-911-g-h-series-1974-1975))
  - **spoke_count**: 5 — _not a spoke wheel: flat disc whose face forms a raised five-pointed star with five openings between the points (1975 brochure cover close-up)_ ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf))
  - **finish**: silver-painted face — _photo_ ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf))
  - **hub_cap_center**: small black centre cap with Porsche crest, five exposed lug nuts — _photo (1975 brochure cover)_ ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf))
  - **options_notes**: Base 911: 5.5J x 15 steel, 165 HR 15. Carrera 1974: 6J/7J x 15 light alloy (Fuchs), 185/70 & 215/60 VR 15. US Carrera 1975: 7J/8J x 15 forged alloy (Fuchs) with black centres. ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1974.pdf), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf))
- **headlights**
  - **shape**: round, upright in the (slightly modified) rounded front wings ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-%E2%80%93-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html))
  - **surround_ring**: chrome (plated) ring on 1974 factory photos; MY1975 Carreras got body-coloured headlight surrounds as standard — _photo + Supercar Nostalgia_ ([porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250550&w=1000&h=1000&crop=0&public=1&cs=b07864ac7bd7f798), [supercarnostalgia.com](https://supercarnostalgia.com/blog/porsche-911-g-h-series-1974-1975))
  - **mounting**: upright in the wing tunnels ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-%E2%80%93-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/2.-Generation---Design-und-Modellvarianten.html))
  - **lens_technology**: _not found_ — _not found_
  - **washers**: optional headlight washer jets (nozzle below each headlamp, visible in 1975 brochure detail photo); fitted to the German 1975 anniversary model, not to US cars ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(1963)), [supercarnostalgia.com](https://supercarnostalgia.com/blog/porsche-911-g-h-series-1974-1975))
  - **other**: _not found_
- **front**
  - **bumper_type**: impact bumper: body-coloured bar with full-width black neoprene rubbing strip ('rubber lip on the nose') and matching black bellows at each end where it meets the wing; compresses up to 50 mm; US cars on hydraulic impact absorbers (optional elsewhere), RoW on collapsible steel tubes; new wings, front lid and engine lid were needed to take the bumpers ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-%E2%80%93-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-seven-generations-part-2-g-model-16459.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:a835f71f-f307-4a3e-88e4-eebb7a94d247/S18_3622_en.pdf), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(1963)), [supercarnostalgia.com](https://supercarnostalgia.com/blog/porsche-911-g-h-series-1974-1975))
  - **overriders**: none at the front on US 1975 drawing and 1974 factory photos ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250550&w=1000&h=1000&crop=0&public=1&cs=b07864ac7bd7f798))
  - **indicators**: moved from the wing corners into the bumper: amber lens in the bumper under each headlight ([de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(1963)), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250550&w=1000&h=1000&crop=0&public=1&cs=b07864ac7bd7f798))
  - **fog_lights**: rectangular fog lights set below the bumper - standard on US 1975 Carrera, optional otherwise ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf), [supercarnostalgia.com](https://supercarnostalgia.com/blog/porsche-911-g-h-series-1974-1975))
  - **horn_grilles**: no wing-corner horn grilles visible (area now covered by the bumper) — _photo evidence_ ([porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250550&w=1000&h=1000&crop=0&public=1&cs=b07864ac7bd7f798))
  - **air_intakes**: Carrera: front spoiler lip 'where regulations allowed'; 911 / 911 S plain lower apron ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-%E2%80%93-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf))
  - **front_lid_hood**: flat bonnet between higher wings, unchanged ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/2.-Generation---Design-und-Modellvarianten.html))
  - **badge_crest**: Porsche crest on front lid ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250550&w=1000&h=1000&crop=0&public=1&cs=b07864ac7bd7f798))
  - **other**: slatted cowl grille ahead of the windscreen (1975 brochure detail photo) ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf))
- **rear**
  - **taillights**: F-series wrap-around corner lamp units carried over (US cars with red rear lenses/extra side lights) ([supercarnostalgia.com](https://supercarnostalgia.com/blog/porsche-911-g-h-series-1974-1975), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250543&w=1000&h=528&crop=0&public=1&cs=b972431cd02e5e65))
  - **reflector_band**: NEW: red light/reflector strip with 'PORSCHE' lettering spanning between the tail lamps ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-%E2%80%93-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/2.-Generation---Design-und-Modellvarianten.html), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(1963)))
  - **bumper**: impact bumper with black rubbing strip and bellows ends; RoW 1974 911 S/Carrera: two small black rubber overrider blocks on the bar either side of the plate; US 1975 cars: two tall black rubber bumper guards — _photo evidence for RoW overriders (Porsche archive 1974 trio photo)_ ([porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=251486&w=1000&h=766&crop=0&public=1&cs=e2a75e90f5a898e0), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-%E2%80%93-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html))
  - **exhaust**: single tailpipe, car's left, below bumper ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf))
  - **license_plate**: centred in the rear bumper, below the reflector strip ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf))
  - **badging**: 'PORSCHE' in the reflector strip; model script on the engine lid ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-%E2%80%93-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html))
  - **other**: _not found_
- **rear_spoiler**
  - **type**: 911 / 911 S: none as standard. Carrera 2.7 MY1974: ducktail (standard in North America, optional elsewhere, not allowed in Germany by the TUV); whale-tail wing optional MY1974-75 and standard on the US 1975 Carrera, framed in black rubber, paired with a matt-black chin spoiler ([en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(classic)), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2019/history/porsche-klassik-911-eight-generations-dna-18914.html), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-%E2%80%93-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html), [supercarnostalgia.com](https://supercarnostalgia.com/blog/porsche-911-g-h-series-1974-1975))
  - **deploy**: fixed ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf))
- **engine_lid_grille**
  - **design**: black horizontally-slatted grille in the upper engine lid with a '2.7' badge on the grille; model script ('911 S') on the lid below it ([porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=251486&w=1000&h=766&crop=0&public=1&cs=e2a75e90f5a898e0), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf))
- **body**
  - **fender_flares**: 911 / 911 S: narrow rear wings (no flares). Carrera: rear wings widened by 42 mm - early 1974 Carreras with welded-on RS-style flares, later with stamped (SC-style) flares ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-%E2%80%93-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1974.pdf), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911))
  - **rear_arch_width**: 1,610 mm overall (911/911 S); Carrera 1,652 mm ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1974.pdf))
  - **mirrors**: chrome door mirror (squarer housing than F series) on the driver's door — _DNA article: chrome disappeared 'with the exception of the exterior mirror' (1975 Carrera)_ ([porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250550&w=1000&h=1000&crop=0&public=1&cs=b07864ac7bd7f798), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2019/history/porsche-klassik-911-eight-generations-dna-18914.html))
  - **door_handles**: 911/911 S: plated (chrome); Carrera: anodised matt black ('Black Look', optional on others) — _Porsche: 'less decorative chrome, matt black window frames and door handles'_ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-%E2%80%93-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1974.pdf), [supercarnostalgia.com](https://supercarnostalgia.com/blog/porsche-911-g-h-series-1974-1975), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-model-research/porsche-911-research/porsche-911-g-series-2nd-generation-research-hub/))
  - **side_windows**: window surrounds: chrome on 911 and 911 S, black on Carrera (1974 US brochure) ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1974.pdf), [supercarnostalgia.com](https://supercarnostalgia.com/blog/porsche-911-g-h-series-1974-1975))
  - **quarter_window**: opening rear quarter windows standard on Carrera, optional on 911/911 S (listed as fitted on the US 1975 911 S) ([supercarnostalgia.com](https://supercarnostalgia.com/blog/porsche-911-g-h-series-1974-1975), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf))
  - **drip_rails**: roof rain channels continuing as the 'flyline' ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2019/history/porsche-klassik-911-eight-generations-dna-18914.html))
  - **roofline**: unchanged classic 911 silhouette ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-%E2%80%93-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html))
  - **fuel_filler**: flap on left front wing (visible in drawing and photos) ([auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250543&w=1000&h=528&crop=0&public=1&cs=b972431cd02e5e65))
  - **windshield**: _not found_
  - **chrome_trim**: reduced chrome; Carrera 'Black trim' ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-%E2%80%93-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf))
  - **side_repeaters**: US cars had 'extra sidelights' vs RoW (position not stated); RoW side repeaters not documented ([supercarnostalgia.com](https://supercarnostalgia.com/blog/porsche-911-g-h-series-1974-1975))
  - **rocker_sills**: new body-coloured side sill with integrated jacking point below a black rubbing strip; 911 S: rubber door-sill mouldings; sills sometimes bright-finished on 911/911 S ([supercarnostalgia.com](https://supercarnostalgia.com/blog/porsche-911-g-h-series-1974-1975), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf))
  - **other**: From MY1976 body in hot-dip galvanised steel (not visible) ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-%E2%80%93-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html))
- **lighting_signature**: Round headlights; indicators now in the bumper; new red reflector strip visually linking the two tail-lamp clusters with 'PORSCHE' lettering ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-%E2%80%93-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html), [auto-brochures.com](https://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_1975-2.pdf))
- **distinctive**
  - Bellows impact bumpers - the G-series signature for 15-16 years ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-%E2%80%93-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2019/history/porsche-klassik-911-eight-generations-dna-18914.html))
  - Red rear reflector strip with PORSCHE lettering ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-%E2%80%93-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html))
- **open_questions**
  - Front overriders: none on 1974 factory photos or 1975 US drawing - confirm for all markets.
  - Steel vs ATS wheels as standard on RoW 911 S in MY1974 (brochure vs Supercar Nostalgia).

### 06-930-turbo-1975 — Porsche 911 Turbo (Type 930) Coupé

- **blueprints**: `blueprints/06-930-turbo-1975/` — primary: `dd_911-turbo-1986_4view-mm-dims.jpg`, `dd_911-turbo_plan01_polish-4view-sections-mm.jpg` — Excellent: two fully dimensioned 4-view drawings (side/top/front/rear) + two 4-view line drawings (3.0 wing and 3.3 wing).
- **production_years**: MY1975–MY1989. Prototype shown IAA Frankfurt Sept 1973; production version shown Paris Motor Show 3 Oct 1974; production started Feb 1975 ('launched globally in spring 1975'); 3.0 l/260 PS MY1975–77, 3.3 l/300 PS with intercooler MY1978–89; Targa/Cabriolet from 1987; discontinued June 1989 ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-turbo/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/50-years-porsche-turbo/The-beginning-of-the-series.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/50-years-porsche-turbo/The-911-Turbo-generations.html), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-turbo-3-0-930/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-turbo-3-3-930/))
- **reference_variant**: Recommended reference: MY1977 911 Turbo 3.0 Coupé (RoW spec) — original 'whale tail' on the GRP engine lid, 16-in Fuchs (standard from MY1977), body-colour electric mirror, black stone-chip foils ahead of the rear arches (from MY1977). Photographic match: white Turbo 3.0 in the Porsche German range brochure archived as 'Porsche 1976 RFN' (content is MY1977 spec: Turbo on 16-in wheels, Carrera 3.0 and 924 in range) and the 1976 US 'Turbo Carrera' brochure cover. Body length/wheelbase/widths/overhangs are identical 1975–89 (FIA hom. 645 of 1976 and FISA hom. B-208 of 1982 give the same figures). — _Stop is labelled 1975 and precedes the 1978 SC, so the whale-tail 3.0 is the chronologically faithful choice. Alternative: 1978–85 Turbo 3.3 with the tea-tray wing (see rear_spoiler)._ ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-turbo/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-turbo-3-0-930/), [archive.org](https://archive.org/details/Porsche1976RFN), [archive.org](https://archive.org/details/Porsche1976USA), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/1-137.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/02-535.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fia-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-930-3-0-1976/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fisa-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-930-3-3-1982/))
- **dimensions_mm**
  - **length**: 4291 — _FIA homologation no. 645 (valid 1.1.1976): 'Länge über alles mit Stoßfängern 4291 mm'; same in FISA B-208 (1982) and the Porsche 1989 French brochure._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/1-137.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/02-535.jpg), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/36.jpg), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/372), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fia-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-930-3-0-1976/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fisa-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-930-3-3-1982/))
  - **width**: 1775 — _Overall width measured at the rear axle (rear wings). Porsche Classic: flared rear wings made the 930 '12 cm wider' — consistent with 1775 − 1652 (Carrera/SC body, FISA B-207) = 123 mm; vs the 1974 narrow 911/911 S body (1610 mm) it is +165 mm._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/1-137.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/02-535.jpg), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/36.jpg), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/372), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fia-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-930-3-0-1976/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fisa-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-930-3-3-1982/))
  - **body_width_at_front_axle**: 1700 — _Homologation item 204a 'Karosseriebreite an der Vorderachse' = 1700 mm (vs 1610 mm for the SC/Carrera body) → front wings also flared ≈45 mm per side._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/1-137.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/02-535.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fia-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-930-3-0-1976/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fisa-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-930-3-3-1982/))
  - **body_width_at_rear_axle**: 1775 ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/1-137.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/02-535.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fia-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-930-3-0-1976/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fisa-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-930-3-3-1982/))
  - **height**: 1310 — _Porsche 1989 brochure 'Hauteur (à vide selon DIN)' 1310 mm (Turbo 3.3). Excellence: 1320 mm for 1976–77 (3.0) and 1310 mm from 1978; Wikipedia infobox 51.6 in (≈1311 mm). No Porsche primary height found for the 3.0 — use 1310–1320 mm._ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/36.jpg), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/373))
  - **wheelbase**: 2272 ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/1-137.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/02-535.jpg), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/36.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fia-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-930-3-0-1976/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fisa-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-930-3-3-1982/))
  - **track_front**: 1432 ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/36.jpg), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/371), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/372))
  - **track_rear**: 1501 — _CONFLICT: Excellence 1501 mm for every MY; Porsche 1989 brochure 1492 mm (MY1989, 9J rims). Recommend ≈1500 mm for the 8J-rim 3.0 reference car; 1492 mm for MY1986–89._ ([excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/371), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/372), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/373))
  - **ground_clearance**: 130 — _Porsche 1989 brochure, fully laden ('à pleine charge'). Excellence: 120 mm._ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/36.jpg))
- **overhang_mm**
  - **front**: 932 — _FISA homologation B-208 (911 Turbo, valid 1 Mar 1982) item 209a 'Überhang vorne' = 932 mm._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/02-535.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fisa-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-930-3-3-1982/))
  - **rear**: 1087 — _Same form item 209b = 1087 mm (932 + 2272 + 1087 = 4291)._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/02-535.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fisa-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-930-3-3-1982/))
- **other_published**
  - **curb_weight_kg**: 3.0: 1195 kg (stuttcars); 3.3 MY1989 RoW: 1335 kg DIN (Porsche 1989 brochure) ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-turbo-3-3-930/), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/36.jpg))
  - **drag_cd**: _not found_ — _not found in: Porsche Classic Turbo page, Newsroom Turbo press kit, FIA/FISA homologation pages viewed, 1976/1979/1989 brochures, Excellence (N/A)_
  - **windshield_rake_deg**: _not found_ — _not found in: homologation forms, brochures, Porsche Classic pages_
- **wheels**
  - **tyre_front**: MY1975–76: 185/70 VR15 (Porsche 1976 US Technical Data, 'Turbo Carrera'); MY1977–85: 16-in 'ultra-low-profile' tyres (Porsche German range brochure, MY1977 content: 'Ultra-Niederquerschnittsreifen auf 16-Zoll-Rädern ... Höhen-zu-Breiten-Verhältnis: 50 zu 100') — Excellence lists 205/55 VR16; MY1989: 205/55 ZR16 (Porsche 1989 brochure) — _Excellence lists 205/50 VR15 for 1976 — contradicts the Porsche 1976 US tech data (185/70 VR15); primary source preferred._ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/Porsche1976USA), [archive.org](https://archive.org/details/Porsche1976RFN), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/372), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/36.jpg))
  - **tyre_rear**: MY1975–76: 215/60 VR15 (Porsche 1976 US tech data); MY1977–85: 225/50 VR16 (Excellence); MY1986–89: 245/45 16-in (Porsche 1989 brochure: 245/45 ZR16) ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/Porsche1976USA), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/372), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/36.jpg))
  - **rim_front**: 7J x 15 forged alloy (MY1975–76); 7J x 16 (MY1977–89) — _Excellence lists 8J x 16 front for 1986–89 — contradicted by the Porsche 1989 brochure (7J x 16)._ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/Porsche1976USA), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-turbo-3-0-930/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-turbo-3-3-930/), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/36.jpg))
  - **rim_rear**: 8J x 15 forged alloy (MY1975–76); 8J x 16 (MY1977–85); 9J x 16 (MY1986–89) ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/Porsche1976USA), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-turbo-3-3-930/), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/36.jpg))
  - **rim_diameter_in**: 15 (MY1975–76; 16 optional from MY1976); 16 standard from MY1977 ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/Porsche1976USA), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-turbo-3-0-930/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/50-years-porsche-turbo/The-911-Turbo-generations.html), [archive.org](https://archive.org/details/Porsche1976RFN))
  - **design_name**: Fuchs forged light-alloy 5-spoke ('clover leaf' design by Heinrich Klie) ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/Porsche1976USA), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-turbo-3-0-930/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-for-dummies-ducktail-fuchs-rims-10058.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2020/history/porsche-911-turbo-generations-walter-roehrl-23139.html))
  - **spoke_count**: 5 ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-for-dummies-ducktail-fuchs-rims-10058.html))
  - **finish**: Black-painted spoke star with polished/bright rim lip (visual: 1976 US cover, white Turbo in the MY1977-content German brochure, 1989 brochure side photo). Porsche: Fuchs spokes initially bright on a black background, later the whole rim star black. — _Finish is from photos; individual cars vary._ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-for-dummies-ducktail-fuchs-rims-10058.html), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/Porsche1976USA/porsche%201976%20USA.zip), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/Porsche1976RFN/porsche%201976%20RFN.cbz), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/05.jpg))
  - **hub_cap_center**: Small centre cap with Porsche crest (visual, 1989 brochure) ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/05.jpg))
  - **options_notes**: 16-in optional MY1976, standard MY1977; 9-in rear rims from MY1986 ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-turbo-3-0-930/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-turbo-3-3-930/))
- **headlights**
  - **shape**: Round headlamps standing upright in the tops of the front wings ('tunnel-shaped wings and integrated headlights') ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/2.-Generation---Design-und-Modellvarianten.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-–-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html))
  - **surround_ring**: Headlight rings in vehicle colour (all Turbo MYs) — _US cars: 'thicker body coloured headlight shrouds' (stuttcars)._ ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-turbo/))
  - **mounting**: Upright in front wing, classic 911 layout ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-–-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html))
  - **lens_technology**: Clear glass lens with fluted/ribbed optic pattern (visual, 1989 brochure front photo); lamp type not stated in sources ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/29.jpg))
  - **washers**: Headlight washing system standard ('a headlight washing system', 1976 US; 'Headlight and windshield washers ... all standard', 1979 US) ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/Porsche1976USA), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260421_20260421_1846/115.png), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-turbo-3-0-930/))
  - **other**: Slantnose/Flachbau (Sonderwunsch from 1981/83, option M505/506) replaced wing-top lamps with flat wings and 944 pop-up headlamps — not the reference car ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/50-years-porsche-turbo/The-911-Turbo-generations.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-–-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-turbo-3-3-930/), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/31.jpg))
- **front**
  - **bumper_type**: G-series aluminium impact bumper (homologation: front bumper aluminium, 6.6 kg) with black plastic bellows at the ends and a rubber strip; absorbs 5 mph/8 km/h impacts (hydraulic absorbers US, deformation tubes RoW; compresses up to 50 mm) ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/2-160.jpg), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-turbo/), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-911-s/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-–-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-seven-generations-part-2-g-model-16459.html), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fia-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-930-3-0-1976/))
  - **overriders**: None at the front (rubber buffers only at the rear, flanking the plate) — _US cars of this era show small bumper guards in the 1976 US photos — visual only._ ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-911-s/), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/Porsche1976USA/porsche%201976%20USA.zip))
  - **indicators**: Amber indicators set into the outer ends of the front bumper (visual: 1976 US line-up photo, 1979 US bumper close-up, 1989 brochure front photo) — _US cars: amber running lights (stuttcars)._ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/Porsche1976USA), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260421_20260421_1846/115.png), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/29.jpg))
  - **fog_lights**: Standard. Two rectangular white fog lamps in the front spoiler/apron below the bumper, either side of the plate (visual: 1976 US line-up, 1989 brochure front photo) ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/Porsche1976USA), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260421_20260421_1846/115.png), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-turbo-3-0-930/), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/29.jpg))
  - **horn_grilles**: No separate horn grilles visible on the G-series bumper face in the brochure front photos (only amber indicator lenses and the black rubber strip) — _Visual absence only; no text source._ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/Porsche1976USA), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/29.jpg))
  - **air_intakes**: Deep front spoiler lip under the bumper ('The deep spoiler below the nose helps reduce air drag, and also minimizes lift' — 1979 US brochure; 'front and rear spoilers' standard — 1976 US brochure) — _330 PS kit / Turbo S (1983+): remodelled front spoiler with extra oil cooler (stuttcars)._ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260421_20260421_1846/115.png), [archive.org](https://archive.org/details/Porsche1976USA))
  - **front_lid_hood**: Flat steel front luggage lid between the raised wings (homologation: 'Kofferhaube' steel) ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/2.-Generation---Design-und-Modellvarianten.html), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/2-160.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fia-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-930-3-0-1976/))
  - **badge_crest**: Porsche crest on the front lid (visual) ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260421_20260421_1846/115.png), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/02.jpg))
  - **other**: _not found_ — _not found in: —_
- **rear**
  - **taillights**: Separate taillight units in each rear corner, joined by the full-width red light strip carrying PORSCHE lettering (logo black; from MY1987 red reflective logo with integrated rear fog lights per Porsche Classic Targa/Cab list and stuttcars) ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-turbo/), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-911-s/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-–-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-turbo-3-3-930/), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260421_20260421_1846/114.png))
  - **reflector_band**: Full-width red panel between the tail lights with PORSCHE lettering ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-911-s/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-–-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260421_20260421_1846/114.png))
  - **bumper**: Aluminium impact bumper (7.0 kg per homologation) with black bellows; plate flanked by two large black rubber buffers with integrated plate lighting ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/2-160.jpg), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-turbo/), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-911-s/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fia-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-930-3-0-1976/))
  - **exhaust**: Exits under the rear bumper on the LEFT side. Visual: a 1976 US Turbo Carrera shows a single large visible outlet at the left (Commons photo); the MY1989 car in the Porsche 1989 brochure shows two pipes side by side at the left. Porsche Classic: 'twin tailpipes. Exhaust gases only came through the left-hand pipe when the boost-pressure control valve was open'; stuttcars: MY1980 A-series got 'dual instead of single exhausts'. For the MY1977 reference model one visible left outlet (possibly with a small secondary pipe). — _Conflict between Porsche Classic ('twin tailpipes' from launch) and stuttcars (dual from MY1980); photos support single visible outlet on 1976 and twin on 1989._ ([commons.wikimedia.org](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:1976_Porsche_930_Turbo,_Emerald_Green_met,_rear_left.jpg), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/05.jpg), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-turbo/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-turbo-3-3-930/))
  - **license_plate**: Centred in the rear bumper between the two rubber buffers ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-911-s/))
  - **badging**: Script on the engine lid below the wing: 'turbo' (RoW; 1979 US brochure close-up) / 'turbo Carrera' (1976 US car, Commons photo) ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260421_20260421_1846/115.png), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260421_20260421_1846/114.png), [commons.wikimedia.org](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:1976_Porsche_930_Turbo,_Emerald_Green_met,_rear_left.jpg))
  - **other**: MY1987+: fog and reversing lights moved into the rear reflector strip (previously below the bumper) ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-turbo-3-3-930/), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-turbo/))
- **rear_spoiler**
  - **type**: MY1975–77 'whale tail': large fixed wing formed on the fibreglass-reinforced plastic engine lid, framed by a wide black hard-rubber border, with a rectangular air-intake grille in its top surface (visual, MY1977-content German brochure). MY1978–89 'tea tray': larger, flatter wing raised to house the intercooler beneath, with upturned side fences and vents, thicker black surround — _Whale tail visual (1976 US car, MY1977-content German brochure): upper surface in body colour with a black slatted intake grille inset, thick black rubber rim wrapping the trailing edge and sides._ ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-turbo/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-–-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-turbo-3-0-930/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-turbo-3-3-930/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/50-years-porsche-turbo/The-911-Turbo-generations.html), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(930)), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/Porsche1976RFN/porsche%201976%20RFN.cbz), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260421_20260421_1846/115.png), [commons.wikimedia.org](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:1976_Porsche_930_Turbo,_Emerald_Green_met,_rear_left.jpg))
  - **deploy**: Fixed (non-deploying) ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-turbo-3-0-930/), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-turbo/))
- **engine_lid_grille**
  - **design**: Intake grille integrated into the wing's upper surface (black slatted grille inside the rubber-edged wing); engine cooling grille enlarged for MY1976. Lid material: GRP up to MY1977 (Porsche Classic; FIA hom. 645 'Motorhaube: Kunststoff'); Porsche Classic says steel lid + plastic wing from MY1978, but FISA hom. B-208 (1982) still lists the rear lid as plastic ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-turbo-3-0-930/), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260421_20260421_1846/115.png), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/Porsche1976RFN/porsche%201976%20RFN.cbz), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-turbo/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/2-160.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/10-181.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fia-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-930-3-0-1976/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fisa-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-930-3-3-1982/))
- **body**
  - **fender_flares**: Strongly flared rear wings (+12 cm total width) and flared front wings (body 1700 mm wide at front axle vs 1610 mm on the narrow-front SC/Carrera) ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-turbo/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-–-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/1-137.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fia-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-930-3-0-1976/))
  - **rear_arch_width**: 1775 mm at rear axle (Turbo) vs 1652 mm Carrera/SC and 1610 mm 1974 911/911 S ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/1-137.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/02-489.jpg), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/30), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fia-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-930-3-0-1976/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fisa-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-911-sc-1982/))
  - **mirrors**: Door-mounted flag-type exterior mirror in vehicle colour; electrically adjustable from MY1976; passenger mirror standard from MY1988 ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-turbo/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-turbo-3-0-930/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-turbo-3-3-930/))
  - **door_handles**: Anodised-black door handles ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-turbo/))
  - **side_windows**: Anodised-black window surrounds ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-turbo/))
  - **quarter_window**: Fixed rear quarter window (safety glass) with the classic curved 911 shape ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/2-160.jpg), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/Porsche1976USA/porsche%201976%20USA.zip), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fia-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-930-3-0-1976/))
  - **drip_rails**: Roof drip rail/gutter line visible above the side windows (visual, 1976 US cover and 1989 brochure side photos) — _Visual only._ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/Porsche1976USA/porsche%201976%20USA.zip), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/05.jpg))
  - **roofline**: Classic 911 fastback; height 1310 mm (3.3) ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-–-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/36.jpg))
  - **fuel_filler**: Filler flap on the left front wing just ahead of the windscreen/A-pillar (visual: white Turbo in the MY1977-content German brochure; 1989 brochure side photo) — _Visual only; no text source._ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/Porsche1976RFN/porsche%201976%20RFN.cbz), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/05.jpg))
  - **windshield**: Laminated glass; radio antenna integrated in the windscreen from MY1985 ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/2-160.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-turbo-3-3-930/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fia-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-930-3-0-1976/))
  - **chrome_trim**: None — 'black anodized exterior trim, wide wheel flares, and bold front and rear spoilers' ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260421_20260421_1846/115.png), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-turbo/))
  - **side_repeaters**: Not visible on 1976–77 brochure cars; small amber repeater on the front wing behind the wheel arch on the 1989 brochure car — _Visual only; introduction year not found._ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/Porsche1976USA/porsche%201976%20USA.zip), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/Porsche1976RFN/porsche%201976%20RFN.cbz), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/05.jpg))
  - **rocker_sills**: Black trim strip along the sill (visual); Turbo S/power-kit cars had modified rockers/side skirts ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/Porsche1976RFN/porsche%201976%20RFN.cbz), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/05.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-turbo-3-3-930/))
  - **other**: Black stone-chip protection foils in front of the rear wheel arches (from MY1977; absent on the MY1976 Commons car, present on the MY1977-content brochure car); optional 'Turbo' side-script decals from MY1976 ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-turbo/), [commons.wikimedia.org](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:1976_Porsche_930_Turbo,_Emerald_Green_met,_rear_left.jpg), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/Porsche1976RFN/porsche%201976%20RFN.cbz), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-turbo-3-0-930/))
- **lighting_signature**: Round upright wing-top headlamps with body-colour rings; amber indicators in the bumper ends; two white rectangular fog lamps in the front spoiler; red full-width rear strip with PORSCHE lettering between the corner tail lamps ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-turbo/), [archive.org](https://archive.org/details/Porsche1976USA), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/05.jpg))
- **distinctive**
  - Whale-tail (1975–77) / tea-tray (1978–89) rear wing with black rubber edge ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-turbo/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-turbo-3-3-930/))
  - Widest G-series body: 1700 mm front / 1775 mm rear; tracks 1432 / ~1500 mm ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/1-137.jpg), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/36.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fia-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-930-3-0-1976/))
  - Black stone-chip foils ahead of the rear arches ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-turbo/))
  - Exhaust exits under the left rear (single visible outlet on 1976 photo; twin pipes on 1989 brochure car) ([commons.wikimedia.org](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:1976_Porsche_930_Turbo,_Emerald_Green_met,_rear_left.jpg), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/05.jpg), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-turbo/))
- **open_questions**
  - Porsche-primary height for the 3.0 (1310 vs 1320 mm).
  - Rear track 1501 (Excellence) vs 1492 mm (Porsche 1989, 9J rims).
  - Horn grille placement; side-repeater introduction year.
  - MY1977–85 16-in tyre sizes from a Porsche primary source (Excellence: 205/55 VR16 & 225/50 VR16).
  - Rear-lid material after MY1978 (Porsche Classic steel vs FISA B-208 plastic).
  - Exhaust: single (1975–79) vs twin (1980+) visible outlets — photos suggest the stuttcars chronology; Porsche Classic says twin from launch.
- **model_year_changes**
  - MY1975: launch — whale tail on GRP lid, 7J/8J x 15 Fuchs, fog lamps, headlamp washers, front+rear spoilers standard ([archive.org](https://archive.org/details/Porsche1976USA), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-turbo/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-turbo-3-0-930/))
  - MY1976: electrically adjustable body-colour mirrors; engine cooling grille enlarged; 16-in wheels optional; US 'Turbo Carrera' with amber running lights/red rear indicators and thicker headlight shrouds ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-turbo/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-turbo-3-0-930/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-turbo-3-3-930/))
  - MY1977: 16-in Fuchs standard; stone-chip foils ahead of rear arches; boost gauge ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-turbo-3-0-930/), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-turbo/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/50-years-porsche-turbo/The-911-Turbo-generations.html))
  - MY1978: 3.3 l + intercooler under a larger, flatter 'tea tray' wing with upturned side fences; 917-derived brakes ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-turbo/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-turbo-3-3-930/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/50-years-porsche-turbo/The-911-Turbo-generations.html), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(930)))
  - MY1980–85: withdrawn from US/Japan; MY1980 exhaust change claimed by stuttcars ('dual instead of single') ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-turbo-3-3-930/), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(classic)))
  - 1981/1983+: Flachbau/Slantnose Sonderwunsch (flat wings, 944 pop-up lamps); 1983+ 330 PS kit: 4-pipe exhaust, remodelled front spoiler with extra oil cooler, extra vents in rear wings, modified rockers ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/50-years-porsche-turbo/The-911-Turbo-generations.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-–-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-turbo-3-3-930/))
  - MY1985: radio antenna in windscreen ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-turbo-3-3-930/))
  - MY1986: rear rims 9J x 16; back in the US ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-turbo-3-3-930/))
  - MY1987: Targa and Cabriolet; rear strip incorporates fog and reverse lamps; Turbo S / M505 flatnose package ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-turbo-3-3-930/), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-turbo/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/50-years-porsche-turbo/The-911-Turbo-generations.html))
  - MY1988: passenger mirror standard; MY1989: 5-speed G50 ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-turbo-3-3-930/), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-turbo/))

### 07-911-sc-1978 — Porsche 911 SC Coupé

- **blueprints**: `blueprints/07-911-sc-1978/` — primary: `tbp-25361_porsche-911sc-1979_3view.gif`, `vec-30946_porsche-911sc_4view-dims-watermarked.jpg` — Good: 3-view line drawing + vendor 4-view with dims + Autocar-type top/side.
- **production_years**: MY1978–MY1983 (introduced Sept 1977; replaced 911 and Carrera; replaced by the 911 Carrera 3.2 from MY1984). Outputs 180 PS (MY1978–79), 188 PS (MY1980), 204 PS (MY1981–83) RoW ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-sc/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-–-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/2.-Generation---Design-und-Modellvarianten.html), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-carrera-3-2-1984-1989/))
- **reference_variant**: Recommended reference: MY1978 911 SC Coupé (RoW) in standard spec — chrome window surrounds, door handles and headlight rings (MY1978), 15-in ATS cast wheels, no spoilers, no front-wing side repeaters. Dimensions from the Porsche 1981 US Technical Data sheet and FISA homologation B-207 (1982), identical body 1978–83. — _Chrome trim makes the SC read differently from the black-trimmed Turbo (06) and Carrera 3.2 (08). If a 1980–83 look is preferred: black trim, body-colour headlight rings, side repeaters (1981+), optional Turbo-style spoilers (1981+)._ ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-sc/), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/132.png), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/02-489.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fisa-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-911-sc-1982/))
- **dimensions_mm**
  - **length**: 4291 — _Porsche 1981 US tech data 168.9 in (4291 mm); FISA B-207 202 = 4291 mm._ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/132.png), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/02-489.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fisa-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-911-sc-1982/))
  - **width**: 1652 — _Measured above the rear axle (FISA B-207 item 203). Excellence: 1651 mm._ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/132.png), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/02-489.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fisa-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-911-sc-1982/))
  - **body_width_at_front_axle**: 1610 — _FISA B-207 item 204a._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/02-489.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fisa-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-911-sc-1982/))
  - **body_width_at_rear_axle**: 1652 — _FISA B-207 item 204b. Consistency: 1610 + 42 mm (Newsroom: Carrera/SC rear wings widened by a total of 42 mm) = 1652 mm._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/02-489.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fisa-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-911-sc-1982/))
  - **height**: 1320 — _Porsche 1981 'Height (unladen)' 51.6 in (1320 mm)._ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/132.png), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/44))
  - **wheelbase**: 2272 ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/132.png), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/02-489.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fisa-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-911-sc-1982/))
  - **track_front**: 1369 — _Porsche 1981: 53.9 in (1369 mm). Excellence: 1361 mm MY1978–79 / 1369 mm MY1980–83 (6J rims) — for a MY1978 car the smaller value is possible but not Porsche-confirmed._ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/132.png), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/48))
  - **track_rear**: 1379 — _Porsche 1981: 54.3 in (1379 mm). Excellence: 1367 mm MY1978–79 / 1379 mm MY1980–83._ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/132.png), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/48))
  - **ground_clearance**: 120 — _Porsche 1981 'Ground clearance (laden)' 4.7 in (120 mm)._ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/132.png))
- **overhang_mm**
  - **front**: 932 — _FISA B-207 item 209a = 932 mm; Porsche 1981 dimension drawing 36.6 in (≈930 mm)._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/02-489.jpg), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/133.png), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fisa-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-911-sc-1982/))
  - **rear**: 1087 — _FISA B-207 item 209b = 1087 mm; 1981 drawing 42.8 in (≈1087 mm). Drawing also marks approach 16°, departure 17°._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/02-489.jpg), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/133.png), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fisa-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-911-sc-1982/))
- **other_published**
  - **curb_weight_kg**: 1250 — _US 1981 'Unladen weight (DIN standard incl. fuel)' 2756 lb (1250 kg); Excellence 1978: 2557 lb._ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/132.png))
  - **drag_cd**: 0.4 — _Excellence only; no Porsche primary source found._ ([excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/44))
  - **windshield_rake_deg**: _not found_ — _not found in: 1981 Porsche tech data/drawing, FISA B-207 pages viewed_
- **wheels**
  - **tyre_front**: 185/70 VR15 ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/132.png), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/44))
  - **tyre_rear**: 215/60 VR15 ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/132.png), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/44))
  - **rim_front**: 6J x 15 ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/132.png))
  - **rim_rear**: 7J x 15 ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/132.png))
  - **rim_diameter_in**: 15 standard (FISA B-207: 15 in/381 mm front & rear); forged 16-in optional ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/132.png), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/08-189.jpg), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/106.png), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fisa-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-911-sc-1982/))
  - **design_name**: Standard: ATS 15-in cast ('pressure-cast') aluminium wheel — flat disc with a raised 5-point star centre and 5 petal-shaped openings (the design enthusiasts call 'cookie cutter'; that nickname is not used in the fetched Porsche sources). Optional: forged Fuchs 5-spoke 15/16 in — _Opening count from brochure close-ups (visual)._ ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-sc/), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/132.png), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/106.png), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260421_20260421_1846/113.png), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/119.png), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-klassik-911-sc-weissach-edition-merlin-assfalg-28981.html))
  - **spoke_count**: 5 (star points / openings, visual) — _Visual count._ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/106.png), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260421_20260421_1846/113.png))
  - **finish**: Silver-painted cast alloy with polished rim edge (visual); MY1980 US Weissach Edition: platinum-coloured 15-in Fuchs ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/106.png), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-klassik-911-sc-weissach-edition-merlin-assfalg-28981.html))
  - **hub_cap_center**: Centre cap with Porsche crest (visual) ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/106.png))
  - **options_notes**: 'Optional forged light alloy wheels available for the 911SC have a larger 16-inch diameter, and carry the ultra-low profile radial tires originally introduced on the Turbo Carrera' (1981 US brochure). UK Sport Group package: black Fuchs (Wikipedia). ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/106.png), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(classic)))
- **headlights**
  - **shape**: Round, upright in the front wings ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-–-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/106.png))
  - **surround_ring**: Chrome rings on MY1978 (and seen on a 1979 car in Porsche Newsroom); vehicle-colour rings from MY1980 — _Porsche Classic SC page is internally inconsistent (text: body colour from MY1979; list: 'Chrome-plated headlight rings; anodized Black from MY 1979'; 1980 list: vehicle colour)._ ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-sc/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2019/history/porsche-klassik-1979s-911-sc-cashmere-beige-16205.html))
  - **mounting**: Upright in front wing ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-–-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html))
  - **lens_technology**: Halogen headlamps standard (US 1981); 'For 1981, powerful quartz halogen headlamps have been incorporated in the 911SC' — _US-market statements._ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/119.png), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/106.png))
  - **washers**: Optional headlamp washers (jet nozzle on the bumper top in front of each lamp — brochure close-up) ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/119.png), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260421_20260421_1846/113.png), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/116.png))
  - **other**: _not found_ — _not found in: —_
- **front**
  - **bumper_type**: Aluminium impact bumper with black bellows and rubber strip; hydraulic energy absorbers (US) — 'Made of light yet rigid aluminum, the bumpers feature protective rubber moldings and bumperettes at the rear' ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-sc/), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/106.png))
  - **overriders**: None at front; rear 'bumperettes' (rubber buffers) only ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/106.png))
  - **indicators**: Amber indicators in the front bumper ends (visual) ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/106.png), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/116.png))
  - **fog_lights**: Optional ('Fog lights, white'); when fitted, two rectangular lamps mounted below the bumper either side of the plate (visual: red SC Targa, 1981 US brochure) ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/119.png), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/106.png))
  - **horn_grilles**: No separate horn grilles visible on the bumper face in brochure front photos — _Visual absence only._ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/116.png), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/106.png))
  - **air_intakes**: No spoiler as standard; 'Front and rear spoiler' optional (1981 US brochure); Porsche Classic: Turbo 3.0 front/rear spoilers optional MY1981–83 ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/119.png), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-sc/))
  - **front_lid_hood**: Flat front lid with crest (visual) ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/116.png))
  - **badge_crest**: Porsche crest on front lid (visual) ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/116.png))
  - **other**: _not found_ — _not found in: —_
- **rear**
  - **taillights**: Separate corner taillight units joined by the red rear light strip with black PORSCHE logo ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-sc/), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/106.png))
  - **reflector_band**: Full-width red strip with black Porsche logo (MY1978–83) ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-sc/))
  - **bumper**: Aluminium impact bumper with black bellows and two rubber buffers ('bumperettes') flanking the plate ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-sc/), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/106.png), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-911-s/))
  - **exhaust**: Single round tailpipe exiting below the rear bumper on the LEFT side, pointing rearward (visual: 1978 SC, Commons photo) — _Visual only (US car)._ ([commons.wikimedia.org](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Porsche_911_1978_SC_LSideRear_FOSSP_7April2013_(14563945076).jpg))
  - **license_plate**: Centred in the rear bumper between the rubber buffers ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-911-s/), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/106.png))
  - **badging**: Chrome '911 SC' script centred on the engine lid below the grille (visual: 1978 SC Commons photo; 1981 US brochure rear photos) — _Visual only._ ([commons.wikimedia.org](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Porsche_911_1978_SC_LSideRear_FOSSP_7April2013_(14563945076).jpg), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/106.png))
  - **other**: _not found_ — _not found in: —_
- **rear_spoiler**
  - **type**: None as standard (plain engine lid). Options: Turbo 3.0-style front + rear spoiler (Porsche Classic MY1981–83; 1981 US brochure option list); Newsroom: optional rear spoiler for the 911 from MY1983. MY1980 US Weissach Edition (M439) had the whale tail and front chin spoiler; UK Sport Group package added the rear spoiler + front rubber lip ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-sc/), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/119.png), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-–-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-klassik-911-sc-weissach-edition-merlin-assfalg-28981.html), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(classic)))
  - **deploy**: Fixed when fitted ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-sc/))
- **engine_lid_grille**
  - **design**: Black horizontal-louvre ventilation grille at the top of the engine lid, just below the rear window (visual, 1981 US brochure) — _Visual only; Porsche Classic lists a black ventilation grille for the 1974–75 911 lid._ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/106.png))
- **body**
  - **fender_flares**: Carrera-width rear wings — 'The 911 SC had the same wider bodyshell as the 911 Carrera'; 'With the introduction of the 911 SC in 1977, Porsche widened the body at the rear for all models' ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-sc/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/2.-Generation---Karosserievarianten.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-–-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html))
  - **rear_arch_width**: 1652 mm at rear axle vs 1610 mm at front axle (rear wings +42 mm total vs the narrow body) ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/02-489.jpg), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-–-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fisa-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-911-sc-1982/))
  - **mirrors**: Door-mounted, electrically adjustable ('Remote-controlled outside rear-view mirror with electric defroster'), painted in vehicle colour ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-sc/), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/119.png))
  - **door_handles**: Chrome-plated on MY1978; anodised black from MY1979 ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-sc/), [commons.wikimedia.org](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Porsche_911_1978_SC_LSideRear_FOSSP_7April2013_(14563945076).jpg))
  - **side_windows**: Chrome-plated window surrounds on MY1978 (visible on the red SC in the 1979 US brochure); anodised black from MY1979 ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-sc/), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260421_20260421_1846/113.png), [commons.wikimedia.org](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Porsche_911_1978_SC_LSideRear_FOSSP_7April2013_(14563945076).jpg))
  - **quarter_window**: Classic fixed quarter window ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/133.png), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260421_20260421_1846/113.png))
  - **drip_rails**: Present (visual: 1981 drawing and brochure photos) — _Visual only._ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/133.png), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260421_20260421_1846/113.png))
  - **roofline**: Classic 911 fastback, height 1320 mm ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/132.png))
  - **fuel_filler**: Flap on the left front wing ahead of the windscreen (visual, 1979 US brochure side photo) — _Visual only._ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260421_20260421_1846/113.png))
  - **windshield**: Laminated (Verbundglas) windscreen per FISA B-207 materials page ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fisa-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-911-sc-1982/))
  - **chrome_trim**: MY1978: chrome window surrounds, door handles, headlight rings; black from MY1979 ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-sc/))
  - **side_repeaters**: Side indicator lights on the front wings from MY1981 ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-sc/))
  - **rocker_sills**: Black trim strip along the sills (visual, 1981 US brochure photos) — _Visual only._ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/106.png))
  - **other**: Electric sliding sunroof optional (Coupé); luggage rack optional ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/119.png))
- **lighting_signature**: Round wing-top headlamps (chrome rings on 1978), amber bumper indicators, optional white fog lamps under the bumper, red rear strip with black PORSCHE logo ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-sc/), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/106.png), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/119.png))
- **distinctive**
  - Carrera-width rear wings with no Turbo flares and (standard) no spoilers ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-sc/))
  - 15-in ATS cast alloy wheels as standard ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-sc/), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/132.png))
  - Chrome brightwork on MY1978 cars ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-sc/))
- **open_questions**
  - Horn grilles
  - Engine-lid script wording
  - Track 1361/1367 (Excellence, MY1978–79) vs 1369/1379 mm (Porsche 1981)
- **model_year_changes**
  - MY1978: chrome window surrounds, door handles and headlight rings (last chrome G-series) ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-sc/))
  - MY1979: window surrounds and door handles anodised black ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-sc/))
  - MY1980: headlight rings in vehicle colour; 188 PS; US Weissach Edition (M439) with whale tail + chin spoiler; Excellence shows tracks 1369/1379 mm from MY1980 (vs 1361/1367) with unchanged 6J/7J x 15 rims ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-sc/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-–-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-klassik-911-sc-weissach-edition-merlin-assfalg-28981.html), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(classic)), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/46), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/48))
  - MY1981: side indicator lights on the front wings; 204 PS (RoW); Turbo 3.0 front & rear spoilers optional; quartz-halogen headlamps (US) ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-sc/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-–-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/106.png))
  - MY1983: Cabriolet added; Newsroom: optional rear spoiler for the 911 from MY1983 ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-sc/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-–-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html))
  - No evidence found of standard wider rims or wider flares during the SC run (6J/7J x 15 and 1610/1652 mm body throughout) ([excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/44), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/54), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260509_20260509_1504/132.png), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/02-489.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fisa-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-911-sc-1982/))

### 08-carrera-32-1984 — Porsche 911 Carrera 3.2 Coupé

- **blueprints**: `blueprints/08-carrera-32-1984/` — primary: `vec-1941_porsche-911-carrera-1985_4view-dims-watermarked.jpg` — Vendor 4-view with dims is the best; plus small 4-view line drawing, Targa colour 3-view and package drawing.
- **production_years**: MY1984–MY1989 (production from Sept 1983; G-series production ended July 1989). 231 PS RoW, 207 PS US (217 PS catalyst from MY1987); G50 5-speed + hydraulic clutch from MY1987 ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-carrera/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/2.-Generation---Design-und-Modellvarianten.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-–-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-carrera-3-2-1984-1989/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2021/history/porsche-klassik-911-carrera-3-2-granite-green-christian-geistdoerfer-18920.html))
- **reference_variant**: Recommended reference: MY1987–88 911 Carrera 3.2 Coupé (RoW) — G50 year, 15-in Fuchs, rear strip with red reflective logo and integrated fog lamps, no spoilers.
