For 16-in wheels use MY1989 (numbers below are from the Porsche 1989 French brochure tech data and FIA/FISA homologation B-282 of 1986). ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-carrera/), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/36.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/02-514.jpg), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2021/history/porsche-klassik-911-carrera-3-2-granite-green-christian-geistdoerfer-18920.html), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fia-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-911-carrera-3-2-1986/))
- **dimensions_mm**
  - **length**: 4291 ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/36.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/02-514.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fia-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-911-carrera-3-2-1986/))
  - **width**: 1652 — _Measured above the rear axle (B-282 item 203). Excellence: 1650 mm._ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/36.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/02-514.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fia-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-911-carrera-3-2-1986/))
  - **body_width_at_front_axle**: 1610 ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/02-514.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fia-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-911-carrera-3-2-1986/))
  - **body_width_at_rear_axle**: 1652 ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/02-514.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fia-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-911-carrera-3-2-1986/))
  - **height**: 1320 — _'Hauteur (à vide selon DIN)' 1320 mm._ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/36.jpg))
  - **wheelbase**: 2272 ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/36.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/02-514.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fia-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-911-carrera-3-2-1986/))
  - **track_front**: 1372 — _CONFLICT: Porsche 1989 brochure 1372 mm (6J x 16); Excellence 1372 mm MY1984–86 but 1398 mm MY1987–89. Porsche primary preferred._ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/36.jpg), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/385))
  - **track_rear**: 1405 — _Porsche 1989: 1405 mm (8J x 16). Excellence: 1380 mm MY1984–86, 1405 mm MY1987–89. For a 15-in (7J) car ≈1380 mm._ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/36.jpg), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/388))
  - **ground_clearance**: 130 — _Fully laden. Excellence: 120 mm._ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/36.jpg))
- **overhang_mm**
  - **front**: 932 — _FIA/FISA homologation B-282 (911 Carrera, valid 1 June 1986) item 209a._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/02-514.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fia-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-911-carrera-3-2-1986/))
  - **rear**: 1087 — _Item 209b._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/02-514.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fia-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-911-carrera-3-2-1986/))
- **other_published**
  - **curb_weight_kg**: 1210 — _DIN unladen, MY1989 RoW._ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/36.jpg))
  - **drag_cd**: _not found_ — _not found in: Porsche 1989 brochure tech data; homologation B-282; Excellence (N/A)_
  - **windshield_rake_deg**: _not found_ — _not found in: Porsche 1989 brochure; homologation B-282_
- **wheels**
  - **tyre_front**: MY1984–86: 185/70 VR15; MY1987–88: 195/65 VR15; MY1989: 205/55 ZR16 ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-carrera-3-2-1984-1989/), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/385), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/388), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/36.jpg))
  - **tyre_rear**: MY1984–88: 215/60 VR15; MY1989: 225/50 ZR16 ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-carrera-3-2-1984-1989/), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/385), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/36.jpg))
  - **rim_front**: 6J x 15 (MY1984–88); 6J x 16 (MY1989) — _Excellence lists 7J/8J x 15 with 16-in tyres for MY1988 — internally inconsistent; not used._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-carrera-3-2-1984-1989/), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/36.jpg))
  - **rim_rear**: 7J x 15 (MY1984–88); 8J x 16 (MY1989) ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-carrera-3-2-1984-1989/), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/36.jpg))
  - **rim_diameter_in**: 15 standard MY1984–88; 16 standard from MY1989 ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-carrera/), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/36.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/10-159.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fia-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-911-carrera-3-2-1986/))
  - **design_name**: Forged Fuchs 5-spoke light-alloy wheels ('Forged 15" Fuchs alloy wheels were fitted as standard on the 911 Carrera') ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-carrera/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-for-dummies-ducktail-fuchs-rims-10058.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2024/history/porsche-klassik-911-g-model-anniversary-model-1987-35245.html), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-carrera-3-2-1984-1989/))
  - **spoke_count**: 5 ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-for-dummies-ducktail-fuchs-rims-10058.html))
  - **finish**: Black spoke star with polished rim lip (visual, 1989 brochure); 1987 '250,000' anniversary model: centres in body colour (Diamond Blue) ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/02.jpg), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/03.jpg), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2024/history/porsche-klassik-911-g-model-anniversary-model-1987-35245.html))
  - **hub_cap_center**: Crest centre cap (visual) ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/02.jpg))
  - **options_notes**: MY1988: 'new Fuchs wheels as standard'; MY1989 Fuchs 'bigger (both in diameter and width)'; other alloy designs optional ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-carrera-3-2-1984-1989/), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/31.jpg))
- **headlights**
  - **shape**: Round, upright in the front wings ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-–-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/02.jpg))
  - **surround_ring**: Headlight rings in vehicle colour ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-carrera/))
  - **mounting**: Upright in the front wing ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-–-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html))
  - **lens_technology**: Clear glass with fluted optic (visual, 1989 brochure) — _Lamp type not stated._ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/02.jpg))
  - **washers**: _not found_ — _not found in: Porsche Classic Carrera page, stuttcars Carrera 3.2 page, 1989 brochure pages viewed_
  - **other**: _not found_ — _not found in: —_
- **front**
  - **bumper_type**: Aluminium impact bumper with black bellows (homologation B-282: front bumper aluminium) ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-carrera/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/10-159.jpg), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-–-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fia-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-911-carrera-3-2-1986/))
  - **overriders**: None at front — _Visual._ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/02.jpg))
  - **indicators**: Amber indicators in the front bumper ends; amber side markers on the bumper sides (visual, 1989 brochure) ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/02.jpg), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/03.jpg))
  - **fog_lights**: Rectangular fog lamps integrated into the front valance/apron below the bumper — the main external difference from the SC ([en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(classic)), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-carrera-3-2-1984-1989/), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/02.jpg))
  - **horn_grilles**: No separate horn grilles visible on the bumper face in brochure front photos — _Visual absence only._ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/02.jpg))
  - **air_intakes**: Front apron with integrated fog lamps; from MY1985 the front spoiler was changed to accommodate a hidden oil-cooler duct; PU front spoiler optional from MY1984 ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-carrera-3-2-1984-1989/), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-carrera/))
  - **front_lid_hood**: Flat steel front lid with crest (homologation B-282: front lid steel) ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/10-159.jpg), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/02.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fia-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-911-carrera-3-2-1986/))
  - **badge_crest**: Porsche crest on front lid (visual) ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/02.jpg))
  - **other**: _not found_ — _not found in: —_
- **rear**
  - **taillights**: Separate corner taillight units joined by the red rear light strip; black PORSCHE logo MY1984–86, red reflective logo with embedded rear fog lights from MY1987 ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-carrera/), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/25.jpg))
  - **reflector_band**: Full-width red strip with PORSCHE lettering between the taillights ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-carrera/), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/25.jpg))
  - **bumper**: Aluminium impact bumper with black bellows and two large rubber buffers flanking the plate ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-carrera/), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-911-s/), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/25.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/10-159.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fia-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-911-carrera-3-2-1986/))
  - **exhaust**: Single round tailpipe exiting below the rear bumper on the LEFT side (visual: 1988 Carrera 3.2 Coupé at the Porsche Museum, Commons photo) — _Visual only._ ([commons.wikimedia.org](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Porsche_911_1988_Carrera_3.2_Coup%C3%A8_LSideRear_PorscheM_9June2013_(14826030908).jpg))
  - **license_plate**: Centred in rear bumper between rubber buffers ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-911-s/), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/25.jpg))
  - **badging**: 'Carrera' script on the engine lid below the grille (visible on homologation B-282 photo B); Porsche Classic: 'a corresponding logo now adorned the attractive rear'. 1987 anniversary model omitted the lettering ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/01-545.jpg), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2024/history/porsche-klassik-911-g-model-anniversary-model-1987-35245.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/.imaging/mte/porsche-templating-theme/image_1290x726/dam/pnr/2021/History/Felsengr%C3%BCn/b-DJI_0019.jpg/jcr%3Acontent/b-DJI_0019.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fia-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-911-carrera-3-2-1986/))
  - **other**: Raised third brake light ('E.T.') from 1986 so it clears the rear spoiler — _Market applicability not stated._ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-for-dummies-ducktail-fuchs-rims-10058.html))
- **rear_spoiler**
  - **type**: None as standard; optional flat rear wing with black hard-rubber border (plus PU front spoiler) from MY1984; Turbo-look M491 ('Supersport') cars carry the Turbo tea-tray ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-carrera/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/50-Jahre-Porsche-911-(G-Serie)-–-Der-Dauerbrenner-von-Porsche.html), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/25.jpg), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(classic)), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-carrera-3-2-1984-1989/))
  - **deploy**: Fixed when fitted ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-carrera/))
- **engine_lid_grille**
  - **design**: Black horizontal-louvre grille on the engine lid (inside the optional wing when fitted — 1989 brochure top-rear photo) ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/25.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/01-545.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fia-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-911-carrera-3-2-1986/))
- **body**
  - **fender_flares**: Carrera-width rear wings ('slightly wider on the rear wings than that on the 911 and 911 S'), same as SC ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-carrera/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/02-514.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fia-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-911-carrera-3-2-1986/))
  - **rear_arch_width**: 1652 mm at rear axle vs 1610 mm at front axle ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/02-514.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fia-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-911-carrera-3-2-1986/))
  - **mirrors**: Door-mounted, electrically adjustable, vehicle colour ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-carrera/))
  - **door_handles**: Anodised black ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-carrera/))
  - **side_windows**: Anodised-black window surrounds ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-carrera/))
  - **quarter_window**: Classic fixed quarter window (safety glass per B-282) ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/10-159.jpg), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/25.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fia-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-911-carrera-3-2-1986/))
  - **drip_rails**: Present (visual) — _Visual._ ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/25.jpg))
  - **roofline**: Classic 911 fastback; height 1320 mm ([archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/36.jpg))
  - **fuel_filler**: Round flap on the left front wing just ahead of the windscreen (visual: Porsche Newsroom aerial photo of a 1987 Carrera 3.2; same position on SC and Turbo brochure photos) — _Visual only._ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2021/history/porsche-klassik-911-carrera-3-2-granite-green-christian-geistdoerfer-18920.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/.imaging/mte/porsche-templating-theme/image_1290x726/dam/pnr/2021/History/Felsengr%C3%BCn/b-DJI_0019.jpg/jcr%3Acontent/b-DJI_0019.jpg), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/05.jpg), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/101_20260421_20260421_1846/113.png))
  - **windshield**: Laminated windscreen (B-282); radio antenna integrated in the windscreen from MY1985 ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/10-159.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-carrera-3-2-1984-1989/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fia-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-911-carrera-3-2-1986/))
  - **chrome_trim**: None (black trim) ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-carrera/))
  - **side_repeaters**: Side indicator lights on the front wings (Carrera identifier from MY1984), just behind the front wheel arch, roughly level with the top of the arch (visual, 1989 brochure side photo) ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-carrera/), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/03.jpg), [commons.wikimedia.org](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Porsche_911_1988_Carrera_3.2_Coup%C3%A8_LSideRear_PorscheM_9June2013_(14826030908).jpg))
  - **rocker_sills**: Black trim strip along the sill below the doors (visual) — _Visual only._ ([commons.wikimedia.org](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Porsche_911_1988_Carrera_3.2_Coup%C3%A8_LSideRear_PorscheM_9June2013_(14826030908).jpg), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/03.jpg))
  - **other**: Turbo-look/Supersport M491 (Turbo body, brakes, wheels, suspension; Coupé first, all bodies from MY1986) and lightweight Club Sport Coupé (1987–89) exist — not the reference ([en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(classic)), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-carrera-3-2-1984-1989/), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/31.jpg))
- **lighting_signature**: Round wing-top headlamps with body-colour rings, amber bumper indicators, fog lamps flush in the front apron, red rear strip with PORSCHE logo (red reflective + rear fog lamps from MY1987) ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-carrera/), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/02.jpg))
- **distinctive**
  - Fog lamps integrated in the front valance ([en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(classic)), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-carrera-3-2-1984-1989/))
  - Front-wing side repeaters ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-carrera/))
  - 'Carrera' rear lid script ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/01-545.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/fia-homologation-protocol-for-porsche-911-carrera-3-2-1986/))
- **open_questions**
  - Headlamp washers standard or optional
  - Front track 1372 (Porsche 1989) vs 1398 mm (Excellence MY1987–89)
  - Turbo-look introduction: 1984 (Wikipedia) vs 1986 standard option (stuttcars)
- **model_year_changes**
  - MY1984: launch — side repeaters on front wings, integrated fog lamps, 'Carrera' rear script; optional PU front spoiler + flat rear wing; Turbo-look M491 via specials department ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-carrera/), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-carrera-3-2-1984-1989/), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(classic)))
  - MY1985: radio antenna in windscreen, heated washers, front spoiler reshaped for a hidden oil-cooler duct ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-carrera-3-2-1984-1989/))
  - MY1986: Turbo-look for Coupé, Targa and Cabriolet; raised third brake light ('E.T.') ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-carrera-3-2-1984-1989/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-for-dummies-ducktail-fuchs-rims-10058.html))
  - MY1987: G50 5-speed + hydraulic clutch; rear strip with red reflective logo and embedded fog lamps; 195/65 front tyres; Club Sport ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-carrera/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-carrera-3-2-1984-1989/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2021/history/porsche-klassik-911-carrera-3-2-granite-green-christian-geistdoerfer-18920.html), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(classic)))
  - MY1988: new Fuchs wheels standard; 250,000th-911 anniversary model (body-colour wheel centres) ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-carrera-3-2-1984-1989/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2024/history/porsche-klassik-911-g-model-anniversary-model-1987-35245.html))
  - MY1989: 16-in Fuchs 6J/8J standard; Speedster ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/usa/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/911-g/911-carrera/), [archive.org](https://archive.org/download/01_20260422_20260422_1505/36.jpg), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-carrera-3-2-1984-1989/))

### 09-964-1989 — Porsche 911 Carrera 4 / Carrera 2 (Type 964) Coupé

- **blueprints**: `blueprints/09-964-1989/` — primary: `vec-10947_porsche-911-carrera-964-1990_4view-dims-watermarked.jpg`, `vec-23631_porsche-911-964-targa-1993_4view-dims-watermarked.jpg` — Weakest modern stop: the vendor 4-view (dims printed) is ~3% off in WB/L; plus Autocar-type top/side and a cutaway. Scale every 964 drawing by the official wheelbase (2272 mm) and check overhangs against geometry.json.
- **production_years**: 1988–1994 (Carrera 4 launched Aug 1988 as MY1989; Carrera 2 from MY1990; 63,762 built) — _Porsche: C4 at 114,500 DM from August 1988, C2 'a year later'. en-Wikipedia gives production 1989–Dec 1993 (https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(964))._ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-seven-generations-part-3-type-964-16466.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/3.-Generation-Porsche-911,-(964),-1988---1994.html), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/964/964/))
- **reference_variant**: 911 Carrera 2 Coupé, MY1990–1991, RoW/European spec, standard 16-in 'Design 90' wheels, spoiler retracted, pre-MY1992 door mirrors — _Numbers below are from Porsche's MY1991 brochure 'Technical data and specification 1991 model year' (UK edition, WVK 127 120). Carrera 4 is externally identical except the '4' script on the engine lid (stuttcars). If the morph should show the later look, switch mirrors to the MY1992+ 'Cup design' mirrors (Porsche Classic)._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/964-1991.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1989-1994-sales-brochures/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-carrera-2-coupe-964-1990-1994/))
- **dimensions_mm**
  - **length**: 4250 — _RoW brochure 4250. Conflicts: en-Wikipedia infobox 4,275 / body text 4,270 (https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(964)), AMS database record for 'Carrera Cup/RS' 4275. Use 4250._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/964-1991.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1989-1994-sales-brochures/), [auto-motor-und-sport.de](https://www.auto-motor-und-sport.de/oldtimer/porsche-911-964-carrera-2-im-fahrbericht-auch-der-964-ist-ein-echter-elfer/technische-daten/), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/291))
  - **width**: 1652 — _Excellence (US) 1,651; en-Wikipedia 1,650. Use 1652 (body without mirrors)._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/964-1991.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1989-1994-sales-brochures/), [auto-motor-und-sport.de](https://www.auto-motor-und-sport.de/oldtimer/porsche-911-964-carrera-2-im-fahrbericht-auch-der-964-ist-ein-echter-elfer/technische-daten/))
  - **height**: 1310 — _Brochure: 'Height (unladen) 1310 mm'. AMS test data 1320; Excellence 1,318; de-Wikipedia 1310 (tech table) / 1320 (infobox). Recommend 1310 unladen._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/964-1991.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1989-1994-sales-brochures/))
  - **wheelbase**: 2272 — _en-Wikipedia rounds to 2,270._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/964-1991.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1989-1994-sales-brochures/), [auto-motor-und-sport.de](https://www.auto-motor-und-sport.de/oldtimer/porsche-911-964-carrera-2-im-fahrbericht-auch-der-964-ist-ein-echter-elfer/technische-daten/), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/291), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_964))
  - **track_front**: 1380 — _16-in Design 90 wheels._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/964-1991.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1989-1994-sales-brochures/), [auto-motor-und-sport.de](https://www.auto-motor-und-sport.de/oldtimer/porsche-911-964-carrera-2-im-fahrbericht-auch-der-964-ist-ein-echter-elfer/technische-daten/), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/291))
  - **track_rear**: 1374 — _en-Wikipedia rounds to 1,370._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/964-1991.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1989-1994-sales-brochures/), [auto-motor-und-sport.de](https://www.auto-motor-und-sport.de/oldtimer/porsche-911-964-carrera-2-im-fahrbericht-auch-der-964-ist-ein-echter-elfer/technische-daten/), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/291))
  - **ground_clearance**: 120 — _Brochure: 'Ground clearance (laden) 120 mm'. Excellence 4.72 in/120 mm._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/964-1991.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1989-1994-sales-brochures/), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/291))
- **overhang_mm**
  - **front**: 880 — _ESTIMATE (±50 mm), not a published figure. Measured on Porsche's studio side profile (press photo id 250539, 1440 px): bumper nose x=153, front hub x=390, rear hub x=1028, rear bumper x=1322. Photo overhang ratio front:rear = 237:294 applied to (length 4250 − wheelbase 2272 = 1978 mm). Photo WB/L = 0.546 vs true 0.535 → ~2% perspective error._ ([porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250539&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=d9cd9b43aeb42fe7), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/964-1991.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1989-1994-sales-brochures/))
  - **rear**: 1095 — _ESTIMATE (±50 mm) – same method as front._ ([porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250539&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=d9cd9b43aeb42fe7), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/964-1991.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1989-1994-sales-brochures/))
- **other_published**
  - **curb_weight_kg**: 1350 — _DIN unladen, Carrera 2 manual; 1380 Tiptronic; 1450 Carrera 4._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/964-1991.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1989-1994-sales-brochures/))
  - **drag_cd**: 0.32 — _Porsche: 'the most aerodynamic 911 to date' / 'most aerodynamic air-cooled 911'._ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/3.-Generation-Porsche-911,-(964),-1988---1994.html), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/964-1991.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1989-1994-sales-brochures/), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/291))
  - **windshield_rake_deg**: _not found_ — _not found in: 1991 brochure, Porsche newsroom 964 pages, Wikipedia en/de; brochure only says 'more aerodynamic bonded windscreen'_
- **wheels**
  - **tyre_front**: 205/55 ZR 16 ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/964-1991.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1989-1994-sales-brochures/), [auto-motor-und-sport.de](https://www.auto-motor-und-sport.de/oldtimer/porsche-911-964-carrera-2-im-fahrbericht-auch-der-964-ist-ein-echter-elfer/technische-daten/), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/291))
  - **tyre_rear**: 225/50 ZR 16 ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/964-1991.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1989-1994-sales-brochures/), [auto-motor-und-sport.de](https://www.auto-motor-und-sport.de/oldtimer/porsche-911-964-carrera-2-im-fahrbericht-auch-der-964-ist-ein-echter-elfer/technische-daten/), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/291))
  - **rim_front**: 6J x 16 ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/964-1991.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1989-1994-sales-brochures/), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/291))
  - **rim_rear**: 8J x 16 ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/964-1991.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1989-1994-sales-brochures/), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/291))
  - **rim_diameter_in**: 16 ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/964-1991.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1989-1994-sales-brochures/))
  - **design_name**: 'Design 90' pressure-cast light-alloy wheel — _Brochure wheel row: Carrera 2/Carrera 4 'Pressure cast light alloy Design 90'; the 964 Turbo got 'Cup design' 7J/9J x 17._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/964-1991.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1989-1994-sales-brochures/))
  - **spoke_count**: 7 — _visual: flat, slightly dished disc face with 7 teardrop cut-outs near the rim (i.e. 7 broad 'spokes'), 5 lug bolts, crest centre cap._ ([porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250539&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=d9cd9b43aeb42fe7), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250555&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=c4f1d101d31a67c4), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/964-1991.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1989-1994-sales-brochures/))
  - **finish**: silver-painted alloy (as on Porsche press cars) — _visual, Porsche press photos_ ([porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250539&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=d9cd9b43aeb42fe7), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250555&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=c4f1d101d31a67c4))
  - **hub_cap_center**: centre cap with Porsche crest — _visual, Porsche press photo_ ([porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250555&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=c4f1d101d31a67c4))
  - **options_notes**: 17-in 'Cup design' 7J x 17 / 9J x 17 with 205/50 ZR17 / 255/40 ZR17 is the 964 Turbo fitment; 17-in wheels also appear on later Carrera-based variants (AMS: Speedster 7J/8J x 17). The blue Porsche press car (S-KU 90) wears 5-hole Cup-style wheels. — _Care: the AMS 17-in 'Carrera 2 Cabriolet 1991–94' record is 1775 mm wide (apparently the Turbo-look body); the AMS Speedster record lists 7J/8J x 17. Exact model year from which 17-in Cup wheels were offered on Carrera 2/4 coupés not confirmed from a primary source._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/964-1991.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1989-1994-sales-brochures/), [auto-motor-und-sport.de](https://www.auto-motor-und-sport.de/marken-modelle/porsche/911/964/technische-daten/), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250554&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=97ea7ef2fa8b6365))
- **headlights**
  - **shape**: round headlamps — _Porsche: 'headlights were positioned almost vertically in the tunnels of the wings'; 993 article contrasts 'the upright round headlights of the older 964'._ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/3.-Generation---Design-und-Modellvarianten.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/30-Jahre-911-Carrera-der-Generation-993-%E2%80%93-Der-letzte-seiner-Art.html), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250539&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=d9cd9b43aeb42fe7))
  - **surround_ring**: thin bright lens rim around the round lamp, which sits in a body-colour wing 'tunnel' — _visual only (Porsche press photo)_ ([porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250554&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=97ea7ef2fa8b6365))
  - **mounting**: upright/almost vertical in the traditional front-wing 'tunnels' — _Porsche Classic 993 page contrasts the 993's flatter wings with the previous headlamp 'tunnels'; de-Wikipedia calls them 'Torpedorohre' (https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_993)._ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/3.-Generation---Design-und-Modellvarianten.html), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/993/993/))
  - **lens_technology**: _not found_ — _not found in: brochure and Porsche text (H4 etc. not stated)_
  - **washers**: high-pressure headlight washer jets (nozzle on the bumper top edge below each lamp) — _Nozzle position visual. Brochure caption: 'The powerful headlights are kept clean with a high pressure water-jet'; capacities list 'Screenwasher 7.4 litres (inclusive of fluid for headlamp washer system)'._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/964-1991.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1989-1994-sales-brochures/), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250554&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=97ea7ef2fa8b6365))
  - **other**: _not found_ — _no additional feature recorded_
- **front**
  - **bumper_type**: integrated, body-coloured deformable thermoplastic front panel encasing a resilient bumper (first integrated bumper on the 911) — _Brochure: 'front and rear deformable thermoplastic body panels ... encase resilient bumpers'; Porsche Classic: 'Front and rear panels made of deformable plastic'; Porsche newsroom: 'aerodynamic polyurethane bumpers', bumpers 'integrated into the body line as solid elements', body 'in practice only modified by having integrated bumpers'._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/964-1991.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1989-1994-sales-brochures/), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/964/964/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-seven-generations-part-3-type-964-16466.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/products/porsche-one-millionth-911-milestone-seven-generations-13735.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2019/history/porsche-klassik-911-eight-generations-dna-18914.html))
  - **overriders**: none — _integrated bumper; no separate overriders listed or visible_ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/964-1991.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1989-1994-sales-brochures/), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/964/964/))
  - **indicators**: in the front bumper, recessed/flush, amber lens beside a clear lamp at each corner — _Porsche: 'number plate, air intakes, indicators and fog lights were integrated into the front bumper for the first time'; brochure caption 'Integral foglamps and indicators are recessed to improve aerodynamics'._ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/3.-Generation---Design-und-Modellvarianten.html), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/964-1991.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1989-1994-sales-brochures/), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250555&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=c4f1d101d31a67c4))
  - **fog_lights**: rectangular fog lamps integrated flush in the front bumper corners, inboard of the amber indicators — _shape/position visual (brochure caption photo, press photo)_ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/3.-Generation---Design-und-Modellvarianten.html), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/964-1991.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1989-1994-sales-brochures/), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(964)), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250554&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=97ea7ef2fa8b6365))
  - **horn_grilles**: none visible on wings or bumper — _visual only; no text source_ ([porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250554&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=97ea7ef2fa8b6365), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250555&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=c4f1d101d31a67c4))
  - **air_intakes**: air intakes integrated in the front bumper: two long horizontal black slots across the lower bumper face plus slots below the corner lamp units — _Porsche: air intakes integrated into the bumper; layout visual (press photo + brochure corner close-up)._ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/3.-Generation---Design-und-Modellvarianten.html), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/964-1991.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1989-1994-sales-brochures/), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250554&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=97ea7ef2fa8b6365))
  - **front_lid_hood**: black rubber lip between the front luggage lid and the bumper (as on G-model) — _Porsche Classic (993 page): the 993's shortened lid 'eliminated the Black rubber lip that connected the bodyshell and the front bumper on the G model and 964'._ ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/993/993/), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250554&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=97ea7ef2fa8b6365))
  - **badge_crest**: Porsche crest on the front lid — _visual_ ([porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250554&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=97ea7ef2fa8b6365))
  - **other**: number plate integrated in the centre of the front bumper face ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/3.-Generation---Design-und-Modellvarianten.html), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250554&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=97ea7ef2fa8b6365))
- **rear**
  - **taillights**: full-width rear light strip: red reflective panel with 'PORSCHE' logo between the tail lamps, integrated rear fog lights; red (not amber) rear indicator lenses — _Porsche Classic (C2 & C4 Coupé): 'Rear light strip with Red reflective Porsche logo and integrated rear fog lights // Red rear indicators'._ ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/964/964/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:a01e2e4e-8283-4b4a-95a3-d7c01ea06f9f/03_911er_Generationen_964_EN.pdf))
  - **reflector_band**: yes – red reflective band with PORSCHE lettering spanning between the lamps ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/964/964/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:a01e2e4e-8283-4b4a-95a3-d7c01ea06f9f/03_911er_Generationen_964_EN.pdf))
  - **bumper**: integrated deformable thermoplastic rear panel encasing a resilient bumper ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/964-1991.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1989-1994-sales-brochures/), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/964/964/))
  - **exhaust**: single round tailpipe exiting below the right-hand end of the rear bumper — _visual only (1991 brochure pp.12–13, rear-three-quarter photo of a Carrera 2); no text source found (checked brochure text, Porsche Classic, Porsche newsroom, Wikipedia en/de)._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/964-1991.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1989-1994-sales-brochures/))
  - **license_plate**: recessed plate panel in the centre of the rear bumper — _visual (1991 brochure p.13 Carrera 2; Turbo 3.6 in Porsche infographic)_ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/964-1991.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1989-1994-sales-brochures/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:a01e2e4e-8283-4b4a-95a3-d7c01ea06f9f/03_911er_Generationen_964_EN.pdf))
  - **badging**: 'Carrera 2' or 'Carrera 4' script on the engine lid — _stuttcars: C2 'looks exactly like the Carrera 4 other than the obligatory 2 on engine lid versus a 4'._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-carrera-2-coupe-964-1990-1994/))
  - **other**: _not found_ — _no additional feature recorded_
- **rear_spoiler**
  - **type**: electrically extending rear spoiler integrated flush in the engine lid (Porsche patent); retracts flush at low speed ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/3.-Generation-Porsche-911,-(964),-1988---1994.html), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/964/964/), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(964)))
  - **deploy**: extends at ~80 km/h (~50 mph); retracts at ~10 km/h (~6 mph) — _Porsche newsroom (60 Years press kit): 'extended at speeds over 80 km/h'. 1991 brochure: 'Automatically extending at around 50 mph ... the spoiler retracts at around 6 mph'; it 'doubles the volume of engine air-intake'. Conflict: Porsche 2019 'For eternity' article says 'above 50 km/h' (https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2019/history/porsche-klassik-911-eight-generations-dna-18914.html) – likely an mph/km/h mix-up; use 80 km/h._ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/3.-Generation-Porsche-911,-(964),-1988---1994.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/3.-Generation---Design-und-Modellvarianten.html), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/964-1991.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1989-1994-sales-brochures/), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_964))
- **engine_lid_grille**
  - **design**: black horizontal-louvre grille forming the top of the flush spoiler panel on the engine lid; the whole grille/spoiler panel lifts when deployed ('doubles the volume of engine air-intake') — _visual (1991 brochure pp.12–13 incl. inset photo of the raised spoiler) + brochure text._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/964-1991.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1989-1994-sales-brochures/))
- **body**
  - **fender_flares**: narrow body; classic 911 wing shapes retained (Turbo/Jubilee/RS 3.8 used the wide body) — _Brochure widths: Carrera 1652 mm vs Turbo 1775 mm._ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-seven-generations-part-3-type-964-16466.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/3.-Generation-Porsche-911,-(964),-1988---1994.html), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/964-1991.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1989-1994-sales-brochures/))
  - **rear_arch_width**: narrow (Carrera 1652 mm overall width) ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/964-1991.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1989-1994-sales-brochures/))
  - **mirrors**: door-mounted exterior mirrors in body colour, electrically adjustable; 'Cup design' aero mirrors from MY1992 — _Press photos: the early black C4 (BB-PW 215) has the flat older-style mirror, the blue car (S-KU 90) the rounded Cup mirror._ ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/964/964/), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250555&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=c4f1d101d31a67c4), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250554&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=97ea7ef2fa8b6365))
  - **door_handles**: black door handle mounted high on the door, behind the window line — _visual only (1991 brochure p.12; Porsche press profile). The 993 brochure notes the 911 door itself stayed 'basically unchanged for more than 30 years' (https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1995.pdf)._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/964-1991.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1989-1994-sales-brochures/), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250539&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=d9cd9b43aeb42fe7))
  - **side_windows**: classic 911 greenhouse carried over: door glass with a slim vertical division bar near its front edge, B-pillar trim, fixed rear quarter window — _Porsche: familiar body, only modified by integrated bumpers; window details visual._ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-seven-generations-part-3-type-964-16466.html), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250539&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=d9cd9b43aeb42fe7))
  - **quarter_window**: fixed rear side (quarter) window, classic 911 shape — _visual_ ([porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250539&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=d9cd9b43aeb42fe7))
  - **drip_rails**: rain gutters retained but smoothed/aerodynamically optimised ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/3.-Generation-Porsche-911,-(964),-1988---1994.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/3.-Generation---Design-und-Modellvarianten.html), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/964-1991.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1989-1994-sales-brochures/))
  - **roofline**: unchanged classic 911 roofline ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-seven-generations-part-3-type-964-16466.html))
  - **fuel_filler**: round flap on top of the left front wing, just ahead of the windscreen/door — _visual only (1991 brochure p.12 close-up of LHD car); no text source found._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/964-1991.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1989-1994-sales-brochures/))
  - **windshield**: bonded windscreen (more aerodynamic) ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/964-1991.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1989-1994-sales-brochures/))
  - **chrome_trim**: _not found_ — _not found in: Porsche text (visual check pending)_
  - **side_repeaters**: small amber side repeater on each front wing behind the wheel arch — _visual_ ([porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250539&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=d9cd9b43aeb42fe7), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250555&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=c4f1d101d31a67c4))
  - **rocker_sills**: new aerodynamic plastic sill covers (side skirts) between the wheel arches — _Porsche: 'most obvious in the bullish-looking front end and the modified sill covers'; brochure 'streamlined sill mouldings'._ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/3.-Generation-Porsche-911,-(964),-1988---1994.html), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/964-1991.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1989-1994-sales-brochures/))
  - **other**: completely smooth underbody panels; doors and front lid almost identical to the G-model ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/3.-Generation---Design-und-Modellvarianten.html), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/964-1991.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1989-1994-sales-brochures/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2019/history/porsche-klassik-911-eight-generations-dna-18914.html))
- **lighting_signature**: Upright round headlamps in wing tunnels; flush amber-and-clear lamp clusters (indicator + fog) in the front bumper corners; amber side repeaters on front wings; full-width red rear strip with reflective PORSCHE logo and red rear indicators. ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/3.-Generation---Design-und-Modellvarianten.html), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/964/964/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/964-1991.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1989-1994-sales-brochures/))
- **distinctive**
  - First 911 with integrated (deformable plastic) bumpers – the classic silhouette otherwise kept; ~85% new parts ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-seven-generations-part-3-type-964-16466.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/3.-Generation-Porsche-911,-(964),-1988---1994.html), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/964/964/))
  - Electric rear spoiler that pops up above ~80 km/h and sits flush in the engine lid when retracted ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/3.-Generation-Porsche-911,-(964),-1988---1994.html), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/964-1991.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1989-1994-sales-brochures/))
  - Cd 0.32 (vs classic 911 shape) thanks to front/rear panels, sill covers, rain gutters and smooth underbody ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/3.-Generation-Porsche-911,-(964),-1988---1994.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/3.-Generation---Design-und-Modellvarianten.html))
- **open_questions**
  - Exhaust layout is visual only (single tailpipe, right rear) – no text source found.
  - Exact MY when 17-in Cup wheels became available on Carrera 2/4 (AMS shows 17-in on C2 Cabriolet 1991–94; Porsche text not found).
  - Windshield rake not published; the 993 kept the 964 roofline and Porsche's 996 brochure implies ~60° for the predecessor – plausibly the same glass angle (inference, not verified).
  - Overhangs are photo estimates (±50 mm), no official figure.

### 10-993-1994 — Porsche 911 Carrera (Type 993) Coupé

- **blueprints**: `blueprints/10-993-1994/` — primary: `tbp-92535_porsche-911-carrera-1997_4view.png` — Good: 4-view line drawing (ratio matches official within 0.5%) + vendor 5-view + 993 Cabriolet 4-view.
- **production_years**: 1993–1998 (unveiled IAA autumn 1993, model year 1994 onward; 68,881 built) — _en-Wikipedia: production January 1994 – March 1998 (https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(993))._ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/4.-Generation-Porsche-911,-(993),-1993-1998.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-seven-generations-part-4-type-993-16486.html), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/993/993/))
- **reference_variant**: 911 Carrera Coupé MY1994–1995 (272 PS), European spec, standard 16-in wheels, spoiler retracted — _MY1996+ Carrera: 285 PS VarioRam, 'two slightly rectangular tailpipes' instead of oval (Porsche Classic). Widebody Carrera 4S/Carrera S are separate variants (60 mm wider Turbo body)._ ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/993/993/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1993.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1994-1998-sales-brochures/))
- **dimensions_mm**
  - **length**: 4245 — _European length 4245 (AMS database, de-Wikipedia). US-market figure 4260 (PCNA MY1995 brochure '167.7 in (4,260 mm)', https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1995.pdf; Excellence; en-Wikipedia) – US bumpers. Use 4245 for a European car, 4260 if modelling a US car._ ([auto-motor-und-sport.de](https://www.auto-motor-und-sport.de/marken-modelle/porsche/911/993/technische-daten/), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_993))
  - **width**: 1735 — _PCNA MY1995 brochure: 68.3 in (1,735 mm)._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1995.pdf), [auto-motor-und-sport.de](https://www.auto-motor-und-sport.de/marken-modelle/porsche/911/993/technische-daten/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1993.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1994-1998-sales-brochures/), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/312))
  - **height**: 1300 — _Conflicts: AMS 1300; de-Wikipedia 1310; PCNA MY1995 brochure 51.8 in (1,315 mm); Excellence 1,315; en-Wikipedia 1,311 (1994–95)/1,316 (1996–98). Recommend 1300 (European database value); US-spec sources run 10–16 mm higher._ ([auto-motor-und-sport.de](https://www.auto-motor-und-sport.de/marken-modelle/porsche/911/993/technische-daten/))
  - **wheelbase**: 2272 — _PCNA: 89.4 in (2,272 mm)._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1995.pdf), [auto-motor-und-sport.de](https://www.auto-motor-und-sport.de/marken-modelle/porsche/911/993/technische-daten/), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/312), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_993), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1993.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1994-1998-sales-brochures/))
  - **track_front**: 1405 — _16-in wheels_ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1995.pdf), [auto-motor-und-sport.de](https://www.auto-motor-und-sport.de/marken-modelle/porsche/911/993/technische-daten/), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/312), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_993))
  - **track_rear**: 1444 — _de-Wikipedia 1445; PCNA 56.9 in (1,444 mm)._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1995.pdf), [auto-motor-und-sport.de](https://www.auto-motor-und-sport.de/marken-modelle/porsche/911/993/technische-daten/), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/312))
  - **ground_clearance**: 120 — _PCNA MY1994 brochure 4.7 in; Excellence 4.72 in/120 mm._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1993.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1994-1998-sales-brochures/), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/312))
- **overhang_mm**
  - **front**: 938 — _ESTIMATE (±50 mm). Porsche studio side profile (press photo id 250540): nose x=155, front hub x=389, rear hub x=995, tail x=1253 → ratio 234:258 applied to 4245 − 2272 = 1973 mm. Photo WB/L 0.552 vs true 0.535 (perspective)._ ([porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250540&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=eedee361158555dc), [auto-motor-und-sport.de](https://www.auto-motor-und-sport.de/marken-modelle/porsche/911/993/technische-daten/))
  - **rear**: 1035 — _ESTIMATE (±50 mm), same method._ ([porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250540&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=eedee361158555dc), [auto-motor-und-sport.de](https://www.auto-motor-und-sport.de/marken-modelle/porsche/911/993/technische-daten/))
- **other_published**
  - **curb_weight_kg**: 1370 — _AMS 1370 (manual, Euro). PCNA MY1995 brochure 3,064 lb (1,390 kg) manual / 1,415 kg Tiptronic S (https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1995.pdf); Excellence 3,020 lb._ ([auto-motor-und-sport.de](https://www.auto-motor-und-sport.de/marken-modelle/porsche/911/993/technische-daten/))
  - **drag_cd**: 0.33 — _Excellence 0.33; de-Wikipedia (996 article) says 993 cw 0.34 (https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_996). No Porsche figure found yet._ ([excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/312))
  - **windshield_rake_deg**: 60 — _Derived from Porsche's 996 brochure: the 996 windshield is 'raked more steeply at 55 degrees versus 60' (i.e. predecessor 993 = 60°). Angle reference (from vertical/horizontal) not stated._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/))
- **wheels**
  - **tyre_front**: 205/55 ZR 16 ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1993.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1994-1998-sales-brochures/), [auto-motor-und-sport.de](https://www.auto-motor-und-sport.de/marken-modelle/porsche/911/993/technische-daten/), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/312))
  - **tyre_rear**: 245/45 ZR 16 ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1993.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1994-1998-sales-brochures/), [auto-motor-und-sport.de](https://www.auto-motor-und-sport.de/marken-modelle/porsche/911/993/technische-daten/), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/312))
  - **rim_front**: 7J x 16 ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1993.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1994-1998-sales-brochures/), [auto-motor-und-sport.de](https://www.auto-motor-und-sport.de/marken-modelle/porsche/911/993/technische-daten/))
  - **rim_rear**: 9J x 16 ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1993.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1994-1998-sales-brochures/), [auto-motor-und-sport.de](https://www.auto-motor-und-sport.de/marken-modelle/porsche/911/993/technische-daten/))
  - **rim_diameter_in**: 16 ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1993.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1994-1998-sales-brochures/))
  - **design_name**: 5-spoke cast alloy (Cup-style) – exact Porsche design name not yet confirmed — _US brochure: 'Std. Cast Alloy' 7Jx16/9Jx16; option 'Carrera Cup 5-spoke 17 inch wheels'. Press photos show 5-spoke wheels with crest centre caps._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1993.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1994-1998-sales-brochures/), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250540&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=eedee361158555dc))
  - **spoke_count**: 5 — _visual + brochure ('5-spoke' for the 17-in Cup option)_ ([porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250540&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=eedee361158555dc), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1993.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1994-1998-sales-brochures/))
  - **finish**: silver painted — _visual_ ([porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250540&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=eedee361158555dc))
  - **hub_cap_center**: Porsche crest centre cap — _brochure wheel close-up_ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1993.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1994-1998-sales-brochures/), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250540&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=eedee361158555dc))
  - **options_notes**: Optional 7J x 17 (205/50 ZR17) front / 9J x 17 (255/40 ZR17) rear 'Carrera Cup 5-spoke 17 inch wheels'; Sport package includes 17-in wheels (coupé only). Carrera 4 (MY1995) had 7J/9J x 17 as standard; Turbo 8J/10J x 18. ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1993.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1994-1998-sales-brochures/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1995.pdf))
- **headlights**
  - **shape**: flatter, inclined (raked-back) poly-ellipsoid headlamp units with sleek diffusing lenses (no longer upright 'tunnels') — _Porsche: 'inclined ellipsoid headlights'; 'The front section is lower-slung than on the earlier models, made possible by a switch from round to polyellipsoidal headlights'._ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/4.-Generation-Porsche-911,-(993),-1993-1998.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/4.-Generation---Design-und-Modellvarianten.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/30-Jahre-911-Carrera-der-Generation-993-%E2%80%93-Der-letzte-seiner-Art.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2019/history/porsche-klassik-911-eight-generations-dna-18914.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/products/porsche-one-millionth-911-milestone-seven-generations-13735.html))
  - **surround_ring**: clear rounded lens with thin rim, near-flush with the wing surface — _visual only_ ([porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250552&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=55632b1f4497cd6f))
  - **mounting**: laid flatter in lower, wider front wings ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/4.-Generation-Porsche-911,-(993),-1993-1998.html), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/993/993/))
  - **lens_technology**: modular unit separating poly-ellipsoid low beam from variable-focus high beam behind one clear, rounded lens — _PCNA MY1994: 'Unique modular poly-ellipsoid headlights with variable focus and washer system'; PCNA MY1995: 'a module separating the poly-ellipsoid low beams from the variable-focus high beams'. Litronic/xenon option not verified._ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/4.-Generation---Design-und-Modellvarianten.html), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1993.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1994-1998-sales-brochures/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1995.pdf))
  - **washers**: headlight washer system (nozzle visible below each lamp); heated windshield washer nozzles — _nozzle position visual (brochure close-ups)_ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1993.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1994-1998-sales-brochures/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1995.pdf))
  - **other**: _not found_ — _no additional feature recorded_
- **front**
  - **bumper_type**: integrated front bumper/apron (deformable plastic panel), smoother, redesigned ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/993/993/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-seven-generations-part-4-type-993-16486.html), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1993.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1994-1998-sales-brochures/), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(993)))
  - **overriders**: none (integrated bumper) ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/993/993/))
  - **indicators**: in the front apron; orange lenses on Carrera (white on Carrera 4 / 4S) ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/993/993/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/30-Jahre-911-Carrera-der-Generation-993-%E2%80%93-Der-letzte-seiner-Art.html), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1993.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1994-1998-sales-brochures/))
  - **fog_lights**: integrated into the front apron — _Porsche: 'The designers integrated side lights, fog lights and indicators into the front'._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1993.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1994-1998-sales-brochures/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/30-Jahre-911-Carrera-der-Generation-993-%E2%80%93-Der-letzte-seiner-Art.html))
  - **horn_grilles**: none visible — _visual only_ ([porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250552&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=55632b1f4497cd6f))
  - **air_intakes**: wide low central intake with two horizontal bars across the apron; indicator+fog pods in horizontal oval housings each side; front wheel vents underneath for brake cooling — _layout visual (Porsche press photo, PCNA brochure cover); 'front wheel vents improve both brake cooling and aerodynamics' is PCNA MY1995 text._ ([porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250552&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=55632b1f4497cd6f), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1993.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1994-1998-sales-brochures/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1995.pdf))
  - **front_lid_hood**: shortened luggage-compartment lid – the black rubber lip between body and bumper (G-model/964) is gone ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/993/993/))
  - **badge_crest**: Porsche crest on front lid — _visual_ ([porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250552&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=55632b1f4497cd6f))
  - **other**: US-market cars (PCNA brochure cover) show small black rubber guards on the apron either side; the European press car does not — _visual comparison only_ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1993.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1994-1998-sales-brochures/), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250552&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=55632b1f4497cd6f))
- **rear**
  - **taillights**: angled full-width light strip above the rear bumper with integrated tail lights and yellow (orange) indicators; tail lights set higher ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/30-Jahre-911-Carrera-der-Generation-993-%E2%80%93-Der-letzte-seiner-Art.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/4.-Generation---Design-und-Modellvarianten.html), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/993/993/))
  - **reflector_band**: yes – red reflective PORSCHE logo in the light strip ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/993/993/))
  - **bumper**: integrated deformable plastic rear panel ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/993/993/))
  - **exhaust**: two oval-shaped tailpipes, one at each side (MY1994–95); 'slightly rectangular' tailpipes from MY1996 — _en-Wikipedia: 'new dual-flow exhaust system'. PCNA MY1995 engine photo shows the silencer with an outlet at each end._ ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/993/993/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1995.pdf))
  - **license_plate**: centred in a recess in the rear bumper, below the light strip — _visual (PCNA MY1995 brochure rear photos)_ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1995.pdf))
  - **badging**: anodised-black 'Carrera' script on the engine lid, below the grille ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/993/993/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1995.pdf))
  - **other**: redesigned third brake light; clear rectangular reversing-lamp sections inboard of each tail lamp in the strip — _lamp layout visual_ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1993.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1994-1998-sales-brochures/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1995.pdf))
- **rear_spoiler**
  - **type**: larger, speed-dependent automatically extending rear spoiler integrated in the rear lid ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/993/993/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1993.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1994-1998-sales-brochures/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/30-Jahre-911-Carrera-der-Generation-993-%E2%80%93-Der-letzte-seiner-Art.html))
  - **deploy**: extends from ~80 km/h — _Primary Porsche figure not yet found; de-Wikipedia tech table: 'ab 80 km/h automatisch ausfahrendem Heckspoiler'._ ([de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_993))
- **engine_lid_grille**
  - **design**: black horizontal-louvre grille in a rounded-rectangle recess on the engine lid (top of the retractable spoiler); Carrera S instead had a two-part grille in body colour — _visual + Porsche Classic Carrera S contrast_ ([porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250553&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=ca6ec725c449d11f), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1995.pdf), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/993/993/))
- **body**
  - **fender_flares**: wider, flatter front wings; wider rear wings running straighter to the rear (more flared arches) ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/993/993/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/30-Jahre-911-Carrera-der-Generation-993-%E2%80%93-Der-letzte-seiner-Art.html), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(993)))
  - **rear_arch_width**: wider hips than 964: overall width 1735 vs 1652 mm (Carrera). Turbo/C4S/C S body 60 mm wider still ([auto-motor-und-sport.de](https://www.auto-motor-und-sport.de/marken-modelle/porsche/911/993/technische-daten/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/30-Jahre-911-Carrera-der-Generation-993-%E2%80%93-Der-letzte-seiner-Art.html), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/964-1991.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1989-1994-sales-brochures/))
  - **mirrors**: door-mounted 'Cup design' (teardrop) mirrors, electrically adjustable and heated ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/993/993/), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(993)), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1993.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1994-1998-sales-brochures/))
  - **door_handles**: redesigned body-colour pull handle (horizontal grab bar) with a separate round key cylinder at its front end — _PCNA MY1994: 'Redesigned outside door handles, painted to match exterior color'; PCNA MY1995: 'an improved door-handle design'; shape visual. Door shell itself 'basically unchanged for more than 30 years' (PCNA MY1995)._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1993.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1994-1998-sales-brochures/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1995.pdf))
  - **side_windows**: flush-mounted windows; same greenhouse layout as 964 (door glass with slim front division bar, B-pillar trim, fixed quarter window) — _layout visual_ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-seven-generations-part-4-type-993-16486.html), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250540&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=eedee361158555dc))
  - **quarter_window**: fixed rear quarter window (roofline unchanged from 964) — _roofline carried over_ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/4.-Generation-Porsche-911,-(993),-1993-1998.html), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250540&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=eedee361158555dc))
  - **drip_rails**: rain channels along the roof edges retained, running back toward the tail ('flyline') — _visual; Porsche describes the rain-channel 'flyline' as a 911 DNA feature (generic, not 993-specific)_ ([porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250540&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=eedee361158555dc), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2019/history/porsche-klassik-911-eight-generations-dna-18914.html))
  - **roofline**: practically unchanged from 964 – 'the only thing to remain unchanged' ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/4.-Generation-Porsche-911,-(993),-1993-1998.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-seven-generations-part-4-type-993-16486.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/30-Jahre-911-Carrera-der-Generation-993-%E2%80%93-Der-letzte-seiner-Art.html))
  - **fuel_filler**: _not found_ — _not found in: Porsche text (visual: flap on the front wing ahead of the door – verify)_
  - **windshield**: ~60° rake (per Porsche's 996 brochure comparison) ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/))
  - **chrome_trim**: essentially none (body-colour handles, mirrors and bumpers) — _visual + brochure (body-colour handles)_ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1993.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1994-1998-sales-brochures/), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250540&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=eedee361158555dc))
  - **side_repeaters**: orange side repeater on front wing behind the wheel arch — _visual_ ([porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250540&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=eedee361158555dc))
  - **rocker_sills**: _not found_ — _not found in: Porsche text (visual: body-colour sills)_
  - **other**: interplay of concave and convex surfaces; except the doors practically every sheet-metal part new (front wings, luggage lid, rear side panels, engine lid, bumpers) ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-seven-generations-part-4-type-993-16486.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/4.-Generation-Porsche-911,-(993),-1993-1998.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2019/history/porsche-klassik-911-eight-generations-dna-18914.html))
- **lighting_signature**: Raked, flatter poly-ellipsoid headlamps; orange indicators and fog lamps in the apron; angled full-width rear light strip with red reflective PORSCHE logo and orange indicators at the ends. ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/4.-Generation---Design-und-Modellvarianten.html), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/993/993/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/993-1993.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1994-1998-sales-brochures/))
- **distinctive**
