  - Wide hips / flared rear wings and flatter front wings; only the roofline carried over ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/4.-Generation-Porsche-911,-(993),-1993-1998.html), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/993/993/))
  - Last air-cooled 911 ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-seven-generations-part-4-type-993-16486.html))
  - Twin tailpipes, one each side ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/993/993/))
- **open_questions**
  - Porsche-official Cd for 993 Carrera (0.33 Excellence vs 0.34 de-Wikipedia) – none of the fetched Porsche pages/brochures state it.
  - Spoiler deploy speed only from de-Wikipedia (80 km/h); retract speed not found.
  - Litronic (xenon) option year not verified.
  - Official name of the standard 16-in 5-spoke wheel not verified (brochure only names the optional 17-in 'Carrera Cup 5-spoke').
  - Overhangs are photo estimates (±50 mm).

### 11-996-1-1998 — Porsche 911 Carrera (Type 996, first series '996.1') Coupé

- **blueprints**: `blueprints/11-996-1-1998/` — primary: `tbp-765_porsche-911-carrera-4-1998_4view.gif` — Excellent: detailed 4-view drawing of a 996.1 Carrera 4 (1998) + vendor 4-view with dims + Cabriolet 4-view.
- **production_years**: 1997–2001 for the pre-facelift Carrera (IAA Frankfurt 1997 debut, MY1998–MY2001); 996 generation overall 1997–2005, 175,262 built — _Porsche: 'The 996 generation was reworked in the 2002 model year' (new headlights etc.). European MY1998 start (Porsche: '911 Carrera Coupé of model year 1998'); in the US the car was sold from MY1999 (PCNA: 'introduced for model-year 1999')._ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-25-years-911-generation-996-30384.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/5.-Generation-Porsche-911,-(996),-1997-2005.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-seven-generations-part-5-typ-996-16487.html))
- **reference_variant**: 911 Carrera Coupé (996) MY1998–2001, 3.4 l 300 PS, rear-wheel drive, standard 17-in wheels, spoiler retracted — _Carrera 4 (from MY1999) body identical, but white front/side indicators and white-grey rear indicators, titanium 'Carrera 4' script (Porsche Classic)._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/996/996/))
- **dimensions_mm**
  - **length**: 4430 — _en-Wikipedia 4,432._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/), [auto-motor-und-sport.de](https://www.auto-motor-und-sport.de/marken-modelle/porsche/911/996/technische-daten/), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/57), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_996))
  - **width**: 1765 ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/), [auto-motor-und-sport.de](https://www.auto-motor-und-sport.de/marken-modelle/porsche/911/996/technische-daten/), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/57))
  - **height**: 1305 — _de-Wikipedia 1295; en-Wikipedia 1,306._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/), [auto-motor-und-sport.de](https://www.auto-motor-und-sport.de/marken-modelle/porsche/911/996/technische-daten/), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/57))
  - **wheelbase**: 2350 — _en-Wikipedia 2,352. Porsche: wheelbase +80 mm vs 993._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/), [auto-motor-und-sport.de](https://www.auto-motor-und-sport.de/marken-modelle/porsche/911/996/technische-daten/), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/57), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_996))
  - **track_front**: 1455 — _with 17-in wheels_ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/), [auto-motor-und-sport.de](https://www.auto-motor-und-sport.de/marken-modelle/porsche/911/996/technische-daten/), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/57), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_996))
  - **track_rear**: 1500 — _with 17-in wheels_ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/), [auto-motor-und-sport.de](https://www.auto-motor-und-sport.de/marken-modelle/porsche/911/996/technische-daten/), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/57), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_996))
  - **ground_clearance**: 100 — _US brochure 'Min. Ground Clearance 3.9 in (100 mm)'; Excellence 4.33 in/110 mm._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/))
- **overhang_mm**
  - **front**: 1008 — _ESTIMATE (±50 mm). Porsche studio side profile (press photo id 250544): nose x=161, front hub x=417, rear hub x=1036, tail x=1308 → ratio 256:272 applied to 4430 − 2350 = 2080 mm. Photo WB/L 0.540 vs true 0.530._ ([porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250544&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=186184ec88388985), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/))
  - **rear**: 1072 — _ESTIMATE (±50 mm), same method._ ([porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250544&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=186184ec88388985), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/))
- **other_published**
  - **curb_weight_kg**: 1320 — _US brochure 2910 lb (manual) / 3010 lb Tiptronic; de-Wikipedia 1320 kg._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20150324053349/http://press.porsche.com/archive/products/press_kits/press_kits_2002/PDF/34-35_CarreraFastFacts.pdf))
  - **drag_cd**: 0.3 ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/5.-Generation---Design-und-Modellvarianten.html), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/57))
  - **windshield_rake_deg**: 55 — _US brochure: 'the windshield has been reprofiled, raked more steeply at 55 degrees versus 60'._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/))
- **wheels**
  - **tyre_front**: 205/50 ZR 17 ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/), [auto-motor-und-sport.de](https://www.auto-motor-und-sport.de/marken-modelle/porsche/911/996/technische-daten/), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/57))
  - **tyre_rear**: 255/40 ZR 17 ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/), [auto-motor-und-sport.de](https://www.auto-motor-und-sport.de/marken-modelle/porsche/911/996/technische-daten/), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/57))
  - **rim_front**: 7J x 17 ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/), [auto-motor-und-sport.de](https://www.auto-motor-und-sport.de/marken-modelle/porsche/911/996/technische-daten/))
  - **rim_rear**: 9J x 17 ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/), [auto-motor-und-sport.de](https://www.auto-motor-und-sport.de/marken-modelle/porsche/911/996/technische-daten/))
  - **rim_diameter_in**: 17 ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/))
  - **design_name**: 17-in 5-spoke cast-alloy wheel with curved (twisted) spokes – exact Porsche name not confirmed — _Brochure wheel close-up and press profile show 5 curved spokes, 5 lug bolts, crest cap. PCNA: the 2002 facelift introduced 'new-design Carrera II 17-inch alloy wheels' (suggesting the 996.1 wheel was the first 'Carrera' design – inference), and 2001 Carrera 4 models had 'a different wheel design than the standard 911 Carrera'._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250544&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=186184ec88388985), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20150324053349/http://press.porsche.com/archive/products/press_kits/press_kits_2002/PDF/34-35_CarreraFastFacts.pdf), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20120916103006/http://press.porsche.com/archive/products/press_kits/press_kits_2001/PDF/1-4_FULL_LINE_RELEASE.pdf))
  - **spoke_count**: 5 — _visual_ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250544&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=186184ec88388985))
  - **finish**: silver painted — _visual_ ([porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250544&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=186184ec88388985))
  - **hub_cap_center**: Porsche crest centre cap (hub covers carried over from the 993) ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/5.-Generation-Porsche-911,-(996),-1997-2005.html), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/))
  - **options_notes**: Optional 18-in cast alloy 7.5J x 18 (225/40 ZR18) front / 10J x 18 (265/35 ZR18) rear ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/))
- **headlights**
  - **shape**: teardrop 'fried egg' headlamp units (so called for the amber indicator section), shared with the Boxster 986 ([en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(996)), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_996), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/996/996/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-25-years-911-generation-996-30384.html))
  - **surround_ring**: _not found_ — _not found in: Porsche text_
  - **mounting**: low-angle units laid flat into bonnet and wing contours ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_996))
  - **lens_technology**: single teardrop module with five functions: low beam, high beam, indicator, side/parking light and fog light (visual: big round main lens upper-outer, amber indicator + small round lamp in the lower inner 'tail') — _Lagaaij: 'five functions in a single module'; US brochure: 'the headlights integrate fog lights, turn indicators and optional headlight washers'._ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-25-years-911-generation-996-30384.html), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_996), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/))
  - **washers**: optional headlight washers ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/))
  - **other**: identical to Boxster (bonnet, headlight units, front wings, doors shared); Porsche later describes them as the 'oval' headlights the 997 replaced with round ones ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/996/996/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2019/history/porsche-klassik-911-eight-generations-dna-18914.html))
- **front**
  - **bumper_type**: integrated deformable plastic front panel with sculpted air dam ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/996/996/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/))
  - **overriders**: none ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/996/996/))
  - **indicators**: in the headlamp unit (amber on Carrera; white on Carrera 4) ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/5.-Generation---Design-und-Modellvarianten.html), [porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/996/996-4/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/))
  - **fog_lights**: in the headlamp unit (not in the bumper) ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_996))
  - **horn_grilles**: none visible — _visual only_ ([porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250559&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=cb2b16d5dd5afa94))
  - **air_intakes**: sculpted front air dam feeding twin radiators (air exits through the front wheel wells); visual: three openings (centre + two sides) — _layout detail visual_ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250559&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=cb2b16d5dd5afa94))
  - **front_lid_hood**: front lid shared with Boxster ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/996/996/))
  - **badge_crest**: Porsche crest on the bonnet (one of the few parts kept from the 993) ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/5.-Generation-Porsche-911,-(996),-1997-2005.html))
  - **other**: _not found_ — _no additional feature recorded_
- **rear**
  - **taillights**: separate wrap-around tail-light units (no continuous light band); amber indicator, red tail/brake and clear reversing segments — _de-Wikipedia: loss of the 'durchgehendes Leuchtband' was criticised (band returned on 4S). Segment colours visual from brochure photo._ ([de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_996), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/))
  - **reflector_band**: none on Carrera (no full-width band) ([de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_996))
  - **bumper**: integrated deformable plastic rear panel ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/996/996/))
  - **exhaust**: two oval tailpipes, one below each end of the rear bumper (dual exhaust) — _visual: PCNA brochure rear-corner close-up (oval pipe, right side) and Porsche Museum 996.1 Carrera 4 'Millennium' rear photo (one pipe each side; C4 pipes differ in shape per Porsche Heritage data sheet https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/Porsche-Heritage-Experience/Porsche-911-(Typ-996)-Carrera-4-Cabriolet.html). Brochure text: 'Twin monitors in the dual exhaust'. PCNA says the 2002 facelift brought 'redesigned oval exhaust tailpipes' (https://web.archive.org/web/20150324053349/http://press.porsche.com/archive/products/press_kits/press_kits_2002/PDF/34-35_CarreraFastFacts.pdf)._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/e4f3b58a-04a8-409e-94c1-1de81d94744a.zip), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-25-years-911-generation-996-30384.html))
  - **license_plate**: centred in a recess in the rear bumper, below the tail-lamp line — _visual (Porsche Museum 996.1 C4 Millennium rear photo)_ ([pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/e4f3b58a-04a8-409e-94c1-1de81d94744a.zip))
  - **badging**: 'Carrera' script on the rear lid — _visual (brochure photo)_ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/))
  - **other**: rear lamps wrap around the rear-wing corners; lower edge of lamps roughly level with the top of the bumper — _visual_ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/e4f3b58a-04a8-409e-94c1-1de81d94744a.zip))
- **rear_spoiler**
  - **type**: automatically extending rear spoiler in the rear lid; flush when retracted ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/996/996/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/))
  - **deploy**: extends above ~75 mph (~120 km/h) (US brochure: 'Elevating above 75 mph') — _European km/h figure and retract speed not found from a primary source; 120 km/h is only the unit conversion._ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/))
- **engine_lid_grille**
  - **design**: black horizontal-slat grille across the top of the rear lid (the extending spoiler surface) with a red third-brake-light strip just above it, under the rear window — _visual (PCNA brochure spoiler photos; Porsche Museum Millennium rear/top photos)_ ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/e4f3b58a-04a8-409e-94c1-1de81d94744a.zip))
- **body**
  - **fender_flares**: new, longer and wider body; narrow-body Carrera (Turbo/C4S wider) ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/5.-Generation-Porsche-911,-(996),-1997-2005.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-seven-generations-part-5-typ-996-16487.html))
  - **rear_arch_width**: overall width 1765 mm (+30 mm vs 993) ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/5.-Generation-Porsche-911,-(996),-1997-2005.html))
  - **mirrors**: body-colour oval mirror heads on a black triangular base at the front corner of the side window (A-pillar apex) – 'no longer on the doors' — _Porsche Classic: 'fixed in front of the side windows and no longer on the doors'; PCNA: 'repositioned at the apex of the A-pillar'. Shape visual._ ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/996/996/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250558&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=974aa611c105be6e))
  - **door_handles**: body-colour pull handle set in a recessed dish near the rear upper corner of the door — _visual only_ ([porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250544&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=186184ec88388985), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250558&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=974aa611c105be6e))
  - **side_windows**: no division bar in the door glass (mirror sits on a black triangle ahead of it); black window surrounds — _visual only_ ([porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250544&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=186184ec88388985), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250558&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=974aa611c105be6e))
  - **quarter_window**: fixed rear side window tapering to a rounded point toward the C-pillar — _visual only_ ([porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250544&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=186184ec88388985))
  - **drip_rails**: _not found_ — _not found in: Porsche text_
  - **roofline**: classic 911 profile and C-pillar preserved ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/))
  - **fuel_filler**: _not found_ — _not found in: Porsche text_
  - **windshield**: more steeply raked, 55° (vs 60° on 993) ([stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(996)))
  - **chrome_trim**: _not found_ — _not found in: Porsche text_
  - **side_repeaters**: elongated side indicator low on the front wing just behind the wheel arch (white on Carrera 4) — _position visual_ ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/996/996-4/), [porschepictures.flowcenter.de](https://porschepictures.flowcenter.de/pmdb/thumbnail.cgi?id=250559&w=1440&h=812&crop=1&public=1&cs=cb2b16d5dd5afa94))
  - **rocker_sills**: _not found_ — _not found in: Porsche text_
  - **other**: first water-cooled 911; 185 mm longer, wheelbase +80 mm, 30 mm wider than 993; 'provocative organic elements', 'very soft look' ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-seven-generations-part-5-typ-996-16487.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/5.-Generation-Porsche-911,-(996),-1997-2005.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2019/history/porsche-klassik-911-eight-generations-dna-18914.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/products/porsche-911-design-production-anniversary-13742.html))
- **lighting_signature**: Teardrop 'fried-egg' headlamps with amber indicator lobe below the main lens; separate wrap-around rear lamps (no full-width band). ([en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(996)), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_996), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/))
- **distinctive**
  - Front end (bonnet, headlamps, wings, doors) shared with Boxster 986 ([porsche.com](https://www.porsche.com/international/accessoriesandservice/classic/models/996/996/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-25-years-911-generation-996-30384.html))
  - Cd 0.30; windshield 55° ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/5.-Generation---Design-und-Modellvarianten.html), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/996-1997-1.pdf), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-1998-2005-sales-brochures/))
- **open_questions**
  - European spoiler deploy/retract speeds (only US 'above 75 mph' found).
  - Official name of the standard 17-in wheel (PCNA calls the 2002 replacement 'Carrera II').
  - Drip rails / door-glass framing / fuel-filler position not verified.
  - Overhangs are photo estimates (±50 mm).

### 12-996-2-2002 — 996.2 Carrera Coupé (facelift, 3.6 l)

- **blueprints**: `blueprints/12-996-2-2002/` — primary: `tbp-68297_porsche-911-carrera-4s-2003_3view-colour.png` — Only a colour 3-view of the Carrera 4S (wide body) and cutaways found for the 996.2; no top view -> use the 996.1 top view (../11) with the 996.2 front/rear.
- **production_years**: Market launch September 2001 as model year 2002 ("second generation Carrera in the 996 series"); US spec sheets list MY2002-2004 as 996 v2; the 997 Carrera replaced it from 17 July 2004 (Europe) ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20010801160045/http://www.porsche.com:80/english/news/pressreleases/010529.htm), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/63), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/67), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20040807201106/http://www2.porsche.com:80/english/int/news/pressreleases/pag/2004-07-16-1.htm))
- **reference_variant**: 911 Carrera Coupé (rear-drive, narrow body), MY2002-2004, 3.6 l M96/03 320 hp; RoW/US official spec page (PCNA 2003) used for dimensions ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20030724115326/http://www3.porsche.com:80/english/usa/911/c2/technicalspecifications/default.htm), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20010801160045/http://www.porsche.com:80/english/news/pressreleases/010529.htm))
- **dimensions_mm**
  - **length**: 4430 — _Unchanged vs 996.1 (4,430). Porsche's 2001 release mentions an 'extended rear end panel' but overall length did not change._ ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20160706001137/http://press.porsche.com:80/archive/products/press_kits/press_kits_2004/PDF/new/12-13_Carrera_Specs.pdf), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20150324053404/http://press.porsche.com:80/archive/products/press_kits/press_kits_2003/PDF/46-47_911_car_cp_cabspecs.pdf), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20030724115326/http://www3.porsche.com:80/english/usa/911/c2/technicalspecifications/default.htm), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/63))
  - **width**: 1770 — _excl. mirrors. 996.1: 1,765 (EXC57) -> +5 mm ('widened rear quarter panels')._ ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20160706001137/http://press.porsche.com:80/archive/products/press_kits/press_kits_2004/PDF/new/12-13_Carrera_Specs.pdf), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20150324053404/http://press.porsche.com:80/archive/products/press_kits/press_kits_2003/PDF/46-47_911_car_cp_cabspecs.pdf), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20030724115326/http://www3.porsche.com:80/english/usa/911/c2/technicalspecifications/default.htm), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/63))
  - **height**: 1305 ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20160706001137/http://press.porsche.com:80/archive/products/press_kits/press_kits_2004/PDF/new/12-13_Carrera_Specs.pdf), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20030724115326/http://www3.porsche.com:80/english/usa/911/c2/technicalspecifications/default.htm), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/63))
  - **wheelbase**: 2350 ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20160706001137/http://press.porsche.com:80/archive/products/press_kits/press_kits_2004/PDF/new/12-13_Carrera_Specs.pdf), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20030724115326/http://www3.porsche.com:80/english/usa/911/c2/technicalspecifications/default.htm), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/63))
  - **track_front**: 1465 — _same with optional 18-inch wheels; 996.1: 1,455 (EXC57)_ ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20160706001137/http://press.porsche.com:80/archive/products/press_kits/press_kits_2004/PDF/new/12-13_Carrera_Specs.pdf), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20150324053404/http://press.porsche.com:80/archive/products/press_kits/press_kits_2003/PDF/46-47_911_car_cp_cabspecs.pdf), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/63))
  - **track_rear**: 1500 — _1,480 with optional 18-inch wheels (18x10)_ ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20160706001137/http://press.porsche.com:80/archive/products/press_kits/press_kits_2004/PDF/new/12-13_Carrera_Specs.pdf), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20150324053404/http://press.porsche.com:80/archive/products/press_kits/press_kits_2003/PDF/46-47_911_car_cp_cabspecs.pdf), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/63))
  - **ground_clearance**: 110 — _'Ground clearance (laden)' per PCNA spec sheets_ ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20160706001137/http://press.porsche.com:80/archive/products/press_kits/press_kits_2004/PDF/new/12-13_Carrera_Specs.pdf), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20150324053404/http://press.porsche.com:80/archive/products/press_kits/press_kits_2003/PDF/46-47_911_car_cp_cabspecs.pdf), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/63))
- **overhang_mm**
  - **front**: _not found_ — _not published in fetched sources; length - wheelbase = 2,080 mm total overhang (4,430 - 2,350). Split must be measured from a scaled side-view blueprint._
  - **rear**: _not found_ — _see front; total front+rear overhang = 2,080 mm_
- **other_published**
  - **curb_weight_kg**: 1370 — _DIN unladen, manual (Tiptronic 1,425; EC 1,445/1,500 incl. 75 kg driver+luggage). Excellence: 2,959 lb manual (US)._ ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20030724115326/http://www3.porsche.com:80/english/usa/911/c2/technicalspecifications/default.htm))
  - **drag_cd**: 0.3 — _the 2003 PCNA sheet prints 0.34 (likely typo/cabriolet); 2004 sheet 0.30_ ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20160706001137/http://press.porsche.com:80/archive/products/press_kits/press_kits_2004/PDF/new/12-13_Carrera_Specs.pdf), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/63))
  - **windshield_rake_deg**: _not found_ — _not found in: PCNA/Porsche AG spec pages, press releases 2001, Excellence spec sheet_
  - **turning_circle_m**: 10.6 — _34.8 ft_ ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2002-911_carrera/), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2003-911_carrera/))
  - **weight_distribution**: 36 % front / 64 % rear (Coupé) ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20160706001137/http://press.porsche.com:80/archive/products/press_kits/press_kits_2004/PDF/new/12-13_Carrera_Specs.pdf), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20150324053404/http://press.porsche.com:80/archive/products/press_kits/press_kits_2003/PDF/46-47_911_car_cp_cabspecs.pdf))
- **wheels**
  - **tyre_front**: 205/50 ZR 17 ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20030724115326/http://www3.porsche.com:80/english/usa/911/c2/technicalspecifications/default.htm), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2002-911_carrera/), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/63))
  - **tyre_rear**: 255/40 ZR 17 ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20030724115326/http://www3.porsche.com:80/english/usa/911/c2/technicalspecifications/default.htm), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2002-911_carrera/), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/63))
  - **rim_front**: 7 J x 17 ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20030724115326/http://www3.porsche.com:80/english/usa/911/c2/technicalspecifications/default.htm), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2002-911_carrera/))
  - **rim_rear**: 9 J x 17 ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20030724115326/http://www3.porsche.com:80/english/usa/911/c2/technicalspecifications/default.htm), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2002-911_carrera/))
  - **rim_diameter_in**: 17 — _Optional 18-inch package: 8Jx18 225/40 ZR18 front, 10Jx18 285/30 ZR18 rear (NCS2002)._ ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20030724115326/http://www3.porsche.com:80/english/usa/911/c2/technicalspecifications/default.htm))
  - **design_name**: 'Carrera II' pressure-cast aluminium-alloy wheel, new for MY2002 ('slender spoke design'), standard 17-inch; optional 18-inch package 18x8 / 18x10 with 225/40 ZR18 / 285/30 ZR18 ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20160706001137/http://press.porsche.com:80/archive/products/press_kits/press_kits_2004/PDF/new/12-13_Carrera_Specs.pdf), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20150324053404/http://press.porsche.com:80/archive/products/press_kits/press_kits_2003/PDF/46-47_911_car_cp_cabspecs.pdf), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20150324053349/http://press.porsche.com:80/archive/products/press_kits/press_kits_2002/PDF/34-35_CarreraFastFacts.pdf), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2002-911_carrera/))
  - **spoke_count**: 5 — _counted on Porsche press photos of the 2002 Carrera (silver 5-spoke with curved/tapered spokes, crest centre cap)_ ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2002-1280-cd535fce152a4eabc3eacd6520e08c9628.jpg), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2002-Rear_Three-Quarter.a8496a1a.jpg))
  - **finish**: silver-painted alloy (press photos) ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2002-1280-cd535fce152a4eabc3eacd6520e08c9628.jpg), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2002-Rear_Three-Quarter.a8496a1a.jpg))
  - **hub_cap_center**: coloured Porsche crest centre cap (visible on press photos) ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2002-Rear_Three-Quarter.a8496a1a.jpg))
  - **options_notes**: 18-inch option wheels 18x8 / 18x10 (0.5 in wider front than 2001 option); unsprung weight -5 lb per wheel vs previous 18-inch design ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2002-911_carrera/))
- **headlights**
  - **shape**: 911 Turbo (996) headlamp adopted for all Carreras: 'teardrop' unit 'rounded off at the bottom' (Harm Lagaay calls it an S-shape at the bottom) -- replaces the Boxster-shared 996.1 'fried-egg' unit; one combined module (low/high beam, indicator, side light) ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/5.-Generation-Porsche-911,-(996),-1997-2005.html), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20010801160045/http://www.porsche.com:80/english/news/pressreleases/010529.htm), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2002-911_carrera/), [elferspot.com](https://www.elferspot.com/en/magazine/porsche-996-1-and-996-2-carrera-in-comparison-what-are-the-differences/), [porscheclubgb.com](https://www.porscheclubgb.com/regions-registers/registers/classic/996/porsche-911-(996)-buyers-guide), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2002-1280-cd535fce152a4eabc3eacd6520e08c9628.jpg))
  - **surround_ring**: no separate chrome ring; clear-glass cover following the teardrop outline (press photo) — _visual observation_ ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2002-1280-cd535fce152a4eabc3eacd6520e08c9628.jpg))
  - **mounting**: sloping, faired into the front wing/lid shut-line (not upright like the air-cooled cars) — _visual observation_ ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2002-1280-cd535fce152a4eabc3eacd6520e08c9628.jpg))
  - **lens_technology**: halogen standard; Bi-Xenon (low + high beam gas-discharge, as on 911 Turbo) new option for MY2002 ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2002-911_carrera/))
  - **washers**: _not found_ — _not found in: fetched 2001/2002 releases and spec pages_
  - **other**: Indicator lenses: MY1998 996 had orange/amber indicator lenses ('fried eggs'), clear/smoked lenses from MY1999 (standard halogen, optional xenon) -- so the 996.2 carries clear/smoked lenses; the 996 headlamp concept = 'five functions in a single module' (Porsche/Lagaaij) ([elferspot.com](https://www.elferspot.com/en/magazine/porsche-996-1-and-996-2-carrera-in-comparison-what-are-the-differences/), [porscheclubgb.com](https://www.porscheclubgb.com/regions-registers/registers/classic/996/porsche-911-(996)-buyers-guide), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-996-the-story/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-25-years-911-generation-996-30384.html))
- **front**
  - **bumper_type**: integrated body-colour plastic front apron, 'completely reshaped front' for MY2002 with new air intakes (+15 % radiator airflow); reshaped front wheel-arch radii and small flexible spoilers ahead of the front wheels ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20010801160045/http://www.porsche.com:80/english/news/pressreleases/010529.htm), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2002-911_carrera/))
  - **overriders**: none — _visual_ ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2002-1280-cd535fce152a4eabc3eacd6520e08c9628.jpg))
  - **indicators**: in the headlight unit (Turbo-type headlamp); amber side marker/repeater in the front wing ahead of the door on US cars (photo) — _side-marker observation from PCNA press photo_ ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2002-911_carrera/), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2002-1280-cd535fce152a4eabc3eacd6520e08c9628.jpg))
  - **fog_lights**: _not found_ — _not found in: PCNA 2002-2004 spec sheets/releases, Porsche AG 2001 releases, Elferspot/PCGB/PCA guides. Porsche describes the 996 headlamp as 'five functions in a single module' (NEWS_996_25), which suggests the fog lamp sits in the headlamp unit -- unconfirmed._
  - **horn_grilles**: none (no separate horn grilles; three apron openings) — _visual_ ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2002-1280-cd535fce152a4eabc3eacd6520e08c9628.jpg))
  - **air_intakes**: three openings: wide centre intake plus left/right radiator intakes, each outer intake split by a horizontal slat (press photo) ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2002-1280-cd535fce152a4eabc3eacd6520e08c9628.jpg), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2002-911_carrera/))
  - **front_lid_hood**: front lid with crest at leading edge, lid shut-line runs between headlights (photo) ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2002-1280-cd535fce152a4eabc3eacd6520e08c9628.jpg))
  - **badge_crest**: Porsche crest on front lid (photo) ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2002-1280-cd535fce152a4eabc3eacd6520e08c9628.jpg))
  - **other**: None
- **rear**
  - **taillights**: Horizontal wedge-shaped units in the rear wings either side of the engine lid; red lower band with smoked/clear upper indicator/reverse section (smoked rear indicator lenses since MY1999) ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2002-Rear_Three-Quarter.a8496a1a.jpg), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2002-1280-7e0d9b1b4cb23a26b7955b9560b5431e0e.jpg), [porscheclubgb.com](https://www.porscheclubgb.com/regions-registers/registers/classic/996/porsche-911-(996)-buyers-guide))
  - **reflector_band**: none on Carrera Coupé (red lamp strip connecting the rear lights is a Carrera 4S identifier) ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20011013232017/http://www.porsche.com:80/english/news/pressreleases/010903.htm), [pca.org](https://www.pca.org/news/model-guide-the-996-generation-911-part-i))
  - **bumper**: Integrated body-colour rear apron, 'extended rear end panel' for MY2002: instead of the 996.1's continuous round surface with a fine notch at plate height, a raised section runs from wheel arch to wheel arch below the licence-plate recess like a brace; design based on the 996 Turbo apron (which the C4S uses with side vents deleted) ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20010801160045/http://www.porsche.com:80/english/news/pressreleases/010529.htm), [elferspot.com](https://www.elferspot.com/en/magazine/porsche-996-1-and-996-2-carrera-in-comparison-what-are-the-differences/), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2002-Rear_Three-Quarter.a8496a1a.jpg))
  - **exhaust**: two oval tailpipes, one at each outer corner of the rear apron (redesigned oval exhaust tailpipes for MY2002) ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20010801160045/http://www.porsche.com:80/english/news/pressreleases/010529.htm), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2002-911_carrera/), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2002-Rear_Three-Quarter.a8496a1a.jpg))
  - **license_plate**: recess in the centre of the rear apron (photo) ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2002-Rear_Three-Quarter.a8496a1a.jpg))
  - **badging**: 'Carrera' script on engine lid (photo) ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2002-Rear_Three-Quarter.a8496a1a.jpg))
  - **other**: third brake light at top of engine lid below rear window (photo) ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2002-Rear_Three-Quarter.a8496a1a.jpg))
- **rear_spoiler**
  - **type**: electrically extending rear spoiler integrated in the engine lid with grille-work; engine breathes through ducts under the spoiler (air flows through the spoiler grille when lowered); C4S keeps this Carrera spoiler instead of the Turbo's two-piece wing ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2001-911_carrera_cabriolet/), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2002-911_carrera_4s/), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2002-Rear_Three-Quarter.a8496a1a.jpg))
  - **deploy**: raises at 120 km/h (75 mph), retracts below 80 km/h (50 mph); dashboard switch to raise manually — _From the PCNA MY2001 (996.1) Carrera Cabriolet text; MY2002 996.2 not separately confirmed (system carried over per NCS2002C4S wording 'automatic-deploying rear spoiler used on other 911 Carrera models')._ ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2001-911_carrera_cabriolet/))
- **engine_lid_grille**
  - **design**: fine horizontal louvre grille in the rear lid/spoiler area below the rear window, third brake light at its top edge (photo); engine air enters through ducts under the spoiler and the spoiler grille-work ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2002-Rear_Three-Quarter.a8496a1a.jpg), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2001-911_carrera_cabriolet/))
- **body**
  - **fender_flares**: narrow Carrera body; 'widened rear quarter panels' for MY2002 (width 1,770 vs 1,765) ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2002-911_carrera/), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/63), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/57))
  - **rear_arch_width**: narrow body (C4S uses 911 Turbo wide body: width 1,830, 60 mm wider rear than Carrera 4) ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20030724115326/http://www3.porsche.com:80/english/usa/911/c2/technicalspecifications/default.htm), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20030805042711/http://www3.porsche.com:80/english/int/911/c4s/technicalspecifications/default.htm), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20011013232017/http://www.porsche.com:80/english/news/pressreleases/010903.htm))
  - **mirrors**: door-mounted single-stalk mirrors, body colour (photo) — _visual_ ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2002-1280-cd535fce152a4eabc3eacd6520e08c9628.jpg))
  - **door_handles**: body-colour pull handles on doors (photo) ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2002-Rear_Three-Quarter.a8496a1a.jpg))
  - **side_windows**: frameless door glass + fixed rear quarter window (photo) ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2002-Rear_Three-Quarter.a8496a1a.jpg))
  - **quarter_window**: rear quarter window with rounded rear edge (photo) ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2002-Rear_Three-Quarter.a8496a1a.jpg))
  - **drip_rails**: none visible (photo) ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2002-Rear_Three-Quarter.a8496a1a.jpg))
  - **roofline**: unchanged vs 996.1 (roof unchanged through 997 too per Porsche: 'Apart from the roof, every visible body part of the 997 differed') ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/6.-Generation-Porsche-911,-(997),-2004-012.html))
  - **fuel_filler**: _not found_ — _not found in fetched 996.2 sources (997 owner's manuals place it in the right front wing; 996 not confirmed)_
  - **windshield**: 996 platform introduced 'a more raked windshield' than the 993 (no angle published in fetched sources) ([en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Porsche_911_(996)&action=raw))
  - **chrome_trim**: essentially none; black window surrounds (photo) ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2002-1280-cd535fce152a4eabc3eacd6520e08c9628.jpg))
  - **side_repeaters**: side marker at front of front wing (US photo) ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2002-1280-cd535fce152a4eabc3eacd6520e08c9628.jpg))
  - **rocker_sills**: plain body-colour sills (photo) ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2002-Rear_Three-Quarter.a8496a1a.jpg))
  - **other**: None
- **lighting_signature**: Turbo-style clear headlamps with S-curved/rounded lower edge; horizontal red/clear tail lamps; no rear light bar on Carrera ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/5.-Generation-Porsche-911,-(996),-1997-2005.html), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2002-1280-cd535fce152a4eabc3eacd6520e08c9628.jpg), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2002-Rear_Three-Quarter.a8496a1a.jpg))
- **distinctive**
  - First facelift to separate the 911 face from the Boxster: Turbo headlamps + new front apron ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/5.-Generation-Porsche-911,-(996),-1997-2005.html), [pca.org](https://www.pca.org/news/model-guide-the-996-generation-911-part-i))
  - Design: Pinky Lai under design chief Harm Lagaay ([en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Porsche_911_(996)&action=raw), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2022/history/porsche-25-years-911-generation-996-30384.html))
  - Carrera 4S (MY2002): Turbo body shell 60 mm wider at the rear (width 1,830, length 4,435, height 1,295, tracks 1,472/1,528), 18-inch 'Turbo-look hollow-spoke' wheels 8J/11J with 225/40 & 295/30 ZR18 and red calipers, three large dark-finish front intake grilles, own front spoiler with slightly raised centre, Turbo lower rear-quarter louvres retained but Turbo side intakes deleted, own GRP decklid with Carrera auto spoiler, exclusive (red) reflector strip connecting the tail lights, Cd 0.30 ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2002-911_carrera_4s/), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20151004064215/http://press.porsche.com/archive/products/press_kits/press_kits_2002/PDF/13-14_911C4SSpecs.pdf), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20011013232017/http://www.porsche.com:80/english/news/pressreleases/010903.htm), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20030805042711/http://www3.porsche.com:80/english/int/911/c4s/technicalspecifications/default.htm), [pca.org](https://www.pca.org/news/model-guide-the-996-generation-911-part-i))
- **open_questions**
  - Fog-lamp location on 996.2 (probably inside the Turbo-style headlamp module -- unconfirmed)
  - Fuel-filler flap position on 996 (not found)
  - Rear-spoiler speeds taken from the MY2001 PCNA text (120 km/h up / 80 km/h down); MY2002+ not separately confirmed
  - Front/rear overhang split not published (length - wheelbase = 2,080 mm total) -- measure on a scaled blueprint
  - Windshield rake angle not published in any fetched source

### 13-997-1-2005 — 997.1 Carrera Coupé (3.6 l)

- **blueprints**: `blueprints/13-997-1-2005/` — primary: `vec-1030_porsche-911-carrera-2-997_4view-dims-watermarked.jpg` — Vendor 4-view (997.1 front) + 997.1 C4S Cabriolet 4-view + cutaway and side line drawings.
- **production_years**: Press presentation June 2004; European market launch 17 July 2004 (MY2005); 997 series built 2004-2012 (213,004 cars); first-gen Carrera until the July 2008 update ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20040807201106/http://www2.porsche.com:80/english/int/news/pressreleases/pag/2004-07-16-1.htm), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20040614021122/http://www2.porsche.com:80/english/int/news/pressreleases/pag/2004-05-12-2.htm), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/6.-Generation-Porsche-911,-(997),-2004-012.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-seven-generations-part-6-type-997-16489.html))
- **reference_variant**: 911 Carrera Coupé (3.6 l, 325 PS), European/UK spec, MY2005-2008 (dimensions identical in porsche.com 2007 and UK catalogue MY08) ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071130055840/http://www.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY08_PCGB.pdf), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20070618195353/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/featuresandspecs/))
- **dimensions_mm**
  - **length**: 4427 — _Porsche AG UK catalogue MY08 and porsche.com: 4,427; Porsche Japan owner's manual (5/05): 4,425; US-spec sheets 4,461 (EXC96) with US bumpers/plate. Recommend 4,427. PCNA 2005 spec sheet confirms US 4,461._ ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071130055840/http://www.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY08_PCGB.pdf), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20070618195353/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/featuresandspecs/), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071203210816/http://www.porsche.com/filestore.aspx/default.pdf?pool=japan&type=download&id=download-911-all&lang=jp&filetype=default), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20150122124455/http://press.porsche.com:80/archive/products/press_kits/press_kits_2005/pdf/carrera_carreras/911_carrera_specs.pdf))
  - **width**: 1808 — _excl. mirrors (JP manual rounds to 1,810); C4/C4S/Targa 4: 1,852 (44 mm wider across the rear arches)_ ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071130055840/http://www.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY08_PCGB.pdf), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20070618195353/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/featuresandspecs/), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2005-911_carrera/), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071203210816/http://www.porsche.com/filestore.aspx/default.pdf?pool=japan&type=download&id=download-911-all&lang=jp&filetype=default))
  - **height**: 1310 — _Carrera S 1,300 (PASM, -10 mm); sports running gear 1,290_ ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071130055840/http://www.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY08_PCGB.pdf), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20070618195353/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/featuresandspecs/), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071203210816/http://www.porsche.com/filestore.aspx/default.pdf?pool=japan&type=download&id=download-911-all&lang=jp&filetype=default))
  - **wheelbase**: 2350 ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20070618195353/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/featuresandspecs/), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2005-911_carrera/), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/96))
  - **track_front**: 1486 — _C4/C4S 1,488 (OM)_ ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2005-911_carrera/), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/96), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071203210816/http://www.porsche.com/filestore.aspx/default.pdf?pool=japan&type=download&id=download-911-all&lang=jp&filetype=default))
  - **track_rear**: 1534 — _Carrera S with 19-inch 11J ET67: 1,516; C4/C4S 1,548 (OM)_ ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2005-911_carrera/), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/96), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071203210816/http://www.porsche.com/filestore.aspx/default.pdf?pool=japan&type=download&id=download-911-all&lang=jp&filetype=default))
  - **ground_clearance**: 104 — _Porsche Japan manual: 104 mm at max. load (Carrera); PCNA 2005 spec sheet: 106 mm (Carrera), 96 mm (Carrera S); PASM -10 mm; sports running gear 88/92 (JP manual)_ ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071203210816/http://www.porsche.com/filestore.aspx/default.pdf?pool=japan&type=download&id=download-911-all&lang=jp&filetype=default), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20150122124455/http://press.porsche.com:80/archive/products/press_kits/press_kits_2005/pdf/carrera_carreras/911_carrera_specs.pdf))
- **overhang_mm**
  - **front**: _not found_ — _not published in fetched sources; length - wheelbase = 2,077 mm total (4,427 - 2,350)_
  - **rear**: _not found_ — _see front_
- **other_published**
  - **curb_weight_kg**: 1395 — _DIN unladen, manual (Tiptronic S 1,435; EC 1,470/1,510)_ ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20070618195353/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/featuresandspecs/))
  - **drag_cd**: 0.28 — _Carrera S 0.29; Tiptronic 0.29 (US)_ ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20070618195353/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/featuresandspecs/), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2005-911_carrera/))
  - **windshield_rake_deg**: _not found_ — _not found in: porsche.com spec pages, PCNA 2005 release, Porsche AG 2004 releases_
  - **turning_circle_m**: 10.9 ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071203210816/http://www.porsche.com/filestore.aspx/default.pdf?pool=japan&type=download&id=download-911-all&lang=jp&filetype=default))
- **wheels**
  - **tyre_front**: 235/40 ZR 18 (91Y) ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20040614021122/http://www2.porsche.com:80/english/int/news/pressreleases/pag/2004-05-12-2.htm), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2005-911_carrera/), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071203210816/http://www.porsche.com/filestore.aspx/default.pdf?pool=japan&type=download&id=download-911-all&lang=jp&filetype=default))
  - **tyre_rear**: 265/40 ZR 18 (101Y) XL ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20040614021122/http://www2.porsche.com:80/english/int/news/pressreleases/pag/2004-05-12-2.htm), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2005-911_carrera/), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071203210816/http://www.porsche.com/filestore.aspx/default.pdf?pool=japan&type=download&id=download-911-all&lang=jp&filetype=default))
  - **rim_front**: 8 J x 18 ET 57 ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071130055840/http://www.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY08_PCGB.pdf), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071203210816/http://www.porsche.com/filestore.aspx/default.pdf?pool=japan&type=download&id=download-911-all&lang=jp&filetype=default), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20040614021122/http://www2.porsche.com:80/english/int/news/pressreleases/pag/2004-05-12-2.htm))
  - **rim_rear**: 10 J x 18 ET 58 — _Excellence 2008 sheet (EXC102) and the MY2008 PCNA text say 11 in -- contradicted by the Porsche AG catalogue MY08, owner's manual and 2004 release. Carrera S: 8Jx19 ET57 / 11Jx19 ET67._ ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071130055840/http://www.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY08_PCGB.pdf), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071203210816/http://www.porsche.com/filestore.aspx/default.pdf?pool=japan&type=download&id=download-911-all&lang=jp&filetype=default), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20040614021122/http://www2.porsche.com:80/english/int/news/pressreleases/pag/2004-05-12-2.htm))
  - **rim_diameter_in**: 18 — _first 911 Carrera with standard 18-inch wheels (NCS2005). Carrera S: 8Jx19 235/35 ZR19 / 11Jx19 295/30 ZR19_ ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20040614021122/http://www2.porsche.com:80/english/int/news/pressreleases/pag/2004-05-12-2.htm))
  - **design_name**: '18-inch Carrera III wheel' (standard on 911 Carrera, five-spoke, flow-formed); Carrera S: '19-inch Carrera S wheel'; options 19-inch Carrera Classic, SportDesign, Carrera Sport, Turbo ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071130055840/http://www.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY08_PCGB.pdf), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2005-911_carrera/))
  - **spoke_count**: 5 ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2005-911_carrera/), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2005-Front_Three-Quarter.8e284e72.jpg))
  - **finish**: silver (press photo) ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2005-Front_Three-Quarter.8e284e72.jpg))
  - **hub_cap_center**: Porsche crest centre cap (photo) ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2005-Front_Three-Quarter.8e284e72.jpg))
  - **options_notes**: Carrera S standard 19-inch; PCCB 350 mm discs optional ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20040614021122/http://www2.porsche.com:80/english/int/news/pressreleases/pag/2004-05-12-2.htm), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2005-911_carrera/))
- **headlights**
  - **shape**: compact, oval clear-glass headlamp units, steeper, set into an arching front wing -- return to the classic round/oval 911 cue, replacing the 996 teardrop units ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071130055840/http://www.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY08_PCGB.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-seven-generations-part-6-type-997-16489.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/6.-Generation-Porsche-911,-(997),-2004-012.html), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2005-911_carrera/), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20040614021122/http://www2.porsche.com:80/english/int/news/pressreleases/pag/2004-05-12-2.htm), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(997)), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20150105235627/http://press.porsche.com:80/archive/products/press_kits/press_kits_2005/pdf/carrera_carreras/911_carrera_release.pdf))
  - **surround_ring**: body-colour wing surround; headlamp interior with separate projector/reflector (photo) — _visual_ ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2005-Front_Three-Quarter.8e284e72.jpg))
  - **mounting**: upright-ish in the wing crown, 'steeper' than 996 ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-seven-generations-part-6-type-997-16489.html), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2005-Front_Three-Quarter.8e284e72.jpg))
  - **lens_technology**: halogen (low H7 / high H9) standard on 3.6-litre cars, Bi-Xenon with dynamic range control and headlight cleaning standard on S/4S (option on Carrera) ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071130055840/http://www.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY08_PCGB.pdf), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071203210816/http://www.porsche.com/filestore.aspx/default.pdf?pool=japan&type=download&id=download-911-all&lang=jp&filetype=default), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2005-911_carrera/))
  - **washers**: headlight cleaning system fitted with Bi-Xenon (option P74 on 3.6 models); washer reservoir 2.5 l without / approx. 6 l with headlamp washers ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071130055840/http://www.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY08_PCGB.pdf), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071203210816/http://www.porsche.com/filestore.aspx/default.pdf?pool=japan&type=download&id=download-911-all&lang=jp&filetype=default))
  - **other**: separate turn indicators and fog lamps moved out of the headlamp into horizontal units in the front bumper ('separate additional headlamps in the nose area') ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20040614021122/http://www2.porsche.com:80/english/int/news/pressreleases/pag/2004-05-12-2.htm), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2005-911_carrera/))
- **front**
  - **bumper_type**: integrated body-colour front apron with redesigned air inlets ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2005-911_carrera/))
  - **overriders**: none ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2005-Front_Three-Quarter.8e284e72.jpg))
  - **indicators**: the parking (side) lights, fog lights and indicators are housed in two separate horizontal modules on the front apron moulding, above the outer intakes (bulbs: indicator PY21W, fog H8 35 W, side light W5W) ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071130055840/http://www.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY08_PCGB.pdf), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071203210816/http://www.porsche.com/filestore.aspx/default.pdf?pool=japan&type=download&id=download-911-all&lang=jp&filetype=default), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2005-911_carrera/), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2005-Front_Three-Quarter.8e284e72.jpg), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20150105235627/http://press.porsche.com:80/archive/products/press_kits/press_kits_2005/pdf/carrera_carreras/911_carrera_release.pdf))
  - **fog_lights**: standard on all models, in the separate front-apron modules ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071130055840/http://www.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY08_PCGB.pdf), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071203210816/http://www.porsche.com/filestore.aspx/default.pdf?pool=japan&type=download&id=download-911-all&lang=jp&filetype=default), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20150105235627/http://press.porsche.com:80/archive/products/press_kits/press_kits_2005/pdf/carrera_carreras/911_carrera_release.pdf))
  - **horn_grilles**: none ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2005-Front_Three-Quarter.8e284e72.jpg))
  - **air_intakes**: three: large central intake + two outer intakes with a horizontal bar (photo) ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2005-Front_Three-Quarter.8e284e72.jpg), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2005-911_carrera/))
  - **front_lid_hood**: new aluminium front lid (-6 kg) ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2005-911_carrera/))
  - **badge_crest**: crest on front lid ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2005-Front_Three-Quarter.8e284e72.jpg))
  - **other**: amber side marker in bumper corner + clear repeater on wing behind the front wheel (US photo) — _visual; region-dependent_ ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2005-Front_Three-Quarter.8e284e72.jpg))
- **rear**
  - **taillights**: wide red-and-silver tail lamps either side of the engine lid, bulb type: reversing light, indicator (PY21W), tail/brake, tail/reflector, rear fog/tail per cluster ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2005-911_carrera/), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071203210816/http://www.porsche.com/filestore.aspx/default.pdf?pool=japan&type=download&id=download-911-all&lang=jp&filetype=default), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2005-Rear_Three-Quarter.8e284e72.jpg), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20150105235627/http://press.porsche.com:80/archive/products/press_kits/press_kits_2005/pdf/carrera_carreras/911_carrera_release.pdf))
  - **reflector_band**: none on Carrera ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2005-Rear_Three-Quarter.8e284e72.jpg))
  - **bumper**: integrated rear apron with flared wheel wells; plate recess in centre ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2005-911_carrera/), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2005-Rear_Three-Quarter.8e284e72.jpg))
  - **exhaust**: Carrera: twin single-tube tailpipes, i.e. one oval tailpipe each side at the outer corners of the apron; Carrera S: twin round pipes each side (four outlets) ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071130055840/http://www.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY08_PCGB.pdf), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2005-911_carrera/), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2005-Rear_Three-Quarter.8e284e72.jpg), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20150105235627/http://press.porsche.com:80/archive/products/press_kits/press_kits_2005/pdf/carrera_carreras/911_carrera_release.pdf))
  - **license_plate**: centre of rear apron ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2005-Rear_Three-Quarter.8e284e72.jpg))
  - **badging**: 'Carrera' script on engine lid; Carrera S has silver lid logo ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2005-Rear_Three-Quarter.8e284e72.jpg), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2005-911_carrera/))
  - **other**: LED high-level third brake light at the base of the rear screen plus another LED brake light on the trailing edge of the auto-deploying rear spoiler; rear wiper mounted directly on the glass ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071130055840/http://www.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY08_PCGB.pdf), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2005-911_carrera/))
- **rear_spoiler**
  - **type**: automatically extending rear spoiler in engine lid with integrated air scoops ('more aerodynamic rear spoiler') ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2005-911_carrera/), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20040614021122/http://www2.porsche.com:80/english/int/news/pressreleases/pag/2004-05-12-2.htm), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20150105235627/http://press.porsche.com:80/archive/products/press_kits/press_kits_2005/pdf/carrera_carreras/911_carrera_release.pdf))
  - **deploy**: Automatic: engine-bay temp <=55 °C -> extends at 120 km/h, retracts at 60 km/h; >=60 °C -> extends at 80 km/h, retracts at 30 km/h; manual switch on centre console — _PCNA MY2005 release/press kit says 'up at 75 mph (120 km/h), down below 50 mph (80 km/h)' (PCNA05_REL, NCS2005); the PCNA MY2008 text and both Porsche Japan owner's manuals say retract at 60 km/h (37 mph). Owner's manual values recommended._ ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071203210816/http://www.porsche.com/filestore.aspx/default.pdf?pool=japan&type=download&id=download-911-all&lang=jp&filetype=default), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2008-911_carrera/), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20150105235627/http://press.porsche.com:80/archive/products/press_kits/press_kits_2005/pdf/carrera_carreras/911_carrera_release.pdf))
- **engine_lid_grille**
  - **design**: horizontal louvred grille in the engine lid / spoiler (air scoops built into the rear spoiler) ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2005-911_carrera/), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2005-Rear_Three-Quarter.8e284e72.jpg))
- **body**
  - **fender_flares**: more muscular wings, more accentuated arches, flared rear wheel wells; 'more emphasis on the waist' ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2005-911_carrera/), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20040614021122/http://www2.porsche.com:80/english/int/news/pressreleases/pag/2004-05-12-2.htm), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20150105235627/http://press.porsche.com:80/archive/products/press_kits/press_kits_2005/pdf/carrera_carreras/911_carrera_release.pdf))
  - **rear_arch_width**: narrow body 1,808 mm on Carrera and Carrera S; C4/C4S wide body 1,852 mm (+44 mm) ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20070618195353/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/featuresandspecs/), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/143), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-seven-generations-part-6-type-997-16489.html))
  - **mirrors**: dual-arm (double-arm) door mirrors, similar to Carrera GT; passenger mirror convex, driver aspheric; manually foldable ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071130055840/http://www.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY08_PCGB.pdf), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20040614021122/http://www2.porsche.com:80/english/int/news/pressreleases/pag/2004-05-12-2.htm), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2005-911_carrera/), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071203210816/http://www.porsche.com/filestore.aspx/default.pdf?pool=japan&type=download&id=download-911-all&lang=jp&filetype=default), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2005-Front_Three-Quarter.8e284e72.jpg), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20150105235627/http://press.porsche.com:80/archive/products/press_kits/press_kits_2005/pdf/carrera_carreras/911_carrera_release.pdf))
  - **door_handles**: bow-type door handles (body colour) ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071130055840/http://www.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY08_PCGB.pdf), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20070602121254/http://www.porsche.com:80/international/models/911/911-carrera-4/indetail/design/), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2005-Front_Three-Quarter.8e284e72.jpg))
  - **side_windows**: slimmer window-seal cross sections for windshield, side and rear glass ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2005-911_carrera/))
  - **quarter_window**: rear quarter window, rounded (photo) ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2005-Front_Three-Quarter.8e284e72.jpg))
  - **drip_rails**: none ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2005-Front_Three-Quarter.8e284e72.jpg))
  - **roofline**: roof carried over from 996 (only unchanged visible body part) ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/6.-Generation-Porsche-911,-(997),-2004-012.html))
  - **fuel_filler**: filler flap in the right front wing (central-locking flap, emergency release ring in right door opening) ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071203210816/http://www.porsche.com/filestore.aspx/default.pdf?pool=japan&type=download&id=download-911-all&lang=jp&filetype=default), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2005-Front_Three-Quarter.8e284e72.jpg))
  - **windshield**: _not found_ — _not found in: fetched sources_
  - **chrome_trim**: minimal ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2005-Front_Three-Quarter.8e284e72.jpg))
  - **side_repeaters**: side indicator repeater (WY5W) on the front wing behind the wheel arch ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071203210816/http://www.porsche.com/filestore.aspx/default.pdf?pool=japan&type=download&id=download-911-all&lang=jp&filetype=default), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2005-Front_Three-Quarter.8e284e72.jpg))
  - **rocker_sills**: doors inset with more pronounced lower sills ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2005-911_carrera/))
  - **other**: 'diagonal transition between tail and body', curving mid-section; 'altered seam characteristics' (panel joints) ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071130055840/http://www.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY08_PCGB.pdf), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20040614021122/http://www2.porsche.com:80/english/int/news/pressreleases/pag/2004-05-12-2.htm))
- **lighting_signature**: oval clear-glass headlamps + separate horizontal indicator/fog units in bumper; wide red/silver tail lamps ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/6.-Generation-Porsche-911,-(997),-2004-012.html), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2005-911_carrera/))
- **distinctive**
  - Every visible body part except the roof new vs 996 ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/6.-Generation-Porsche-911,-(997),-2004-012.html))
  - First standard 18-inch wheels on a 911 Carrera ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2005-911_carrera/))
  - Carrera 4/4S/Targa 4 (from MY2006): body 44 mm wider across the rear wheel arches (1,852 mm), extended arches, separate sill cover with rubber lip acting as rear-wheel air spoiler; rear track 1,548 (+14 mm) ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071130055840/http://www.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY08_PCGB.pdf), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20070602121254/http://www.porsche.com:80/international/models/911/911-carrera-4/indetail/design/), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2007-911_carrera_4/), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20071203210816/http://www.porsche.com/filestore.aspx/default.pdf?pool=japan&type=download&id=download-911-all&lang=jp&filetype=default), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/6.-Generation---Karosserievarianten-und-Innovationen.html))
- **open_questions**
  - US 4,461 vs RoW 4,425/4,427 length -- difference assumed to be US bumper/plate hardware (not stated explicitly)
  - Front/rear overhang split not published (4,427 - 2,350 = 2,077 mm total)
  - Windshield rake angle not published

### 14-997-2-2009 — 997.2 Carrera Coupé (facelift, 3.6 l DFI)

- **blueprints**: `blueprints/14-997-2-2009/` — primary: `vec-838_porsche-911-carrera-997-2010_side-top-dims-watermarked.jpg` — No clean 4-view of a 997.2 Carrera coupe found free. Best: vendor side+top (dims), official-looking side render, and the 997.2-based Sport Classic 4-view for the front/rear (LED daytime lights in apron, 997.2 tail lamps) - ducktail/wide body excepted.
- **production_years**: Updated July 2008 (MY2009) to 2012 ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-seven-generations-part-6-type-997-16489.html), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2009-911_carrera/), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/104))
- **reference_variant**: 911 Carrera Coupé MY2010 (3.6 l DFI, 345 hp), European spec per Porsche AG catalogue 'The 911' MY10 and porsche.com ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20091227041946/http://files.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY10_EN.pdf), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20090223000253/http://www.porsche.com:80/international/models/911/911-carrera/featuresandspecs/), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20110314223211/http://www.porsche.com:80/international/models/911/911-carrera/featuresandspecs/))
- **dimensions_mm**
  - **length**: 4435 — _+8-10 mm vs 997.1 RoW (4,425-4,427). The PCNA US catalogue (MY2011/12) lists 4,466 mm for the same car -> US-spec bumpers/plate add ~31 mm (also Excellence EXC104)._ ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20091227041946/http://files.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY10_EN.pdf), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20090223000253/http://www.porsche.com:80/international/models/911/911-carrera/featuresandspecs/), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20110314223211/http://www.porsche.com:80/international/models/911/911-carrera/featuresandspecs/), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20110928143539/http://files.porsche.com/filestore.aspx/default.pdf?pool=japan&type=download&id=porscheservice-ownersmanualdownload-911carrera&lang=jp&filetype=default&version=3))
  - **width**: 1808 — _excl. mirrors; 1,952 incl. mirrors (OM). C4/C4S/Targa 1,852._ ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20091227041946/http://files.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY10_EN.pdf), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20090223000253/http://www.porsche.com:80/international/models/911/911-carrera/featuresandspecs/), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20110928143539/http://files.porsche.com/filestore.aspx/default.pdf?pool=japan&type=download&id=porscheservice-ownersmanualdownload-911carrera&lang=jp&filetype=default&version=3))
  - **height**: 1310 — _Carrera S 1,300 (PASM standard); sports chassis 1,290_ ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20091227041946/http://files.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY10_EN.pdf), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20110928143539/http://files.porsche.com/filestore.aspx/default.pdf?pool=japan&type=download&id=porscheservice-ownersmanualdownload-911carrera&lang=jp&filetype=default&version=3))
