  - **wheelbase**: 2350 ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20090223000253/http://www.porsche.com:80/international/models/911/911-carrera/featuresandspecs/), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/104))
  - **track_front**: 1486 ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20110928143539/http://files.porsche.com/filestore.aspx/default.pdf?pool=japan&type=download&id=porscheservice-ownersmanualdownload-911carrera&lang=jp&filetype=default&version=3), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2009-911_carrera/), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/104))
  - **track_rear**: 1534 — _Carrera S 19-inch: 1,516; C4 family 1,548_ ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20110928143539/http://files.porsche.com/filestore.aspx/default.pdf?pool=japan&type=download&id=porscheservice-ownersmanualdownload-911carrera&lang=jp&filetype=default&version=3), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2009-911_carrera/), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/104))
  - **ground_clearance**: 113 — _Porsche Japan manual, at max. load (Carrera); PASM: Carrera 107, Carrera S 110; sports chassis 100. Excellence lists 106._ ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20110928143539/http://files.porsche.com/filestore.aspx/default.pdf?pool=japan&type=download&id=porscheservice-ownersmanualdownload-911carrera&lang=jp&filetype=default&version=3))
- **overhang_mm**
  - **front**: _not found_ — _length - wheelbase = 2,085 mm total (4,435 - 2,350); split needs drawing_
  - **rear**: _not found_ — _see front_
- **other_published**
  - **curb_weight_kg**: 1415 — _DIN, manual (PDK 1,445; EC 1,490/1,520)_ ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20091227041946/http://files.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY10_EN.pdf), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20090223000253/http://www.porsche.com:80/international/models/911/911-carrera/featuresandspecs/))
  - **drag_cd**: 0.29 ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20090223000253/http://www.porsche.com:80/international/models/911/911-carrera/featuresandspecs/), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2009-911_carrera/))
  - **windshield_rake_deg**: _not found_ — _not found in: porsche.com spec pages, PCNA 2009 release_
  - **width_incl_mirrors_mm**: 1952 ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20110928143539/http://files.porsche.com/filestore.aspx/default.pdf?pool=japan&type=download&id=porscheservice-ownersmanualdownload-911carrera&lang=jp&filetype=default&version=3))
  - **turning_circle_m**: 10.9 ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20091227041946/http://files.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY10_EN.pdf), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20110928143539/http://files.porsche.com/filestore.aspx/default.pdf?pool=japan&type=download&id=porscheservice-ownersmanualdownload-911carrera&lang=jp&filetype=default&version=3))
- **wheels**
  - **tyre_front**: 235/40 ZR 18 ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2009-911_carrera/), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/104))
  - **tyre_rear**: 265/40 ZR 18 ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2009-911_carrera/), [excellence-mag.com](https://www.excellence-mag.com/resources/specs/104))
  - **rim_front**: 8 J x 18 ET 57 ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20091227041946/http://files.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY10_EN.pdf), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20110928143539/http://files.porsche.com/filestore.aspx/default.pdf?pool=japan&type=download&id=porscheservice-ownersmanualdownload-911carrera&lang=jp&filetype=default&version=3), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2009-911_carrera/))
  - **rim_rear**: 10.5 J x 18 ET 60 ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20091227041946/http://files.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY10_EN.pdf), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20110928143539/http://files.porsche.com/filestore.aspx/default.pdf?pool=japan&type=download&id=porscheservice-ownersmanualdownload-911carrera&lang=jp&filetype=default&version=3), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2009-911_carrera/))
  - **rim_diameter_in**: 18 — _Carrera S 19-inch 8J/11J 235/35 & 295/30_ ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2009-911_carrera/))
  - **design_name**: 'Carrera IV' 18-inch wheel (standard on 911 Carrera): new twin-blade five-spoke design, each pair of spokes opening outward toward the rim. Carrera S: 19-inch 'Carrera S II' (five pairs of parallel spokes). Options: 19-inch Carrera Classic, Turbo, SportDesign, Carrera Sport ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20091227041946/http://files.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY10_EN.pdf), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2009-911_carrera/))
  - **spoke_count**: 5 double spokes (10 blades) ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2009-911_carrera/))
  - **finish**: silver — _photo shows Carrera S 19-inch_ ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2009-Front_Three-Quarter.ae22976f.jpg))
  - **hub_cap_center**: crest centre cap ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2009-Front_Three-Quarter.ae22976f.jpg))
  - **options_notes**: 19-inch Carrera S wheels and new 19-inch Turbo wheels optional ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2009-911_carrera/))
- **headlights**
  - **shape**: oval headlamp shape carried over from 997.1; Bi-Xenon with dynamic range control now standard on all 911 models; optional dynamic cornering light (dipped beam swivels up to 15° into the bend) ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20091227041946/http://files.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY10_EN.pdf), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2009-911_carrera/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-seven-generations-part-6-type-997-16489.html), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2009-Front_Three-Quarter.ae22976f.jpg))
  - **surround_ring**: as 997.1 ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2009-Front_Three-Quarter.ae22976f.jpg))
  - **mounting**: in front wing, as 997.1 ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2009-Front_Three-Quarter.ae22976f.jpg))
  - **lens_technology**: Bi-Xenon (low beam D2S 35 W, auxiliary high beam H11 or H7 with cornering light) standard; LED front position lights and DRL in separate units ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20110928143539/http://files.porsche.com/filestore.aspx/default.pdf?pool=japan&type=download&id=porscheservice-ownersmanualdownload-911carrera&lang=jp&filetype=default&version=3), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20091227041946/http://files.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY10_EN.pdf))
  - **washers**: headlight cleaning system (SRA) built in as standard ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20091227041946/http://files.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY10_EN.pdf))
  - **other**: None
- **front**
  - **bumper_type**: revised front apron with larger air intakes (drivetrain cooling +20 %); centre intake feeds the two outer radiators ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20091227041946/http://files.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY10_EN.pdf), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2009-911_carrera/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-carrera-coupe-997-2-2009-2012/))
  - **overriders**: none ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2009-Front_Three-Quarter.ae22976f.jpg))
  - **indicators**: separate horizontal front light units above the outer air intakes integrating direction indicators, LED daytime running lights and position lights (glass covers, 6 LEDs each for DRL) ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20091227041946/http://files.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY10_EN.pdf), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2009-911_carrera/), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2009-Front_Three-Quarter.ae22976f.jpg))
  - **fog_lights**: none: no front fog lamp in the bulb list or light switch (switch has rear fog light only); the horizontal LED front units (indicator/DRL/position) replace the 997.1 fog/indicator units ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20110928143539/http://files.porsche.com/filestore.aspx/default.pdf?pool=japan&type=download&id=porscheservice-ownersmanualdownload-911carrera&lang=jp&filetype=default&version=3), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20091227041946/http://files.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY10_EN.pdf), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2009-911_carrera/))
  - **horn_grilles**: none ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2009-Front_Three-Quarter.ae22976f.jpg))
  - **air_intakes**: three large openings, outer ones below the LED units (photo) ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2009-Front_Three-Quarter.ae22976f.jpg), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2009-911_carrera/))
  - **front_lid_hood**: as 997.1 (crest on lid) ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2009-Front_Three-Quarter.ae22976f.jpg))
  - **badge_crest**: crest on lid ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2009-Front_Three-Quarter.ae22976f.jpg))
  - **other**: None
- **rear**
  - **taillights**: LED tail lights 'drawn right into the wing and taper off to the outside'; one-piece cluster with indicator, rear fog, brake, tail, reflector (+ bulb reversing light), 60 LEDs; red and silver-transparent lenses; LED third brake light in the rear spoiler ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20091227041946/http://files.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY10_EN.pdf), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2009-911_carrera/), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2009-Rear_Three-Quarter.ae22976f.jpg))
  - **reflector_band**: none on Carrera (reflector integrated in the cluster) ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2009-911_carrera/))
  - **bumper**: revised rear apron; black trim along the bottom of the rear apron ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2009-911_carrera/), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(997)), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2009-Rear_Three-Quarter.ae22976f.jpg))
  - **exhaust**: stainless-steel tailpipes integrated seamlessly in the rear apron: Carrera two single (oval) tailpipes, one per side; Carrera S two twin round tailpipes (4 outlets) ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20091227041946/http://files.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY10_EN.pdf), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2009-911_carrera/), [stuttcars.com](https://www.stuttcars.com/porsche-911-carrera-coupe-997-2-2009-2012/), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2009-Rear_Three-Quarter.ae22976f.jpg))
  - **license_plate**: centre of rear apron ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2009-Rear_Three-Quarter.ae22976f.jpg))
  - **badging**: 'Carrera' script on lid ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2009-Rear_Three-Quarter.ae22976f.jpg))
  - **other**: None
- **rear_spoiler**
  - **type**: automatically extending rear spoiler ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2009-911_carrera/))
  - **deploy**: Automatic: engine-bay temp <=55 °C -> up at 120 km/h, down at 60 km/h; >=60 °C -> up at 80 km/h, down at 30 km/h; manual button — _PCNA 2009 text: up at 75 mph (120 km/h), retract below 50 mph (80 km/h) -- manual is authoritative._ ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20110928143539/http://files.porsche.com/filestore.aspx/default.pdf?pool=japan&type=download&id=porscheservice-ownersmanualdownload-911carrera&lang=jp&filetype=default&version=3))
- **engine_lid_grille**
  - **design**: horizontal louvre grille; twin intake funnels in rear engine cover (DFI intake) ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2009-911_carrera/), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2009-Rear_Three-Quarter.ae22976f.jpg))
- **body**
  - **fender_flares**: as 997.1 (narrow body 1,808) ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20090223000253/http://www.porsche.com:80/international/models/911/911-carrera/featuresandspecs/))
  - **rear_arch_width**: narrow body; C4/C4S wide ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20090223000253/http://www.porsche.com:80/international/models/911/911-carrera/featuresandspecs/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-seven-generations-part-6-type-997-16489.html))
  - **mirrors**: modified, larger exterior mirrors (double-arm mount, water gutter above the housing edge); width across mirrors 1,952 mm ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20091227041946/http://files.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY10_EN.pdf), [netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2009-911_carrera/), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20110928143539/http://files.porsche.com/filestore.aspx/default.pdf?pool=japan&type=download&id=porscheservice-ownersmanualdownload-911carrera&lang=jp&filetype=default&version=3))
  - **door_handles**: as 997.1 (photo) ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2009-Front_Three-Quarter.ae22976f.jpg))
  - **side_windows**: as 997.1 ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2009-Front_Three-Quarter.ae22976f.jpg))
  - **quarter_window**: as 997.1 ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2009-Front_Three-Quarter.ae22976f.jpg))
  - **drip_rails**: none ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2009-Front_Three-Quarter.ae22976f.jpg))
  - **roofline**: unchanged ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2009-Front_Three-Quarter.ae22976f.jpg))
  - **fuel_filler**: filler flap in the right front wing ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20110928143539/http://files.porsche.com/filestore.aspx/default.pdf?pool=japan&type=download&id=porscheservice-ownersmanualdownload-911carrera&lang=jp&filetype=default&version=3))
  - **windshield**: _not found_ — _not found in: fetched sources_
  - **chrome_trim**: minimal ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2009-Front_Three-Quarter.ae22976f.jpg))
  - **side_repeaters**: clear repeater on front wing behind the wheel arch (photo) ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2009-Front_Three-Quarter.ae22976f.jpg))
  - **rocker_sills**: as 997.1 ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/Porsche-911_Carrera-2009-Front_Three-Quarter.ae22976f.jpg))
  - **other**: None
- **lighting_signature**: LED DRL strips in horizontal units above the front intakes; LED tail lights tapering outward (red/silver) ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2009-911_carrera/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-seven-generations-part-6-type-997-16489.html))
- **distinctive**
  - First 911 Carrera with LED DRL and LED tail lights ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2009-911_carrera/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/history/porsche-911-seven-generations-part-6-type-997-16489.html))
  - All-wheel-drive 997.2 (C4/C4S): +44 mm at rear wings, full-width 'seamless taillight strip', titanium-coloured intake grilles/slats; C4S rear track +32 mm (catalogue wording) ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20091227041946/http://files.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY10_EN.pdf))
  - C4/C4S 997.2: 44 mm wider; 'new horizontal covers' of the LED DRL units extend over the air inlets (PCNA) ([netcarshow.com](https://www.netcarshow.com/porsche/2009-911_carrera_4/), [web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20091227041946/http://files.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY10_EN.pdf))
  - 997.2 all-wheel-drive models gained a reflective/light strip between the tail lights ('seamless taillight strip'); Wikipedia lists it as a 997.2 change ([web.archive.org](https://web.archive.org/web/20091227041946/http://files.porsche.com/all/media/pdf/Catalogue_911_997_MY10_EN.pdf), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(997)))
- **open_questions**
  - Front/rear overhang split not published (4,435 - 2,350 = 2,085 mm total)
  - Windshield rake angle not published
  - Porsche AG press database (press.porsche.com/prod/presse_pag, 'modelle-archiv') may hold 2004/2008 press kits with dimension drawings, but it returned 'Too many requests' every time in this session

### 15-991-1-2012 — 911 Carrera (Type 991, first phase '991.1')

- **blueprints**: `blueprints/15-991-1-2012/` — primary: `vec-4101_porsche-911-carrera-s-991-2012_4view-dims-watermarked.jpg` — Vendor 4-view with dims is the only verified orthographic set; several side drawings (one distorted).
- **production_years**: World premiere IAA Frankfurt Sept 2011; German market launch 3 Dec 2011; superseded by 991.2 (premiere IAA Sept 2015, launch Dec 2015). Wikipedia dates the first phase 2011-2016 (Carrera/Carrera S 2011-2015). ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:05c3198c-772f-4e4d-88ad-b6fce31b66ed/S11_0327_en.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:aed99995-b38a-42ad-846b-878cbd249c6f/~6042931.pdf), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(991)))
- **reference_variant**: 911 Carrera Coupé (991.1, 3.4-litre 350 hp, rear-wheel drive), MY2012-2015; EU data from porsche.com (2013 snapshot), US data from MY2012 brochure ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20130520150153/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/featuresandspecs/), [auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_991_2012.pdf))
- **dimensions_mm**
  - **length**: 4491 — _Same for Carrera and Carrera S_ ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20130520150153/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/featuresandspecs/), [auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_991_2012.pdf), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(991)))
  - **width**: 1808 — _Without mirrors (71.18 in). Narrow body on Carrera/Carrera S; Carrera 4/4S rear wings 44 mm wider [http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_2014.pdf]_ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:269452dc-fbc5-4684-8ae2-1bae59290fd7/Specifications_911_Carrera_Coupe.pdf), [web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20130520150153/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/featuresandspecs/), [auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_991_2012.pdf))
  - **height**: 1303 — _Carrera; Carrera S Coupé 1,295 mm (US brochure)_ ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20130520150153/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/featuresandspecs/), [auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_991_2012.pdf))
  - **wheelbase**: 2450 — _100 mm longer than the 997_ ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20130520150153/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/featuresandspecs/), [auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_991_2012.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:05c3198c-772f-4e4d-88ad-b6fce31b66ed/S11_0327_en.pdf))
  - **track_front**: 1532 — _Porsche spec sheet 911 Carrera Coupé: 1,532 mm / 60.31 in (Carrera S 1,538 mm per US spec). +46 mm vs 997 (Carrera), +52 mm (S)_ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:269452dc-fbc5-4684-8ae2-1bae59290fd7/Specifications_911_Carrera_Coupe.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:6918184a-6bbe-44f3-bf4e-3f8a331dbd46/2012_911_991_Specs.pdf))
  - **track_rear**: 1518 — _1,518 mm / 59.76 in (Carrera S 1,516 mm per US spec)_ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:269452dc-fbc5-4684-8ae2-1bae59290fd7/Specifications_911_Carrera_Coupe.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:6918184a-6bbe-44f3-bf4e-3f8a331dbd46/2012_911_991_Specs.pdf))
  - **ground_clearance**: _not found_ — _not found in: same sources_
- **overhang_mm**
  - **front**: 968 — _Derived, not published. Method: official Porsche side image; wheel-centre x from the Porsche-crest hub caps, body extremes from the silhouette; perspective corrected by scaling near-side wheel positions by k=(true L/WB)/(image L/WB) about the car centre, then total overhang = length - wheelbase split accordingly. Image: porsche.com gallery exterior-15 (real telephoto photo, image L/WB 1.728 vs true 1.833 -> k=1.061). Result front ~966-970 mm, rear ~1,071-1,075 mm; LOW confidence (+/-30 mm) - verify against an orthographic drawing._ ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20131219132823/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/gallery/))
  - **rear**: 1073 — _See front. Total front+rear must equal 4,491-2,450 = 2,041 mm._ ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20131219132823/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/gallery/))
- **other_published**
  - **curb_weight_kg**: 1380 — _DIN, manual (PDK 1,400 kg; EC 1,455/1,475 kg). US brochure: 3,042 lb manual_ ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20130520150153/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/featuresandspecs/))
  - **drag_cd**: 0.29 — _Frontal area 2.01 m2, cd x A 0.58 (Coupé); Cabriolet 0.30_ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:269452dc-fbc5-4684-8ae2-1bae59290fd7/Specifications_911_Carrera_Coupe.pdf), [web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20130520150153/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/featuresandspecs/), [web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20130520121306/http://www.porsche.com/kbase.aspx?pool=uk&type=kbase&id=991-c2-detail-concept-exterior&lang=none&callpath=%2finternational%2fmodels%2f911%2f911-carrera%2fdetail%2f))
  - **windshield_rake_deg**: _not found_ — _not found in: porsche.com 2013 pages, 2011 press release, US brochure_
- **wheels**
  - **tyre_front**: 235/40 ZR 19 ([auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_991_2012.pdf))
  - **tyre_rear**: 285/35 ZR 19 — _Porsche spec sheet: 11 J x 19 with 285/35 ZR 19. (US 2012 spec sheet's '285/30ZR19' is a typo)_ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:269452dc-fbc5-4684-8ae2-1bae59290fd7/Specifications_911_Carrera_Coupe.pdf), [auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_991_2012.pdf))
  - **rim_front**: 8.5J x 19 ([auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_991_2012.pdf))
  - **rim_rear**: 11J x 19 ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:269452dc-fbc5-4684-8ae2-1bae59290fd7/Specifications_911_Carrera_Coupe.pdf), [auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_991_2012.pdf))
  - **rim_diameter_in**: 19 — _Carrera 19-inch, Carrera S 20-inch standard - one inch larger than the respective previous models_ ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20130520121306/http://www.porsche.com/kbase.aspx?pool=uk&type=kbase&id=991-c2-detail-concept-exterior&lang=none&callpath=%2finternational%2fmodels%2f911%2f911-carrera%2fdetail%2f), [auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_991_2012.pdf))
  - **design_name**: _not found_ — _not found in: porsche.com 2013 exterior/specs pages and US brochure tech table (name of the standard 19-inch design not stated)_
  - **spoke_count**: 10 slim spokes grouped in 5 pairs (five twin-spoke) — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo (gallery exterior-15 side view)_ ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20131219132823/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/gallery/))
  - **finish**: Silver-painted alloy (as photographed) — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo_ ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20131219132823/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/gallery/))
  - **hub_cap_center**: Coloured Porsche crest centre cap — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo_ ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20131219132823/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/gallery/))
  - **options_notes**: Carrera S standard: 8.5J x 20 / 11J x 20 with 245/35 ZR 20 / 295/30 ZR 20 ([auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_991_2012.pdf))
- **headlights**
  - **shape**: Round, upright in the wings; Bi-Xenon units 'still have the familiar oval-shaped surrounds' ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20130520121306/http://www.porsche.com/kbase.aspx?pool=uk&type=kbase&id=991-c2-detail-concept-exterior&lang=none&callpath=%2finternational%2fmodels%2f911%2f911-carrera%2fdetail%2f), [web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20131219132823/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/gallery/))
  - **surround_ring**: Oval-shaped chrome inner surround around projector modules — _shape per Porsche text; chrome look from photo exterior-06_ ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20130520121306/http://www.porsche.com/kbase.aspx?pool=uk&type=kbase&id=991-c2-detail-concept-exterior&lang=none&callpath=%2finternational%2fmodels%2f911%2f911-carrera%2fdetail%2f), [web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20131219132823/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/gallery/))
  - **mounting**: Upright in front wings, lens flush with wing surface — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo_ ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20131219132823/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/gallery/))
  - **lens_technology**: Bi-Xenon standard; Porsche Dynamic Light System (PDLS) optional ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20130520121306/http://www.porsche.com/kbase.aspx?pool=uk&type=kbase&id=991-c2-detail-concept-exterior&lang=none&callpath=%2finternational%2fmodels%2f911%2f911-carrera%2fdetail%2f))
  - **washers**: Headlight cleaning system standard (washer-jet cover visible below each lamp) ([auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_2014.pdf), [web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20131219132823/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/gallery/))
  - **other**: No DRL inside the standard Bi-Xenon lamp (DRL/position/indicators are LEDs in the separate apron light units). Optional later: LED main headlights with PDLS+ containing four LED spots as DRL plus an LED light ring ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20130520121306/http://www.porsche.com/kbase.aspx?pool=uk&type=kbase&id=991-c2-detail-concept-exterior&lang=none&callpath=%2finternational%2fmodels%2f911%2f911-carrera%2fdetail%2f), [auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_2014.pdf))
- **front**
  - **bumper_type**: Integrated body-colour front apron with integral air intakes (no separate bumper) ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20130520121306/http://www.porsche.com/kbase.aspx?pool=uk&type=kbase&id=991-c2-detail-concept-exterior&lang=none&callpath=%2finternational%2fmodels%2f911%2f911-carrera%2fdetail%2f), [web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20131219132823/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/gallery/))
  - **overriders**: None — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo_ ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20131219132823/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/gallery/))
  - **indicators**: LED direction indicators, daytime running lights and position lights integrated in separate slim, swept front light units sitting above the outer intakes; 'their slimline design contrasts with that of the rounded headlights' ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20130520121306/http://www.porsche.com/kbase.aspx?pool=uk&type=kbase&id=991-c2-detail-concept-exterior&lang=none&callpath=%2finternational%2fmodels%2f911%2f911-carrera%2fdetail%2f), [web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20131219132823/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/gallery/))
  - **fog_lights**: _not found_ — _not found in: porsche.com 2013 exterior page (none visible in official front photo exterior-01)_
  - **horn_grilles**: None (not applicable to this era) — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo_ ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20131219132823/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/gallery/))
  - **air_intakes**: Two outer cooling-air intakes, each with three horizontal fins that protrude slightly, plus a wide central lower intake with rounded corners — _Porsche text mentions 'two integral air intakes'; photo shows two outer intakes + central opening_ ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20130520121306/http://www.porsche.com/kbase.aspx?pool=uk&type=kbase&id=991-c2-detail-concept-exterior&lang=none&callpath=%2finternational%2fmodels%2f911%2f911-carrera%2fdetail%2f), [web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20131219132823/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/gallery/))
  - **front_lid_hood**: Smooth front lid, shut lines running inboard of the headlights; Porsche crest near leading edge — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo_ ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20131219132823/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/gallery/))
  - **badge_crest**: Porsche crest on front lid — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo_ ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20131219132823/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/gallery/))
  - **other**: Small clear side-marker lens on the wing flank just behind the headlight — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo (exterior-06, exterior-15)_ ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20131219132823/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/gallery/))
- **rear**
  - **taillights**: Narrow LED taillights accentuating the horizontal contour; separate L/R units on the rear-drive Carrera. All-wheel-drive Carrera 4/4S add an illuminated rear light strip between the lamps (with tail/parking light function). LED third brake light ([auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_2014.pdf), [web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20130520121306/http://www.porsche.com/kbase.aspx?pool=uk&type=kbase&id=991-c2-detail-concept-exterior&lang=none&callpath=%2finternational%2fmodels%2f911%2f911-carrera%2fdetail%2f), [web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20131219132823/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/gallery/))
  - **reflector_band**: No reflector band; 'the characteristic reflector units have been positioned lower down' - small horizontal red reflectors low in the bumper either side of the plate ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20130520121306/http://www.porsche.com/kbase.aspx?pool=uk&type=kbase&id=991-c2-detail-concept-exterior&lang=none&callpath=%2finternational%2fmodels%2f911%2f911-carrera%2fdetail%2f), [web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20131219132823/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/gallery/))
  - **bumper**: Integrated body-colour rear apron with recessed plate area and black lower diffuser section — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo (exterior-04)_ ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20131219132823/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/gallery/))
  - **exhaust**: Stainless-steel tailpipes: single-tube tailpipe each side on the 911 Carrera (2 total), twin-tube each side on the Carrera S (4 total); optional sports exhaust with twin tailpipes ([auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_2014.pdf), [web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20130520121306/http://www.porsche.com/kbase.aspx?pool=uk&type=kbase&id=991-c2-detail-concept-exterior&lang=none&callpath=%2finternational%2fmodels%2f911%2f911-carrera%2fdetail%2f), [web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20131219132823/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/gallery/))
  - **license_plate**: Centred in a recess in the rear bumper, between the reflectors — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo_ ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20131219132823/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/gallery/))
  - **badging**: 'PORSCHE' lettering spread between the taillights, '911 Carrera' script below — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo (exterior-04/07)_ ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20131219132823/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/gallery/))
  - **other**: 'Distinctive profile edge' across the tail ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20130520121306/http://www.porsche.com/kbase.aspx?pool=uk&type=kbase&id=991-c2-detail-concept-exterior&lang=none&callpath=%2finternational%2fmodels%2f911%2f911-carrera%2fdetail%2f))
- **rear_spoiler**
  - **type**: Wider, variably extending rear spoiler integrated in the rear lid below the engine grille, spanning between the taillights ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:05c3198c-772f-4e4d-88ad-b6fce31b66ed/S11_0327_en.pdf), [web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20130520121306/http://www.porsche.com/kbase.aspx?pool=uk&type=kbase&id=991-c2-detail-concept-exterior&lang=none&callpath=%2finternational%2fmodels%2f911%2f911-carrera%2fdetail%2f), [web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20131219132823/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/gallery/))
  - **deploy**: _not found_ — _not found in: 2011 press release and porsche.com 2013 pages (deploy speed not stated)_
- **engine_lid_grille**
  - **design**: Trapezoidal rear-lid grille with three bright horizontal louvres; LED third brake light as a horizontal strip across the top of the grille opening — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo (exterior-04 rear, exterior-05 top); LED third brake light per 2014 brochure_ ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20131219132823/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/gallery/), [auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_2014.pdf))
- **body**
  - **fender_flares**: Narrow body on Carrera/Carrera S (1,808 mm). Carrera 4/4S: rear wings 44 mm wider than the Carrera, distinctive side skirts, specially shaped front air intakes ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20130520150153/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/featuresandspecs/), [auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_2014.pdf))
  - **rear_arch_width**: Narrow rear on rear-drive models; +44 mm wider rear body on all-wheel-drive models ([auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_US%20911_2014.pdf))
  - **mirrors**: Remodelled mirrors mounted on the upper edge of the door, no longer on the mirror triangle ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:05c3198c-772f-4e4d-88ad-b6fce31b66ed/S11_0327_en.pdf), [web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20130520121306/http://www.porsche.com/kbase.aspx?pool=uk&type=kbase&id=991-c2-detail-concept-exterior&lang=none&callpath=%2finternational%2fmodels%2f911%2f911-carrera%2fdetail%2f))
  - **door_handles**: Pull-out handle with recessed grip shell (the 991.2 later deleted the recess covers) ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:aed99995-b38a-42ad-846b-878cbd249c6f/~6042931.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:29e27626-6871-4885-93cc-cb48d10b76b9/PCNA18_0115_us.pdf), [web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20131219132823/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/gallery/))
  - **side_windows**: Door glass plus fixed rear quarter window; roof line tapers down into the rear ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20131219132823/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/gallery/), [web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20130520121306/http://www.porsche.com/kbase.aspx?pool=uk&type=kbase&id=991-c2-detail-concept-exterior&lang=none&callpath=%2finternational%2fmodels%2f911%2f911-carrera%2fdetail%2f))
  - **quarter_window**: Fixed quarter window tapering to a point at the rear, follows the flyline — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo_ ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20131219132823/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/gallery/))
  - **drip_rails**: None — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo_ ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20131219132823/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/gallery/))
  - **roofline**: Lower roof line and reduced height vs 997; flat, stretched silhouette ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20130520121306/http://www.porsche.com/kbase.aspx?pool=uk&type=kbase&id=991-c2-detail-concept-exterior&lang=none&callpath=%2finternational%2fmodels%2f911%2f911-carrera%2fdetail%2f), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:05c3198c-772f-4e4d-88ad-b6fce31b66ed/S11_0327_en.pdf))
  - **fuel_filler**: Flap on the right front wing, ahead of the door — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo (exterior-15 side, exterior-05 top)_ ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20131219132823/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/gallery/))
  - **windshield**: _not found_ — _not found in: porsche.com 2013 pages, 2011 press release_
  - **chrome_trim**: _not found_ — _not found in: Porsche text; photos show little/no bright body trim_
  - **side_repeaters**: Small clear marker lens on the front-wing flank behind the headlight — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo_ ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20131219132823/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/gallery/))
  - **rocker_sills**: Body-colour sills — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo_ ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20131219132823/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/gallery/))
  - **other**: Rear wings sit higher than the front lid; all-new aluminium-steel body, up to 45 kg lighter ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20130520121306/http://www.porsche.com/kbase.aspx?pool=uk&type=kbase&id=991-c2-detail-concept-exterior&lang=none&callpath=%2finternational%2fmodels%2f911%2f911-carrera%2fdetail%2f), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:05c3198c-772f-4e4d-88ad-b6fce31b66ed/S11_0327_en.pdf))
- **lighting_signature**: Round Bi-Xenon headlights (no DRL inside) + slim LED DRL/indicator strips above the outer apron intakes; slim horizontally stretched LED taillights, separate L/R ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20130520121306/http://www.porsche.com/kbase.aspx?pool=uk&type=kbase&id=991-c2-detail-concept-exterior&lang=none&callpath=%2finternational%2fmodels%2f911%2f911-carrera%2fdetail%2f), [web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20131219132823/http://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera/gallery/))
- **distinctive**
  - Wheelbase +100 mm to 2,450 mm with shorter front and rear overhangs ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:05c3198c-772f-4e4d-88ad-b6fce31b66ed/S11_0327_en.pdf), [web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20130520121306/http://www.porsche.com/kbase.aspx?pool=uk&type=kbase&id=991-c2-detail-concept-exterior&lang=none&callpath=%2finternational%2fmodels%2f911%2f911-carrera%2fdetail%2f))
  - Mirrors moved from the A-pillar triangle onto the door ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:05c3198c-772f-4e4d-88ad-b6fce31b66ed/S11_0327_en.pdf))
  - Flatter-looking LED tail lights and front bumper with bigger air intakes; front looks wider than predecessor ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/7.-Generation---Design-und-Modellvarianten.html))
  - Wider front track: +46 mm (Carrera), +52 mm (Carrera S) ([web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20130520121306/http://www.porsche.com/kbase.aspx?pool=uk&type=kbase&id=991-c2-detail-concept-exterior&lang=none&callpath=%2finternational%2fmodels%2f911%2f911-carrera%2fdetail%2f))
  - 991 design brief: extended wheelbase, wider track and lower roof line; headlights, rear lights, front mask and rear window were then designed around these new proportions ([presskit.porsche.de](https://presskit.porsche.de/specials/en/porsche-1mio-911/topic/the-design-of-the-911.html (via http://web.archive.org/web/2019/ snapshot)))
- **open_questions**
  - Rear spoiler deploy/retract speeds for 991.1 not found (checked: 2011 press release, porsche.com 2013 pages, US brochures 2012-2015, 2012 US spec sheet, Wikipedia en/de/fr/it)
  - Official name of the standard 19-inch wheel not found (spoke layout verified visually: 5 twin spokes)
  - Overhang split is a photo-derived estimate (+/-30 mm)

### 16-991-2-2016 — 911 Carrera (Type 991, second phase '991.2')

- **blueprints**: `blueprints/16-991-2-2016/` — primary: `dd_porsche-official_911-carrera-s-2016_front-rear-side-mm.gif`, `tbp-83850_porsche-911-carrera-s-coupe-2015_3view.png` — Excellent: official Porsche dimension drawing (front/rear/side with mm and angles) + matching 3-view line drawing + vendor 4-view (top).
- **production_years**: World premiere IAA Frankfurt 15 Sept 2015; German market launch 12 Dec 2015; 991 production ended 20 Dec 2019 ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:791250fa-0ef1-41fb-aef4-56e2d3404a19/S15_0785_en.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:aed99995-b38a-42ad-846b-878cbd249c6f/~6042931.pdf), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(991)))
- **reference_variant**: 911 Carrera Coupé (991.2, 3.0-litre twin-turbo 370 hp, rear-wheel drive), MY2016-2019; EU brochure (07/2015) and porsche.com (2018 snapshot) ([auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_2016-1.pdf), [web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20180923124536/https://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera-models/911-carrera/featuresandspecs/))
- **dimensions_mm**
  - **length**: 4499 ([auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_2016-1.pdf), [web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20180923124536/https://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera-models/911-carrera/featuresandspecs/))
  - **width**: 1808 — _Without mirrors; 1,978 mm with mirrors. Carrera 4/4S: 1,852 mm (44 mm wider rear body) [http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_2017.pdf]_ ([auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_2016-1.pdf), [web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20180923124536/https://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera-models/911-carrera/featuresandspecs/), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:8a8a3990-34a8-4ae0-a11e-f4bb53abd5e0/2017_911_Carrera_and_Carrera_S_Technical_Specifications.pdf))
  - **height**: 1294 — _Carrera Coupé; Carrera S 1,296 (manual)/1,295 (PDK). PASM standard lowers ride height 10 mm vs 991.1_ ([auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_2016-1.pdf), [web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20180923124536/https://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera-models/911-carrera/featuresandspecs/))
  - **wheelbase**: 2450 ([auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_2016-1.pdf), [web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20180923124536/https://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera-models/911-carrera/featuresandspecs/))
  - **track_front**: 1541 — _US 2017 spec, 911 Carrera (Carrera S 1,543 mm; Carrera 4 1,541 mm)_ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:8a8a3990-34a8-4ae0-a11e-f4bb53abd5e0/2017_911_Carrera_and_Carrera_S_Technical_Specifications.pdf))
  - **track_rear**: 1518 — _US 2017 spec, 911 Carrera and Carrera S; Carrera 4/4S rear track 1,558 mm (+40 mm) [https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:60826685-395e-47f8-a50b-046abff854cb/2017_911_Carrera_4_and_Carrera_4S_Technical_Specifications.pdf]_ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:8a8a3990-34a8-4ae0-a11e-f4bb53abd5e0/2017_911_Carrera_and_Carrera_S_Technical_Specifications.pdf))
  - **ground_clearance**: _not found_ — _Not published as a static value. Optional front-axle lift raises front clearance by 40 mm within 5 s [https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:aed99995-b38a-42ad-846b-878cbd249c6f/~6042931.pdf]_
- **overhang_mm**
  - **front**: _not found_ — _Not published; total front+rear = 4,499 - 2,450 = 2,049 mm (8 mm more than 991.1). No straight side image measured for 991.2 - use the official Porsche 991.2 dimension drawing the parent holds._
  - **rear**: _not found_ — _See front_
- **other_published**
  - **curb_weight_kg**: 1430 — _DIN manual (PDK 1,450; EC 1,505/1,525). Note: the 2018 porsche.com snapshot lists 1,570/1,590 kg DIN - inconsistent with the brochure; brochure value recommended_ ([auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_2016-1.pdf))
  - **drag_cd**: 0.29 — _Carrera Coupé; Carrera S 0.30_ ([auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_2016-1.pdf), [web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20180923124536/https://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera-models/911-carrera/featuresandspecs/))
  - **windshield_rake_deg**: _not found_ — _not found in: brochure, press releases_
- **wheels**
  - **tyre_front**: 235/40 ZR 19 ([auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_2016-1.pdf), [web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20180923124536/https://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera-models/911-carrera/featuresandspecs/))
  - **tyre_rear**: 295/35 ZR 19 ([auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_2016-1.pdf), [web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20180923124536/https://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera-models/911-carrera/featuresandspecs/))
  - **rim_front**: 8.5J x 19 ET50 ([auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_2016-1.pdf), [web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20180923124536/https://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera-models/911-carrera/featuresandspecs/))
  - **rim_rear**: 11.5J x 19 ET76 — _+0.5 in wider rear rims on all variants_ ([auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_2016-1.pdf), [web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20180923124536/https://www.porsche.com/international/models/911/911-carrera-models/911-carrera/featuresandspecs/))
  - **rim_diameter_in**: 19 ([auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_2016-1.pdf))
  - **design_name**: '19-inch Carrera wheels' (Carrera S: '20-inch Carrera S wheels') ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:29e27626-6871-4885-93cc-cb48d10b76b9/PCNA18_0115_us.pdf))
  - **spoke_count**: Five slim twin spokes (10 spokes) ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:aed99995-b38a-42ad-846b-878cbd249c6f/~6042931.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/products/porsche-911-carrera-new-2015-11366.html))
  - **finish**: Silver (as photographed) — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo_ ([pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/10f20e1c-d84e-471a-a3c0-5b1b80faf752.zip))
  - **hub_cap_center**: Porsche crest centre cap — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo_ ([pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/10f20e1c-d84e-471a-a3c0-5b1b80faf752.zip))
  - **options_notes**: Carrera S: 8.5J x 20 ET49 / 11.5J x 20 ET76 with 245/35 ZR 20 / 305/30 ZR 20 (rear tyre 305 instead of 295) ([auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_2016-1.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:aed99995-b38a-42ad-846b-878cbd249c6f/~6042931.pdf))
- **headlights**
  - **shape**: Round, upright in the wings (unchanged form) — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo_ ([pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/10f20e1c-d84e-471a-a3c0-5b1b80faf752.zip))
  - **surround_ring**: _not found_ — _not found in: press releases; photo shows dark inner housing_
  - **mounting**: Upright in the front wings — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo_ ([pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/10f20e1c-d84e-471a-a3c0-5b1b80faf752.zip))
  - **lens_technology**: Newly designed Bi-Xenon headlights with integrated four-point LED daytime running lights ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:29e27626-6871-4885-93cc-cb48d10b76b9/PCNA18_0115_us.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:aed99995-b38a-42ad-846b-878cbd249c6f/~6042931.pdf))
  - **washers**: Round washer-jet cover below each headlight — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo_ ([pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/10f20e1c-d84e-471a-a3c0-5b1b80faf752.zip))
  - **other**: Four-point DRL signature now inside the headlight ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:aed99995-b38a-42ad-846b-878cbd249c6f/~6042931.pdf))
- **front**
  - **bumper_type**: New front fascia with a much stronger profile, making the front look wider; V-shaped design lines in the top part; three-dimensional front spoiler lip ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:29e27626-6871-4885-93cc-cb48d10b76b9/PCNA18_0115_us.pdf))
  - **overriders**: None — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo_ ([pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/10f20e1c-d84e-471a-a3c0-5b1b80faf752.zip))
  - **indicators**: Narrowed front lights positioned above the cooling air intakes contain park/position lights and direction indicators ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:29e27626-6871-4885-93cc-cb48d10b76b9/PCNA18_0115_us.pdf))
  - **fog_lights**: _not found_ — _not found in: PCNA press info, 2015 press release_
  - **horn_grilles**: None — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo_ ([pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/10f20e1c-d84e-471a-a3c0-5b1b80faf752.zip))
  - **air_intakes**: Two outer cooling-air intakes with horizontal slats and active cooling-air flaps (three flaps per intake, closed above 9 mph, open in stages from 100 mph), 'airblades'; centre intake with two horizontal vanes ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:29e27626-6871-4885-93cc-cb48d10b76b9/PCNA18_0115_us.pdf))
  - **front_lid_hood**: Smooth lid with crest (carry-over form) — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo_ ([pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/10f20e1c-d84e-471a-a3c0-5b1b80faf752.zip))
  - **badge_crest**: Porsche crest on front lid — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo_ ([pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/10f20e1c-d84e-471a-a3c0-5b1b80faf752.zip))
  - **other**: Larger front spoiler lip with optional PASM Sport suspension ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:29e27626-6871-4885-93cc-cb48d10b76b9/PCNA18_0115_us.pdf))
- **rear**
  - **taillights**: Prominent three-dimensional LED taillights (sculptural look from the 918 Spyder) with four-point brake lights and a delicate lower night-design feature; separate L/R on rear-drive cars. AWD Carrera 4/4S keep a seamless light strip between the lamps - now narrower and appearing to seep into the tail-light units ([auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_2017.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:29e27626-6871-4885-93cc-cb48d10b76b9/PCNA18_0115_us.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:aed99995-b38a-42ad-846b-878cbd249c6f/~6042931.pdf))
  - **reflector_band**: No band; reflectors low in the bumper with an additional edge above them emphasising width ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:29e27626-6871-4885-93cc-cb48d10b76b9/PCNA18_0115_us.pdf))
  - **bumper**: Rear fascia 'appears sharper'; intercooler air outlets at the lower sides of the rear fascia ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:29e27626-6871-4885-93cc-cb48d10b76b9/PCNA18_0115_us.pdf))
  - **exhaust**: Carrera: main muffler with two oval single-tube stainless tailpipes (one each side); Carrera S: twin ('two-tract') tailpipes each side; optional Sport Exhaust: two centrally mounted tailpipes (inspired by the 911 GT3) ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:29e27626-6871-4885-93cc-cb48d10b76b9/PCNA18_0115_us.pdf), [auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_2017.pdf))
  - **license_plate**: Centred in the rear bumper recess (carry-over position) — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo_ ([pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/10f20e1c-d84e-471a-a3c0-5b1b80faf752.zip))
  - **badging**: 'PORSCHE' lettering between taillights, '911 Carrera S' script below — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo_ ([pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/10f20e1c-d84e-471a-a3c0-5b1b80faf752.zip))
  - **other**: _not found_ — _not found in: -_
- **rear_spoiler**
  - **type**: Adaptive extending rear spoiler; angle of attack adjusts with the front flap position and to feed intercooler air ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:29e27626-6871-4885-93cc-cb48d10b76b9/PCNA18_0115_us.pdf))
  - **deploy**: Normal deploy speed not stated; in high outside temperatures it can extend from 37 mph (60 km/h) to increase intercooling air — _normal speed pending_ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:29e27626-6871-4885-93cc-cb48d10b76b9/PCNA18_0115_us.pdf))
- **engine_lid_grille**
  - **design**: Redesigned rear lid with vertical (longitudinally arranged) louvres/slats in high-gloss black feeding the intercoolers ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:aed99995-b38a-42ad-846b-878cbd249c6f/~6042931.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:29e27626-6871-4885-93cc-cb48d10b76b9/PCNA18_0115_us.pdf), [auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_2017.pdf), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/10f20e1c-d84e-471a-a3c0-5b1b80faf752.zip))
- **body**
  - **fender_flares**: Narrow body on Carrera/Carrera S (1,808 mm) ([auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_2016-1.pdf))
  - **rear_arch_width**: Narrow rear on rear-drive Carrera/S (1,808 mm); all-wheel-drive Carrera 4/4S and Targa 4 have a 44 mm wider rear body (1,852 mm) ([auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_2016-1.pdf), [auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/Porsche_int%20911_2017.pdf))
  - **mirrors**: On the door (as 991.1); lane-change assist LEDs in the mirror triangle; optional SportDesign mirrors ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:aed99995-b38a-42ad-846b-878cbd249c6f/~6042931.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:29e27626-6871-4885-93cc-cb48d10b76b9/PCNA18_0115_us.pdf))
  - **door_handles**: Door handles without recess covers/grip shells ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:aed99995-b38a-42ad-846b-878cbd249c6f/~6042931.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:29e27626-6871-4885-93cc-cb48d10b76b9/PCNA18_0115_us.pdf))
  - **side_windows**: Carry-over glasshouse: door glass + fixed quarter window — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo_ ([pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/10f20e1c-d84e-471a-a3c0-5b1b80faf752.zip))
  - **quarter_window**: Fixed, tapered rear quarter window — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo_ ([pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/10f20e1c-d84e-471a-a3c0-5b1b80faf752.zip))
  - **drip_rails**: None — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo_ ([pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/10f20e1c-d84e-471a-a3c0-5b1b80faf752.zip))
  - **roofline**: Unchanged flyline ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:29e27626-6871-4885-93cc-cb48d10b76b9/PCNA18_0115_us.pdf))
  - **fuel_filler**: _not found_ — _not found in: 991.2 press material; not visually verified (991.1 and 992.1 both have it on the right front wing)_
  - **windshield**: _not found_ — _not found in: -_
  - **chrome_trim**: _not found_ — _not found in: -_
  - **side_repeaters**: Clear marker lens on front-wing flank behind the headlight — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo_ ([pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/10f20e1c-d84e-471a-a3c0-5b1b80faf752.zip))
  - **rocker_sills**: Body colour — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo_ ([pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/10f20e1c-d84e-471a-a3c0-5b1b80faf752.zip))
  - **other**: _not found_ — _not found in: -_
- **lighting_signature**: Four-point LED DRL inside round Bi-Xenon headlights + narrow LED position/indicator strips above the intakes; 3D taillights with four-point brake lights ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:29e27626-6871-4885-93cc-cb48d10b76b9/PCNA18_0115_us.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:aed99995-b38a-42ad-846b-878cbd249c6f/~6042931.pdf))
- **distinctive**
  - First turbocharged base Carrera: vertical-louvre black rear-lid grille and intercooler outlets at the lower rear corners ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:29e27626-6871-4885-93cc-cb48d10b76b9/PCNA18_0115_us.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:aed99995-b38a-42ad-846b-878cbd249c6f/~6042931.pdf))
  - Four-point DRL in headlight and four-point brake lights ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:aed99995-b38a-42ad-846b-878cbd249c6f/~6042931.pdf))

  - Normal rear-spoiler deploy speed for 991.2 not found (only the 37 mph high-temperature intercooling case is published)

### 17-992-1-2019 — 911 Carrera (Type 992, first phase '992.1')

- **blueprints**: `blueprints/17-992-1-2019/` — primary: `vec-17529_porsche-911-carrera-4s-992-2019_4view-dims-watermarked.jpg` — Vendor (watermarked) 4-view with dims for the 992.1 Carrera 4S; GTS and Aerokit variants as secondary. Official Porsche dimension drawings: see any official_* files added from press material.
- **production_years**: Carrera S/4S revealed 27-28 Nov 2018 (Los Angeles); base Carrera Coupé/Cabriolet announced 30 Jul 2019; replaced by 992.2 from 28 May 2024 ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/products/porsche-911-new-eigth-generation-992-timeless-machine-design-icon-world-premiere-los-angeles-2018-16522.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2019/products/porsche-911-carrera-coupe-cabriolet-eighth-generation-18248.html), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(992)))
- **reference_variant**: 911 Carrera Coupé (992.1, 3.0 twin-turbo 385 PS, RWD), MY2020-2024; US 2020 brochure + German Wikipedia table; Carrera S where only S data exist (TWS drawing) ([auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/992/Porsche_US%20911_2021.pdf), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_992), [presskit.porsche.de](https://presskit.porsche.de/workshops/en/porsche-new-911/downloads.html?file=daten/assets/workshops-porsche-new-911/documents/presentation/en/TWS_992_Body_EN.pdf (retrieved via http://web.archive.org/web/2024id_/ snapshot)))
- **dimensions_mm**
  - **length**: 4519 — _US brochure 177.9 in; TWS drawing '4519 / 4499 (+20)' vs 991.2_ ([auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/992/Porsche_US%20911_2021.pdf), [presskit.porsche.de](https://presskit.porsche.de/workshops/en/porsche-new-911/downloads.html?file=daten/assets/workshops-porsche-new-911/documents/presentation/en/TWS_992_Body_EN.pdf (retrieved via http://web.archive.org/web/2024id_/ snapshot)), [web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20200227225631/https://www.porsche.com/uk/models/911/911-carrera-models/), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_992))
  - **width**: 1852 — _Without mirrors; 2,024 mm with mirrors_ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:683ce58d-5a57-4bd0-bf5f-b1bf5fe1931d/2020_911_Carrera_and_911_Carrera_Cabriolet_US_specifications.pdf), [web.archive.org](http://web.archive.org/web/20200227225631/https://www.porsche.com/uk/models/911/911-carrera-models/), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_992), [presskit.porsche.de](https://presskit.porsche.de/workshops/en/porsche-new-911/specifications.html?file=daten/assets/workshops-porsche-new-911/documents/technical/en/PAG_911CarreraS_TD_EN.pdf (via http://web.archive.org/web/20221205202833id_/ snapshot)))
  - **height**: 1298 — _911 Carrera Coupé; Carrera S EU 1,300 mm_ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:5f3af6f6-7e71-41c1-85ed-51f215261b42/PAG_911Carrera_EN.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:683ce58d-5a57-4bd0-bf5f-b1bf5fe1931d/2020_911_Carrera_and_911_Carrera_Cabriolet_US_specifications.pdf), [presskit.porsche.de](https://presskit.porsche.de/workshops/en/porsche-new-911/downloads.html?file=daten/assets/workshops-porsche-new-911/documents/presentation/en/TWS_992_Body_EN.pdf (retrieved via http://web.archive.org/web/2024id_/ snapshot)), [de.wikipedia.org](https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_992))
  - **wheelbase**: 2450 — _Unchanged vs 991_ ([presskit.porsche.de](https://presskit.porsche.de/workshops/en/porsche-new-911/downloads.html?file=daten/assets/workshops-porsche-new-911/documents/presentation/en/TWS_992_Body_EN.pdf (retrieved via http://web.archive.org/web/2024id_/ snapshot)), [auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/992/Porsche_US%20911_2021.pdf))
  - **track_front**: 1591 — _911 Carrera (EU technical data and US 2020 spec agree). Carrera S EU: 1,589 mm [https://presskit.porsche.de/workshops/en/porsche-new-911/specifications.html?file=daten/assets/workshops-porsche-new-911/documents/technical/en/PAG_911CarreraS_TD_EN.pdf (via http://web.archive.org/web/20221205202833id_/ snapshot)]. Porsche: +46 mm vs 991.2_ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:5f3af6f6-7e71-41c1-85ed-51f215261b42/PAG_911Carrera_EN.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:683ce58d-5a57-4bd0-bf5f-b1bf5fe1931d/2020_911_Carrera_and_911_Carrera_Cabriolet_US_specifications.pdf))
  - **track_rear**: 1557 — _Same for Carrera and Carrera S_ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:5f3af6f6-7e71-41c1-85ed-51f215261b42/PAG_911Carrera_EN.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:683ce58d-5a57-4bd0-bf5f-b1bf5fe1931d/2020_911_Carrera_and_911_Carrera_Cabriolet_US_specifications.pdf), [presskit.porsche.de](https://presskit.porsche.de/workshops/en/porsche-new-911/specifications.html?file=daten/assets/workshops-porsche-new-911/documents/technical/en/PAG_911CarreraS_TD_EN.pdf (via http://web.archive.org/web/20221205202833id_/ snapshot)))
  - **ground_clearance**: _not found_ — _not found in: press kits/brochure_
- **overhang_mm**
  - **front**: 1028 — _Derived, not published. Method: official Porsche side image; wheel-centre x from the Porsche-crest hub caps, body extremes from the silhouette; perspective corrected by scaling near-side wheel positions by k=(true L/WB)/(image L/WB) about the car centre, then total overhang = length - wheelbase split accordingly. Two official porsche.com 992.1 Carrera side renders (1920 px PNG: image L/WB 1.763, k=1.046; 3840 px JPG: L/WB 1.759) give front 1,026-1,030 mm, rear 1,039-1,042 mm; the TWS dimension drawing gives 1,029/1,040. +/-15 mm._ ([files.porsche.com](https://files.porsche.com/filestore/image/multimedia/none/992-c2-modelimage-sideshot/model/5df3abc0-999a-11e9-80c4-005056bbdc38/porsche-model.png (linked from http://web.archive.org/web/20200227225631/https://www.porsche.com/uk/models/911/911-carrera-models/)), [files.porsche.com](https://files.porsche.com/filestore/image/multimedia/none/992-c2-technicalspecs/normal/ae5c8230-999a-11e9-80c4-005056bbdc38/porsche-normal.jpg), [presskit.porsche.de](https://presskit.porsche.de/workshops/en/porsche-new-911/downloads.html?file=daten/assets/workshops-porsche-new-911/documents/presentation/en/TWS_992_Body_EN.pdf (retrieved via http://web.archive.org/web/2024id_/ snapshot)))
  - **rear**: 1041 — _See front. Total = 4,519 - 2,450 = 2,069 mm._ ([files.porsche.com](https://files.porsche.com/filestore/image/multimedia/none/992-c2-modelimage-sideshot/model/5df3abc0-999a-11e9-80c4-005056bbdc38/porsche-model.png (linked from http://web.archive.org/web/20200227225631/https://www.porsche.com/uk/models/911/911-carrera-models/)), [files.porsche.com](https://files.porsche.com/filestore/image/multimedia/none/992-c2-technicalspecs/normal/ae5c8230-999a-11e9-80c4-005056bbdc38/porsche-normal.jpg), [presskit.porsche.de](https://presskit.porsche.de/workshops/en/porsche-new-911/downloads.html?file=daten/assets/workshops-porsche-new-911/documents/presentation/en/TWS_992_Body_EN.pdf (retrieved via http://web.archive.org/web/2024id_/ snapshot)))
- **other_published**

  - **drag_cd**: 0.29 — _EU (frontal area 2.07 m2); US spec/brochure quote 0.31_ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:5f3af6f6-7e71-41c1-85ed-51f215261b42/PAG_911Carrera_EN.pdf), [presskit.porsche.de](https://presskit.porsche.de/workshops/en/porsche-new-911/specifications.html?file=daten/assets/workshops-porsche-new-911/documents/technical/en/PAG_911CarreraS_TD_EN.pdf (via http://web.archive.org/web/20221205202833id_/ snapshot)))
