  - **tyre_front**: 235/40 ZR 19 ([auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/992/Porsche_US%20911_2021.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2019/products/porsche-911-carrera-coupe-cabriolet-eighth-generation-18248.html))
  - **tyre_rear**: 295/35 ZR 20 ([auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/992/Porsche_US%20911_2021.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2019/products/porsche-911-carrera-coupe-cabriolet-eighth-generation-18248.html))
  - **rim_front**: 8.5J x 19 ET52 — _Carrera S: 8.5J x 20 ET53_ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:5f3af6f6-7e71-41c1-85ed-51f215261b42/PAG_911Carrera_EN.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:683ce58d-5a57-4bd0-bf5f-b1bf5fe1931d/2020_911_Carrera_and_911_Carrera_Cabriolet_US_specifications.pdf), [auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/992/Porsche_US%20911_2021.pdf))
  - **rim_rear**: 11.5J x 20 ET67 — _Carrera S: 11.5J x 21 ET67_ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:5f3af6f6-7e71-41c1-85ed-51f215261b42/PAG_911Carrera_EN.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:683ce58d-5a57-4bd0-bf5f-b1bf5fe1931d/2020_911_Carrera_and_911_Carrera_Cabriolet_US_specifications.pdf), [auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/992/Porsche_US%20911_2021.pdf))
  - **rim_diameter_in**: 19 front / 20 rear (staggered) — _First time different diameters front/rear [https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/8.-Generation---Karosserievarianten-und-Innovationen.html]_ ([auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/992/Porsche_US%20911_2021.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2019/products/porsche-911-carrera-coupe-cabriolet-eighth-generation-18248.html))
  - **design_name**: '19/20-inch Carrera wheel' (standard on Carrera; Carrera S: '20/21-inch Carrera S wheel') ([auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/992/Porsche_US%20911_2021.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:683ce58d-5a57-4bd0-bf5f-b1bf5fe1931d/2020_911_Carrera_and_911_Carrera_Cabriolet_US_specifications.pdf), [presskit.porsche.de](https://presskit.porsche.de/workshops/en/porsche-new-911/downloads.html?file=daten/assets/workshops-porsche-new-911/documents/presentation/en/TWS_992_Chassis_EN.pdf (via http://web.archive.org/web/2024id_/ snapshot)))
  - **spoke_count**: Five dual-arm (V) spokes = 10 spoke arms (Carrera); Carrera S wheel is a 10-spoke design ([auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/992/Porsche_US%20911_2021.pdf), [files.porsche.com](https://files.porsche.com/filestore/image/multimedia/none/992-c2-modelimage-sideshot/model/5df3abc0-999a-11e9-80c4-005056bbdc38/porsche-model.png (linked from http://web.archive.org/web/20200227225631/https://www.porsche.com/uk/models/911/911-carrera-models/)))
  - **finish**: Silver-painted (as shown on the standard-spec porsche.com render) — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo_ ([files.porsche.com](https://files.porsche.com/filestore/image/multimedia/none/992-c2-modelimage-sideshot/model/5df3abc0-999a-11e9-80c4-005056bbdc38/porsche-model.png (linked from http://web.archive.org/web/20200227225631/https://www.porsche.com/uk/models/911/911-carrera-models/)), [auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/992/Porsche_US%20911_2021.pdf))
  - **hub_cap_center**: Coloured Porsche crest centre cap — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo_ ([files.porsche.com](https://files.porsche.com/filestore/image/multimedia/none/992-c2-modelimage-sideshot/model/5df3abc0-999a-11e9-80c4-005056bbdc38/porsche-model.png (linked from http://web.archive.org/web/20200227225631/https://www.porsche.com/uk/models/911/911-carrera-models/)))
  - **options_notes**: Options: 20/21 Carrera S, Carrera Classic, RS Spyder Design, Carrera Exclusive Design (5-spoke) wheels. Carrera S: 245/35 ZR 20 / 305/30 ZR 21 ([auto-brochures.com](http://www.auto-brochures.com/makes/Porsche/911/992/Porsche_US%20911_2021.pdf), [presskit.porsche.de](https://presskit.porsche.de/workshops/en/porsche-new-911/downloads.html?file=daten/assets/workshops-porsche-new-911/documents/presentation/en/TWS_992_Chassis_EN.pdf (via http://web.archive.org/web/2024id_/ snapshot)))
- **headlights**
  - **shape**: Newly developed LED headlights integrated almost seamlessly into the wings, 'typically-911 round and upright form'; positioned at a slightly sharper angle than 991 ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/products/porsche-911-new-eigth-generation-992-timeless-machine-design-icon-world-premiere-los-angeles-2018-16522.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/8.-Generation---Design-und-Modellvarianten.html))
  - **surround_ring**: _not found_ — _not found in: -_
  - **mounting**: Upright in wings, near-flush ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/products/porsche-911-new-eigth-generation-992-timeless-machine-design-icon-world-premiere-los-angeles-2018-16522.html))
  - **lens_technology**: LED headlights standard (incl. auxiliary high beam); optional LED-matrix with PDLS Plus (84 LEDs) ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:cea78bb0-8770-434e-be37-ca597fda76ad/PAG_991_Cabrio_Athen_PM_EN.pdf))
  - **washers**: _not found_ — _not found in: -_
  - **other**: Four-point DRL signature 'extended in a three-dimensional line to the rear' of the lamp; optional LED-matrix (84 LEDs, PDLS Plus) ([presskit.porsche.de](https://presskit.porsche.de/models/en/porsche-new-911/topic/eighth-generation-of-an-icon.html?file=daten/assets/models-porsche-new-911/documents/press/en/PAG_Valencia_PM_EN.pdf (via http://web.archive.org/web/20210509100604id_/ snapshot)), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:6b08630c-aa53-442c-911c-d4c2f384c031/Presskit_2020_Porsche_911_Carrera_S_and_Carrera_4S.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:cea78bb0-8770-434e-be37-ca597fda76ad/PAG_991_Cabrio_Athen_PM_EN.pdf))
- **front**
  - **bumper_type**: New front apron with straight lines; front end 45 mm wider on all models ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/products/porsche-911-new-eigth-generation-992-timeless-machine-design-icon-world-premiere-los-angeles-2018-16522.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:cea78bb0-8770-434e-be37-ca597fda76ad/PAG_991_Cabrio_Athen_PM_EN.pdf))
  - **overriders**: None — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo_ ([presskit.porsche.de](https://presskit.porsche.de/workshops/en/porsche-new-911/downloads.html?file=daten/assets/workshops-porsche-new-911/documents/presentation/en/TWS_992_Introduction_EN.pdf (retrieved via http://web.archive.org/web/2024id_/ snapshot)))
  - **indicators**: Slim front light modules above the outer intakes ('now much narrower') - the 992.2 later deleted these ancillary lights ([presskit.porsche.de](https://presskit.porsche.de/models/en/porsche-new-911/topic/eighth-generation-of-an-icon.html?file=daten/assets/models-porsche-new-911/documents/press/en/PAG_Valencia_PM_EN.pdf (via http://web.archive.org/web/20210509100604id_/ snapshot)), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:baaedc84-1430-447f-9bd1-94636087e9bc/PAG_Pressemappe_911-EN.pdf), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/ee8e5bff-4099-4151-979c-d1ad797d674f.zip))
  - **fog_lights**: _not found_ — _not found in: -_
  - **horn_grilles**: None — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo_ ([presskit.porsche.de](https://presskit.porsche.de/workshops/en/porsche-new-911/downloads.html?file=daten/assets/workshops-porsche-new-911/documents/presentation/en/TWS_992_Introduction_EN.pdf (retrieved via http://web.archive.org/web/2024id_/ snapshot)))
  - **air_intakes**: Front air intakes combined into a visual unit by a continuous black intake trim, horizontal lines dominate; the two side intakes are larger than on 991.2; continuously variable cooling-air flaps (closed 70-150 km/h where possible); ACC radar in the middle of the central intake ([presskit.porsche.de](https://presskit.porsche.de/models/en/porsche-new-911/topic/eighth-generation-of-an-icon.html?file=daten/assets/models-porsche-new-911/documents/press/en/PAG_Valencia_PM_EN.pdf (via http://web.archive.org/web/20210509100604id_/ snapshot)), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:cea78bb0-8770-434e-be37-ca597fda76ad/PAG_991_Cabrio_Athen_PM_EN.pdf))
  - **front_lid_hood**: Forward-extended bonnet with a distinctive recess in front of the windscreen (G-series homage) ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/products/porsche-911-new-eigth-generation-992-timeless-machine-design-icon-world-premiere-los-angeles-2018-16522.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:cea78bb0-8770-434e-be37-ca597fda76ad/PAG_991_Cabrio_Athen_PM_EN.pdf))
  - **badge_crest**: Porsche crest on front lid — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo_ ([presskit.porsche.de](https://presskit.porsche.de/workshops/en/porsche-new-911/downloads.html?file=daten/assets/workshops-porsche-new-911/documents/presentation/en/TWS_992_Introduction_EN.pdf (retrieved via http://web.archive.org/web/2024id_/ snapshot)))
  - **other**: _not found_ — _not found in: -_
- **rear**
  - **taillights**: Seamless one-piece full-width LED light bar with no joints; integrated tail-light modules; direction indicators enclose the light bar and frame the 3D 'PORSCHE' logo; tail lights a little flatter than 991 ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/products/porsche-911-new-eigth-generation-992-timeless-machine-design-icon-world-premiere-los-angeles-2018-16522.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:cea78bb0-8770-434e-be37-ca597fda76ad/PAG_991_Cabrio_Athen_PM_EN.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/press-kits/60-Years-Porsche-911/8.-Generation---Design-und-Modellvarianten.html))
  - **reflector_band**: None; reflectors integrated in the black lower rear area ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:cea78bb0-8770-434e-be37-ca597fda76ad/PAG_991_Cabrio_Athen_PM_EN.pdf))
  - **bumper**: Black lower rear section integrating reflectors and outer air outlets; tailpipes flush; plate low between the tailpipes. A high-gloss black trim panel visually joins rear window and engine grille into one unit above the spoiler ([presskit.porsche.de](https://presskit.porsche.de/models/en/porsche-new-911/topic/eighth-generation-of-an-icon.html?file=daten/assets/models-porsche-new-911/documents/press/en/PAG_Valencia_PM_EN.pdf (via http://web.archive.org/web/20210509100604id_/ snapshot)), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:cea78bb0-8770-434e-be37-ca597fda76ad/PAG_991_Cabrio_Athen_PM_EN.pdf))
  - **exhaust**: Carrera: individual (single) tailpipe cover each side; Carrera S: two double tailpipes; optional sports exhaust: two oval outlets ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/2019/products/porsche-911-carrera-coupe-cabriolet-eighth-generation-18248.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:cea78bb0-8770-434e-be37-ca597fda76ad/PAG_991_Cabrio_Athen_PM_EN.pdf), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/ee8e5bff-4099-4151-979c-d1ad797d674f.zip))
  - **license_plate**: Relocated low, between the tailpipes ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:cea78bb0-8770-434e-be37-ca597fda76ad/PAG_991_Cabrio_Athen_PM_EN.pdf))
  - **badging**: Model designation under the light bar in a new 70s-style font; 'PORSCHE' logo in the light-bar zone ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:cea78bb0-8770-434e-be37-ca597fda76ad/PAG_991_Cabrio_Athen_PM_EN.pdf))
  - **other**: _not found_ — _not found in: -_
- **rear_spoiler**
  - **type**: Adaptive rear spoiler directly below the grille, much wider (almost to the outer tail-light edges, only one join each side), 45 % larger effective area (also quoted as 25 % more air-flow area); second brake light in the spoiler ([presskit.porsche.de](https://presskit.porsche.de/models/en/porsche-new-911/topic/eighth-generation-of-an-icon.html?file=daten/assets/models-porsche-new-911/documents/press/en/PAG_Valencia_PM_EN.pdf (via http://web.archive.org/web/20210509100604id_/ snapshot)), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/products/porsche-911-new-eigth-generation-992-timeless-machine-design-icon-world-premiere-los-angeles-2018-16522.html))
  - **deploy**: EU: retracted up to 90 km/h; Eco position 90-150 km/h; Performance position above 150 km/h; in Sport/Sport Plus/Wet Performance from 90 km/h; extends from 60 km/h if charge-air temperature is high. US press kit: only two positions - retracted up to 56 mph, then Performance ([presskit.porsche.de](https://presskit.porsche.de/models/en/porsche-new-911/topic/eighth-generation-of-an-icon.html?file=daten/assets/models-porsche-new-911/documents/press/en/PAG_Valencia_PM_EN.pdf (via http://web.archive.org/web/20210509100604id_/ snapshot)), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:6b08630c-aa53-442c-911c-d4c2f384c031/Presskit_2020_Porsche_911_Carrera_S_and_Carrera_4S.pdf))
- **engine_lid_grille**
  - **design**: Vertical louvres echoing the rear-window contour - nine slats per side - with the vertical twin-element third brake light in the centre (reads '911'); black louvres on rear-drive, chrome elements on AWD; high-gloss black panel links grille and rear window — _9+9 slat count verified visually on newsroom rear photo img_4_ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/products/porsche-911-new-eigth-generation-992-timeless-machine-design-icon-world-premiere-los-angeles-2018-16522.html), [presskit.porsche.de](https://presskit.porsche.de/models/en/porsche-new-911/topic/eighth-generation-of-an-icon.html?file=daten/assets/models-porsche-new-911/documents/press/en/PAG_Valencia_PM_EN.pdf (via http://web.archive.org/web/20210509100604id_/ snapshot)), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(992)), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/ee8e5bff-4099-4151-979c-d1ad797d674f.zip))
- **body**
  - **fender_flares**: Wide body on all 992s: rear-drive models adopt the AWD body width; rear body +44 mm ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/products/porsche-911-new-eigth-generation-992-timeless-machine-design-icon-world-premiere-los-angeles-2018-16522.html))
  - **rear_arch_width**: Wide rear arches on every model (1,852 mm overall) ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/products/porsche-911-new-eigth-generation-992-timeless-machine-design-icon-world-premiere-los-angeles-2018-16522.html), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(992)))
  - **mirrors**: Redesigned rectangular, aero-optimised door mirrors; black lower mirror trim standard ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:cea78bb0-8770-434e-be37-ca597fda76ad/PAG_991_Cabrio_Athen_PM_EN.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/products/porsche-911-new-eigth-generation-992-timeless-machine-design-icon-world-premiere-los-angeles-2018-16522.html))
  - **door_handles**: Flush electrically pop-out door handles ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/products/porsche-911-new-eigth-generation-992-timeless-machine-design-icon-world-premiere-los-angeles-2018-16522.html), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:cea78bb0-8770-434e-be37-ca597fda76ad/PAG_991_Cabrio_Athen_PM_EN.pdf))
  - **side_windows**: Door glass + fixed quarter window (carry-over layout) — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo_ ([pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/ee8e5bff-4099-4151-979c-d1ad797d674f.zip))
  - **quarter_window**: Fixed tapered quarter window — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo_ ([pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/ee8e5bff-4099-4151-979c-d1ad797d674f.zip))
  - **drip_rails**: None — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo_ ([pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/ee8e5bff-4099-4151-979c-d1ad797d674f.zip))
  - **roofline**: Flyline retained; aluminium outer skin except front/rear sections ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:cea78bb0-8770-434e-be37-ca597fda76ad/PAG_991_Cabrio_Athen_PM_EN.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en/products/porsche-911-new-eigth-generation-992-timeless-machine-design-icon-world-premiere-los-angeles-2018-16522.html))
  - **fuel_filler**: Right front wing — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo (top view img_11)_ ([pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/ee8e5bff-4099-4151-979c-d1ad797d674f.zip))
  - **windshield**: _not found_ — _not found in: -_
  - **chrome_trim**: _not found_ — _not found in: -_
  - **side_repeaters**: _not found_ — _not found in: -_
  - **rocker_sills**: Side-contour sweep starts lower than 991 for a lower visual centre of gravity ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:cea78bb0-8770-434e-be37-ca597fda76ad/PAG_991_Cabrio_Athen_PM_EN.pdf))
  - **other**: Door appears inserted seamlessly between front and rear wings ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:cea78bb0-8770-434e-be37-ca597fda76ad/PAG_991_Cabrio_Athen_PM_EN.pdf))

### 18-992-2-2024 — 911 Carrera (Type 992, second phase '992.2')

- **blueprints**: `blueprints/18-992-2-2024/` — primary: `vec-30945_porsche-911-carrera-s-2025_5view-dims-watermarked.jpg` — Vendor (watermarked) 5-view with dims for the 992.2 Carrera S (2025) + 992.2 Targa GTS 4-view.
- **production_years**: Revealed 28 May 2024 (Carrera + Carrera GTS T-Hybrid); in production ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en_US/2024/products/porsche-new-911-world-premiere-hybrid-36337.html), [en.wikipedia.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_911_(992)))
- **reference_variant**: 911 Carrera Coupé (992.2, 3.0 twin-turbo, 290 kW/394 PS EU; 388 hp US), RWD, PDK; EU spec sheet MY S 05/2024 ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:7f3751ae-477c-48a5-b087-31f4fde1c124/pag-911-carrera-en.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:86fc9a9b-ece1-4cca-9930-39a78e89baf3/992.2%20911%20Carrera%20and%20Carrera%20Cabriolet%20Technical%20Specifications.pdf))
- **dimensions_mm**
  - **length**: 4542 — _GTS 4,553 mm due to model-specific aprons_ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:7f3751ae-477c-48a5-b087-31f4fde1c124/pag-911-carrera-en.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:86fc9a9b-ece1-4cca-9930-39a78e89baf3/992.2%20911%20Carrera%20and%20Carrera%20Cabriolet%20Technical%20Specifications.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:baaedc84-1430-447f-9bd1-94636087e9bc/PAG_Pressemappe_911-EN.pdf))
  - **width**: 1852 — _Without mirrors; 2,033 mm with mirrors_ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:7f3751ae-477c-48a5-b087-31f4fde1c124/pag-911-carrera-en.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:86fc9a9b-ece1-4cca-9930-39a78e89baf3/992.2%20911%20Carrera%20and%20Carrera%20Cabriolet%20Technical%20Specifications.pdf))
  - **height**: 1302 — _EU spec; US spec lists 1,298 mm (51.1 in) with standard PASM; press kit range 1,292-1,302. Recommended 1,302 (EU) or 1,300 as a compromise_ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:7f3751ae-477c-48a5-b087-31f4fde1c124/pag-911-carrera-en.pdf))
  - **wheelbase**: 2450 ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:7f3751ae-477c-48a5-b087-31f4fde1c124/pag-911-carrera-en.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:86fc9a9b-ece1-4cca-9930-39a78e89baf3/992.2%20911%20Carrera%20and%20Carrera%20Cabriolet%20Technical%20Specifications.pdf))
  - **track_front**: 1597 — _EU spec; US spec says 1,591 mm (62.7 in). Recommend EU 1,597_ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:7f3751ae-477c-48a5-b087-31f4fde1c124/pag-911-carrera-en.pdf))
  - **track_rear**: 1551 — _EU spec; US spec says 1,557 mm (61.2 in). Recommend EU 1,551_ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:7f3751ae-477c-48a5-b087-31f4fde1c124/pag-911-carrera-en.pdf))
  - **ground_clearance**: _not found_ — _Not published; optional front-axle lift adds ~1.5 in (~40 mm) up to 37 mph [https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:928354fb-31e2-4b4c-af93-2c22d6de07b4/US%20911%20Carrera%20and%20Carrera%20GTS%20Press%20Kit.pdf]_
- **overhang_mm**
  - **front**: 1020 — _Derived, not published. Method: official Porsche side image; wheel-centre x from the Porsche-crest hub caps, body extremes from the silhouette; perspective corrected by scaling near-side wheel positions by k=(true L/WB)/(image L/WB) about the car centre, then total overhang = length - wheelbase split accordingly. Official porsche.com 992.2 Carrera side render (2616x712 PNG): image L/WB 1.769 vs true 1.854 -> k=1.048; gives front ~1,020 mm, rear ~1,073 mm (+/-15 mm). Implies the +23 mm length growth vs 992.1 sits mostly in the new rear apron._ ([a.storyblok.com](https://a.storyblok.com/f/322327/2616x712/5d81165a01/cz25w01ix0010911-carrera-side.png))
  - **rear**: 1073 — _See front. Total = 4,542 - 2,450 = 2,092 mm._ ([a.storyblok.com](https://a.storyblok.com/f/322327/2616x712/5d81165a01/cz25w01ix0010911-carrera-side.png))

  - **drag_cd**: 0.3 — _Frontal area 2.07 m2, cd x A 0.621 m2; GTS 0.27_ ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:7f3751ae-477c-48a5-b087-31f4fde1c124/pag-911-carrera-en.pdf))

Official porsche.com renders show optional 5-spoke and 5-double-spoke designs, so do not infer the standard design from them._

- **headlights**
  - **shape**: Round, upright; redesigned headlights with three-dimensional moulded light modules and the Porsche four-point DRL ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:baaedc84-1430-447f-9bd1-94636087e9bc/PAG_Pressemappe_911-EN.pdf))
  - **surround_ring**: Thin bright ring around a dark four-module lens (four-point DRL cross visible) — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo (porsche.com front render)_ ([a.storyblok.com](https://a.storyblok.com/f/322327/1720x1210/c6b3d109aa/cz25w01ix0011911-carrera-front.png))
  - **mounting**: Upright in front wings — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo_ ([pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/a48df4f0-05a7-43e0-b74e-fd221f9b2155.zip))
  - **lens_technology**: Matrix LED standard (11 segments); HD-Matrix LED optional (>32,000 pixels per lamp) ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:baaedc84-1430-447f-9bd1-94636087e9bc/PAG_Pressemappe_911-EN.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:928354fb-31e2-4b4c-af93-2c22d6de07b4/US%20911%20Carrera%20and%20Carrera%20GTS%20Press%20Kit.pdf))
  - **washers**: _not found_ — _not found in: -_
  - **other**: ALL front lighting functions integrated in the headlights - ancillary apron lights omitted ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:baaedc84-1430-447f-9bd1-94636087e9bc/PAG_Pressemappe_911-EN.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en_US/2024/products/porsche-new-911-world-premiere-hybrid-36337.html))
- **front**
  - **bumper_type**: New model-specific front apron; the Carrera keeps horizontal cooling-air intakes, now larger because the apron lights were deleted ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:baaedc84-1430-447f-9bd1-94636087e9bc/PAG_Pressemappe_911-EN.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en_US/2024/products/porsche-new-911-world-premiere-hybrid-36337.html))
  - **overriders**: None — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo_ ([pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/a48df4f0-05a7-43e0-b74e-fd221f9b2155.zip))
  - **indicators**: In the headlights (no apron lights) ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:baaedc84-1430-447f-9bd1-94636087e9bc/PAG_Pressemappe_911-EN.pdf))
  - **fog_lights**: None separate (functions in headlights) ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:baaedc84-1430-447f-9bd1-94636087e9bc/PAG_Pressemappe_911-EN.pdf))
  - **horn_grilles**: None — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo_ ([pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/a48df4f0-05a7-43e0-b74e-fd221f9b2155.zip))
  - **air_intakes**: Horizontal outer intakes with horizontal slats (no visible vertical flaps on the base Carrera render); centre section carries the plate with assistance sensors behind a high-gloss black panel below it. GTS instead: five visible vertical active flaps per side (+1 hidden) and adaptive front diffusers ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:baaedc84-1430-447f-9bd1-94636087e9bc/PAG_Pressemappe_911-EN.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en_US/2024/products/porsche-new-911-world-premiere-hybrid-36337.html), [a.storyblok.com](https://a.storyblok.com/f/322327/1720x1210/c6b3d109aa/cz25w01ix0011911-carrera-front.png), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/a48df4f0-05a7-43e0-b74e-fd221f9b2155.zip))
  - **front_lid_hood**: Carry-over bonnet with recess — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo_ ([pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/a48df4f0-05a7-43e0-b74e-fd221f9b2155.zip))
  - **badge_crest**: Porsche crest on front lid — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo_ ([pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/a48df4f0-05a7-43e0-b74e-fd221f9b2155.zip))
  - **other**: New license-plate holder position creates space for sensors concealed behind a high-gloss surface below the plate ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:baaedc84-1430-447f-9bd1-94636087e9bc/PAG_Pressemappe_911-EN.pdf))
- **rear**
  - **taillights**: Redesigned full-width light strip with an integrated LED light arc, seamlessly framed, flank to flank; raised 'PORSCHE' lettering visually integrated; GTS gets transparent taillight strips ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:baaedc84-1430-447f-9bd1-94636087e9bc/PAG_Pressemappe_911-EN.pdf))
  - **reflector_band**: None; red reflectors in the black lower section at the outer corners — _visual check of official Porsche press/gallery photo (img_13)_ ([pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/a48df4f0-05a7-43e0-b74e-fd221f9b2155.zip))
  - **bumper**: Clearly structured rear apron; lower rear section finished in black; charge-air-cooling outlet integrated ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:baaedc84-1430-447f-9bd1-94636087e9bc/PAG_Pressemappe_911-EN.pdf))
  - **exhaust**: Carrera: model-specific tailpipes of unique design integrated into pronounced diffuser fins - one large oval black tailpipe each side as photographed; optional sports exhaust with oval tailpipes in black or silver; GTS: two centrally positioned oval tailpipe trims ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:baaedc84-1430-447f-9bd1-94636087e9bc/PAG_Pressemappe_911-EN.pdf), [newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/en_US/2024/products/porsche-new-911-world-premiere-hybrid-36337.html), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/a48df4f0-05a7-43e0-b74e-fd221f9b2155.zip))
  - **license_plate**: Moved upward (above the black diffuser section) ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:baaedc84-1430-447f-9bd1-94636087e9bc/PAG_Pressemappe_911-EN.pdf), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/a48df4f0-05a7-43e0-b74e-fd221f9b2155.zip))
  - **badging**: Raised 'PORSCHE' lettering in the light strip; '911 Carrera' script below ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:baaedc84-1430-447f-9bd1-94636087e9bc/PAG_Pressemappe_911-EN.pdf), [pmdb.porsche.de](https://pmdb.porsche.de/newsroomzips/a48df4f0-05a7-43e0-b74e-fd221f9b2155.zip))

- **distinctive**
  - No lights in the front apron at all ([newsroom.porsche.com](https://newsroom.porsche.com/dam/jcr:baaedc84-1430-447f-9bd1-94636087e9bc/PAG_Pressemappe_911-EN.pdf))
